### [1] [Enhancing Value Alignment of LLMs with Multi-agent system and Combinatorial Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11126)
*Yuanhong Wu,Djallel Bouneffouf,D. Frank Hsu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: VAS-CFA是一个通过组合融合分析实现多智能体融合对齐的框架，用于提升LLM与人类价值观的对齐效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法如RLHF通常依赖单一评估者或狭窄定义的奖励信号，难以捕捉伦理多元主义，限制了LLM与人类价值观的全面对齐能力

Method: 提出VAS-CFA框架：实例化多个代表不同规范视角的道德智能体，使用组合融合分析（CFA）融合它们的输出，包括基于排名和基于分数的聚合方法

Result: 实证评估显示VAS-CFA在标准指标上优于单智能体基线和先前的聚合方法，表明多智能体融合为LLM价值对齐提供了鲁棒有效的机制

Conclusion: 多智能体融合对齐通过利用智能体间的认知多样性，能够更好地反映人类价值观，是推进LLM价值对齐的有效途径

Abstract: Aligning large language models (LLMs) with human values is a central challenge for ensuring trustworthy and safe deployment. While existing methods such as Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) and its variants have improved alignment, they often rely on a single evaluator or narrowly defined reward signals, limiting their ability to capture ethical pluralism. In this work, we propose the Value Alignment System using Combinatorial Fusion Analysis (VAS-CFA), a framework that operationalizes multi-agent fusion alignment. It instantiates multiple moral agents, each fine-tuned to represent a distinct normative perspective, and fuses their outputs using CFA with both rank- and score-based aggregation. This design leverages cognitive diversity, between agents, to mitigate conflicts and redundancies across multiple agents, producing responses that better reflect human values. Empirical evaluation demonstrates that VAS-CFA outperforms both single agent baselines and prior aggregation approaches on standard metrics, showing that multi-agent fusion provides a robust and effective mechanism for advancing value alignment in LLMs.

</details>


### [2] [How Intelligence Emerges: A Minimal Theory of Dynamic Adaptive Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11560)
*Stefano Grassi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个多智能体系统中自适应协调的动力学理论，将协调视为耦合动力学的结构特性而非集中式优化问题的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法通常通过均衡优化或智能体中心学习来分析协调，但本文旨在建立更全面的框架，将智能体、激励和环境建模为递归闭合的反馈架构，以理解协调作为耦合动力学结构特性的本质。

Method: 采用动力学系统理论框架，将协调建模为智能体、激励和环境之间的递归闭合反馈架构。持久环境存储累积的协调信号，分布式激励场局部传输这些信号，自适应智能体响应更新。通过耗散性假设和谱分析技术研究系统结构特性。

Result: 建立了三个结构结果：1) 在耗散性假设下，闭环系统存在有界前向不变区域，确保可行性而无需全局最优性；2) 当激励信号非平凡地依赖于持久环境记忆时，动力学通常不能简化为仅定义在智能体状态空间上的静态全局目标；3) 持久环境状态诱导历史敏感性，除非系统是全局收缩的。通过最小线性规范说明耦合、持久性和耗散性如何通过雅可比矩阵的谱条件控制局部稳定性和振荡机制。

Conclusion: 智能协调动力学可以从持久环境内的激励介导的自适应交互中涌现，无需预设福利最大化、理性预期或集中设计。协调应被视为耦合动力学的结构特性而非集中式优化问题的解。

Abstract: This paper develops a dynamical theory of adaptive coordination in multi-agent systems. Rather than analyzing coordination through equilibrium optimization or agent-centric learning alone, the framework models agents, incentives, and environment as a recursively closed feedback architecture. A persistent environment stores accumulated coordination signals, a distributed incentive field transmits those signals locally, and adaptive agents update in response. Coordination is thus treated as a structural property of coupled dynamics rather than as the solution to a centralized objective.
  The paper establishes three structural results. First, under dissipativity assumptions, the induced closed-loop system admits a bounded forward-invariant region, ensuring viability without requiring global optimality. Second, when incentive signals depend non-trivially on persistent environmental memory, the resulting dynamics generically cannot be reduced to a static global objective defined solely over the agent state space. Third, persistent environmental state induces history sensitivity unless the system is globally contracting.
  A minimal linear specification illustrates how coupling, persistence, and dissipation govern local stability and oscillatory regimes through spectral conditions on the Jacobian. The results establish structural conditions under which intelligent coordination dynamics emerge from incentive-mediated adaptive interaction within a persistent environment, without presuming welfare maximization, rational expectations, or centralized design.

</details>


### [3] [The price of decentralization in managing engineering systems through multi-agent reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11884)
*Prateek Bhustali,Pablo G. Morato,Konstantinos G. Papakonstantinou,Charalampos P. Andriotis*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了多智能体深度强化学习在检查和维护规划中的应用，通过系统改变冗余度来检验去中心化决策对协调效率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检查和维护规划涉及不确定性和不完全信息下的序列决策，可建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程。单智能体深度强化学习虽能提供近似解，但在多组件系统中扩展性差。多智能体深度强化学习通过去中心化决策实现扩展性，但可能引发合作病理问题，降低学习策略的最优性。

Method: 引入一组系统退化环境，其中冗余度被系统性地改变。这些基准环境设计使得集中式（接近）最优策略的计算保持可处理性，便于直接比较解决方案。实现并基准测试了广泛的多智能体深度强化学习算法，涵盖完全集中式和去中心化训练范式，从价值分解到演员-评论家方法。

Result: 结果显示冗余度对协调有明显影响：在串联式设置中，多智能体深度强化学习算法能实现接近最优的性能，而增加冗余度会放大协调挑战并可能导致最优性损失。尽管如此，去中心化智能体学习到的结构化策略始终优于优化的启发式基线。

Conclusion: 研究强调了去中心化学习在可扩展维护规划中的前景和当前局限性。虽然冗余度增加会带来协调挑战，但多智能体深度强化学习仍能学习到优于传统启发式方法的结构化策略。

Abstract: Inspection and maintenance (I&M) planning involves sequential decision making under uncertainties and incomplete information, and can be modeled as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP). While single-agent deep reinforcement learning provides approximate solutions to POMDPs, it does not scale well in multi-component systems. Scalability can be achieved through multi-agent deep reinforcement learning (MADRL), which decentralizes decision-making across multiple agents, locally controlling individual components. However, this decentralization can induce cooperation pathologies that degrade the optimality of the learned policies. To examine these effects in I&M planning, we introduce a set of deteriorating systems in which redundancy is varied systematically. These benchmark environments are designed such that computation of centralized (near-)optimal policies remains tractable, enabling direct comparison of solution methods. We implement and benchmark a broad set of MADRL algorithms spanning fully centralized and decentralized training paradigms, from value-factorization to actor-critic methods. Our results show a clear effect of redundancy on coordination: MADRL algorithms achieve near-optimal performance in series-like settings, whereas increasing redundancy amplifies coordination challenges and can lead to optimality losses. Nonetheless, decentralized agents learn structured policies that consistently outperform optimized heuristic baselines, highlighting both the promise and current limitations of decentralized learning for scalable maintenance planning.

</details>


### [4] [CogSearch: A Cognitive-Aligned Multi-Agent Framework for Proactive Decision Support in E-Commerce Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11927)
*Zhouwei Zhai,Mengxiang Chen,Haoyun Xia,Jin Li,Renquan Zhou,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: CogSearch：一个面向认知的多智能体框架，将电商搜索重新构想为主动决策支持系统，通过四个专业智能体模拟人类认知工作流，显著降低决策成本并提升转化率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电商搜索引擎主要基于被动检索-排序模型，难以支持复杂决策过程，导致用户面临认知摩擦和决策负担过重的问题。

Method: 提出CogSearch框架，包含四个专业智能体协同工作：分解复杂用户意图、融合内部和外部异构知识、提供可操作的洞察，模拟人类认知工作流程。

Result: 离线基准测试验证了CogSearch在咨询性和复杂搜索场景中的卓越表现。在JD.com的在线A/B测试中，系统降低决策成本5%，整体UCVR提升0.41%，决策密集型查询的转化率显著提升30%。

Conclusion: CogSearch代表了信息检索的根本转变，从传统的以相关性为中心的范式转向整体、协作的决策智能未来。

Abstract: Modern e-commerce search engines, largely rooted in passive retrieval-and-ranking models, frequently fail to support complex decision-making, leaving users overwhelmed by cognitive friction. In this paper, we introduce CogSearch, a novel cognitive-oriented multi-agent framework that reimagines e-commerce search as a proactive decision support system. By synergizing four specialized agents, CogSearch mimics human cognitive workflows: it decomposes intricate user intents, fuses heterogeneous knowledge across internal and external sources, and delivers highly actionable insights. Our offline benchmarks validate CogSearch's excellence in consultative and complex search scenarios. Extensive online A/B testing on JD.com demonstrates the system's transformative impact: it reduced decision costs by 5% and achieved a 0.41% increase in overall UCVR, with a remarkable 30% surge in conversion for decision-heavy queries. CogSearch represents a fundamental shift in information retrieval, moving beyond traditional relevance-centric paradigms toward a future of holistic, collaborative decision intelligence.

</details>


### [5] [Language Model Teams as Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12229)
*Elizabeth Mieczkowski,Katherine M. Collins,Ilia Sucholutsky,Natalia Vélez,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文提出使用分布式系统作为创建和评估LLM团队的原则性框架，以解决LLM团队设计中的关键问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM能力增强，LLM团队部署规模扩大，但缺乏原则性框架来解决关键问题：团队何时有效、需要多少智能体、结构如何影响性能、团队是否优于单个智能体。目前主要依赖试错方法，需要更系统化的理论基础。

Method: 提出将分布式系统理论作为创建和评估LLM团队的原则性基础。通过分布式计算视角分析LLM团队，识别两者之间的相似性和可借鉴的实践洞察。

Result: 研究发现分布式计算中研究的许多基本优势和挑战同样出现在LLM团队中，表明这两个研究领域之间存在丰富的实践洞察交叉。

Conclusion: 分布式系统为LLM团队提供了有价值的理论框架，能够指导团队设计、评估和优化，避免试错方法，促进两个领域的交叉研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are growing increasingly capable, prompting recent interest in LLM teams. Yet, despite increased deployment of LLM teams at scale, we lack a principled framework for addressing key questions such as when a team is helpful, how many agents to use, how structure impacts performance -- and whether a team is better than a single agent. Rather than designing and testing these possibilities through trial-and-error, we propose using distributed systems as a principled foundation for creating and evaluating LLM teams. We find that many of the fundamental advantages and challenges studied in distributed computing also arise in LLM teams, highlighting the rich practical insights that can come from the cross-talk of these two fields of study.

</details>

### [6] [AutoVeriFix+: High-Correctness RTL Generation via Trace-Aware Causal Fix and Semantic Redundancy Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11489)
*Yan Tan,Xiangchen Meng,Zijun Jiang,Yangdi Lyu*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: AutoVeriFix+是一个三阶段框架，通过结合高级语义推理和状态空间探索，提升LLM生成Verilog代码的功能正确性和设计效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在生成高级编程语言代码方面表现出色，但在硬件描述语言（如Verilog）生成方面面临挑战，主要原因是高质量训练数据稀缺。现有方法通常只关注语法正确性，导致生成的代码存在功能错误。

Method: 提出三阶段框架：1) LLM生成定义电路行为的Python参考模型；2) LLM生成初始Verilog RTL候选并迭代修复语法错误；3) 引入Concolic测试引擎执行深度时序逻辑并识别边界情况漏洞，利用周期精确执行轨迹和内部寄存器快照为LLM提供解决复杂状态转换错误所需的因果上下文，并生成覆盖率报告以识别功能冗余分支。

Result: 在严格基准测试中实现了超过80%的功能正确性，在VerilogEval-machine数据集上达到90.2%的pass@10分数。通过轨迹感知优化，在基准测试中平均消除了25%的冗余逻辑。

Conclusion: AutoVeriFix+通过整合高级语义推理和状态空间探索，有效解决了LLM生成Verilog代码时功能正确性不足的问题，同时实现了显著的逻辑优化。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in generating software code for high-level programming languages such as Python and C++. However, their application to hardware description languages, such as Verilog, is challenging due to the scarcity of high-quality training data. Current approaches to Verilog code generation using LLMs often focus on syntactic correctness, resulting in code with functional errors. To address these challenges, we propose AutoVeriFix+, a novel three-stage framework that integrates high-level semantic reasoning with state-space exploration to enhance functional correctness and design efficiency. In the first stage, an LLM is employed to generate high-level Python reference models that define the intended circuit behavior. In the second stage, another LLM generates initial Verilog RTL candidates and iteratively fixes syntactic errors. In the third stage, we introduce a Concolic testing engine to exercise deep sequential logic and identify corner-case vulnerabilities. With cycle-accurate execution traces and internal register snapshots, AutoVeriFix+ provides the LLM with the causal context necessary to resolve complex state-transition errors. Furthermore, it will generate a coverage report to identify functionally redundant branches, enabling the LLM to perform semantic pruning for area optimization. Experimental results demonstrate that AutoVeriFix+ achieves over 80% functional correctness on rigorous benchmarks, reaching a pass@10 score of 90.2% on the VerilogEval-machine dataset. In addition, it eliminates an average of 25% redundant logic across benchmarks through trace-aware optimization.

</details>

### [7] [Context Before Code: An Experience Report on Vibe Coding in Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11073)
*Md Nasir Uddin Shuvo,Md Aidul Islam,Md Mahade Hasan,Muhammad Waseem,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 团队在生成式AI辅助编程实践中发现，虽然"氛围编码"能加速脚手架搭建，但生成代码常忽略未明确定义的隔离规则和基础设施约束，导致工程重点从样板代码实现转向约束规范和审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在真实生产约束下使用对话式代码生成工具（"氛围编码"）的实际经验，了解其在生产环境中的适用性和局限性。

Method: 小型全栈团队应用上下文提示和显式架构约束，构建了两个系统：1）支持多项目隔离的智能体学习平台；2）具有引用基础和访问控制的学术RAG系统。

Result: 氛围编码加速了脚手架搭建和集成，但生成代码常忽略未明确定义的隔离规则和基础设施约束。多租户、访问控制、内存策略和异步处理等需要专门设计验证。工程重点从样板代码实现转向约束规范和审计。

Conclusion: 对话式代码生成在生产环境中存在"不可委托区域"，需要明确的架构约束和验证。工程重点转移至约束规范和审计，而非样板代码实现。

Abstract: Code-generating tools are increasingly used in software development, yet experience reports on conversational "vibe coding" under production constraints remain limited. This paper presents an experience report from a small full-stack team that applied contextual prompting and explicit architectural constraints to build (i) a multi-project agent learning platform designed for sustained, production-oriented use and (ii) an academic retrieval-augmented generation system.
  The agent platform supports multiple isolated projects, each with structured memory and background processing, thereby enforcing project-level isolation. The RAG system provides citation-grounded answers, role-based access control, and evaluation tracking.
  Across both systems, vibe coding accelerated scaffolding and integration. However, the generated code often under-specified isolation rules and infrastructure constraints when these were not explicitly defined. Consequently, aspects such as multi-tenancy, access control, memory policies, and asynchronous processing required deliberate architectural design and verification. We observe a shift in engineering effort from boilerplate implementation toward constraint specification and enforcement auditing. We also identify recurring architectural "non-delegation zones" where conversational code generation remains insufficient for production reliability.

</details>


### [8] [Type-safe Monitoring of Parameterized Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11104)
*Jan Baumeister,Bernd Finkbeiner,Florian Kohn*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 将参数化流集成到RTLola流监控框架中，通过精化类型系统确保运行时错误缺失，支持无界数据域的安全监控


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流监控是无人机等复杂信息物理系统的实时安全保障机制。由于监控器是安全关键组件，必须确保其运行时无错误。当监控器需要处理无界数据域（如无限数量的空域参与者）时，这一保证尤其具有挑战性，因为这需要使用动态数据结构。

Method: 将参数化流类型安全地集成到流监控框架RTLola中。参数化流将单个流泛化为无界数量流实例的集合，并提供系统化的内存管理机制。通过精化类型系统来保证所有内存引用要么成功，要么有默认值支持，从而有效确保运行时错误缺失。

Result: 证明了运行时错误缺失在一般情况下是不可判定的，但可以通过精化类型系统有效确保。报告了在包括自主飞机监控规范在内的多个基准测试示例规范上的类型分析性能。

Conclusion: 参数化流为RTLola流监控框架提供了类型安全的集成，能够处理无界数据域，并通过精化类型系统确保运行时错误缺失，这对于安全关键系统的监控至关重要。

Abstract: Stream-based monitoring is a real-time safety assurance mechanism for complex cyber-physical systems such as unmanned aerial vehicles. The monitor aggregates streams of input data from sensors and other sources to give real-time statistics and assessments of the system's health. Since the monitor is a safety-critical component, it is mandatory to ensure the absence of runtime errors in the monitor. Providing such guarantees is particularly challenging when the monitor must handle unbounded data domains, like an unlimited number of airspace participants, requiring the use of dynamic data structures. This paper provides a type-safe integration of parameterized streams into the stream-based monitoring framework RTLola. Parameterized streams generalize individual streams to sets of an unbounded number of stream instances and provide a systematic mechanism for memory management. We show that the absence of runtime errors is, in general, undecidable but can be effectively ensured with a refinement type system that guarantees all memory references are either successful or backed by a default value. We report on the performance of the type analysis on example specifications from a range of benchmarks, including specifications from the monitoring of autonomous aircraft.

</details>


### [9] [Quality-Driven Agentic Reasoning for LLM-Assisted Software Design: Questions-of-Thoughts (QoT) as a Time-Series Self-QA Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11082)
*Yen-Ku Liu,Yun-Cheng Tsai*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: QoT是一种质量驱动的推理时脚手架，通过将用户目标转化为有序工程步骤和逐步自问来验证约束、减少遗漏错误，从而提升LLM生成软件系统的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型语言模型在AI辅助软件开发方面取得进展，但实际部署仍受限于不完整实现、弱模块化和不一致的安全实践。需要一种方法来解决LLM生成系统中常见的失败模式。

Method: 提出Questions-of-Thoughts (QoT)框架，将用户目标转化为：(1)有序的工程步骤序列；(2)逐步自问以验证约束和减少遗漏错误。该方法保持轻量级推理记录以稳定后续设计决策。在API设计、数据通信和文件系统三个后端工程领域进行评估。

Result: 使用基于ISO/IEC启发的质量评估标准（可扩展性、完整性、模块化、安全性）进行评分。结果显示QoT带来容量依赖的改进：对于较大模型和更复杂领域，QoT产生一致的质量提升；对于较小模型，在紧约束和规划预算下可能出现权衡。

Conclusion: QoT作为推理时脚手架能有效提升LLM生成软件系统的质量，特别是对于复杂任务和大型模型。作者发布了包含提示、评分指南、原始生成和重现脚本的开源工具包。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have accelerated AI-assisted software development, yet practical deployment remains constrained by incomplete implementations, weak modularization, and inconsistent security practices. We introduce Questions-of-Thoughts (QoT), a quality-driven inference-time scaffold that turns a user goal into (i) an ordered sequence of engineering steps and (ii) stepwise self-questioning to verify constraints and reduce omission errors, while maintaining a lightweight reasoning record that stabilizes subsequent design decisions.
  We evaluate QoT across three representative backend engineering domains: API Design, Data Communication, and File Systems. Each task requires multi-module decomposition and exposes standard failure modes in LLM-generated systems. To enable data-driven comparison, we score generated artifacts using an ISO/IEC-inspired quality rubric that measures Scalability, Completeness, Modularity, and Security. We report domain-wise gains as the change in total quality score, defined as the QoT score minus the NoQoT score. Results show capacity-dependent improvements: QoT yields consistent quality improvements for larger models and more complex domains, while smaller models may exhibit trade-offs under tight context and planning budgets.
  We release an open artifact with prompts, scoring guidelines, raw generations, and scripts that reproduce the reported tables and figures to support applied AI and data analytics research.

</details>


### [10] [Bridging Behavioral Biometrics and Source Code Stylometry: A Survey of Programmer Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11150)
*Marek Horvath,Emilia Pietrikova,Diomidis Spinellis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对程序员归因研究的系统性映射研究，分析了2012-2025年间47篇相关论文，建立了特征类型与机器学习技术的分类体系，指出了该领域的研究现状和未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序员归因研究在软件工程、安全和数字取证领域日益重要，但现有研究分散且方法多样，缺乏系统性整理。本研究旨在通过系统性映射研究整合现有成果，建立统一框架，识别研究空白。

Method: 采用系统性映射研究方法，从135篇候选文献中筛选出47篇（2012-2025年）。从多个维度分析：作者身份任务、特征类别、学习建模方法、数据集来源、评估实践。建立分类体系，分析出版趋势、基准测试和编程语言。

Result: 研究发现：1）主要关注封闭世界作者归属，使用文体计量特征；2）严重依赖少数基准数据集；3）行为信号、作者验证和可重复性研究较少；4）识别出主要主题集群；5）建立了特征类型与机器学习技术的分类体系。

Conclusion: 研究整合了现有程序员归因研究，提供了统一框架，指出了方法学空白（如行为信号、作者验证、可重复性），为未来研究提供指导。该领域需要更多开放世界场景、多样化数据集和可重复性研究。

Abstract: Programmer attribution seeks to identify or verify the author of a source code artifact using stylistic, structural, or behavioural characteristics. This problem has been studied across software engineering, security, and digital forensics, resulting in a growing and methodologically diverse set of publications. This paper presents a systematic mapping study of programmer attribution research focused on source code analysis. From an initial set of 135 candidate publications, 47 studies published between 2012 and 2025 were selected through a structured screening process. The included works are analysed along several dimensions, including authorship tasks, feature categories, learning and modelling approaches, dataset sources, and evaluation practices. Based on this analysis, we derive a taxonomy that relates stylistic and behavioural feature types to commonly used machine learning techniques and provide a descriptive overview of publication trends, benchmarks, programming languages. A content-level analysis highlights the main thematic clusters in the field. The results indicate a strong focus on closed-world authorship attribution using stylometric features and a heavy reliance on a small number of benchmark datasets, while behavioural signals, authorship verification, and reproducibility remain less explored. The study consolidates existing research into a unified framework and outlines methodological gaps that can guide future work. This manuscript is currently under review. The present version is a preprint.

</details>


### [11] [ExecVerify: White-Box RL with Verifiable Stepwise Rewards for Code Execution Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11226)
*Lingxiao Tang,He Ye,Zhaoyang Chu,Muyang Ye,Zhongxin Liu,Xiaoxue Ren,Lingfeng Bao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ExecVerify通过结合可验证的白盒奖励（执行轨迹中的下一条语句预测和变量值/类型预测）来改进代码LLM的执行推理能力，使用强化学习奖励中间执行步骤和最终输出的正确性，并通过两阶段训练提升代码生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖监督微调（SFT）和教师生成的解释（I/O预测链或执行轨迹的自然语言描述），但中间执行步骤无法在SFT期间显式验证，训练目标可能简化为仅仅匹配教师解释。此外，训练数据通常缺乏对任务难度的显式控制。

Method: 1. 通过基于约束的程序合成构建具有多个难度级别的数据集；2. 应用强化学习（RL）奖励关于中间执行步骤和最终输出的正确答案，使训练目标与每个执行步骤的语义正确性对齐；3. 采用两阶段训练流程：首先增强执行推理能力，然后迁移到代码生成。

Result: 使用ExecVerify训练的7B模型在代码推理基准测试中达到与32B模型相当的性能，在代码生成任务上比强后训练基线提高了pass@1最多5.9%。

Conclusion: ExecVerify通过结合可验证的白盒奖励和强化学习，有效提升了代码LLM的执行推理能力，使较小模型能够达到较大模型的性能水平，并显著改善代码生成质量。

Abstract: Code LLMs still struggle with code execution reasoning, especially in smaller models. Existing methods rely on supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with teacher-generated explanations, primarily in two forms: (1) input-output (I/O) prediction chains and (2) natural-language descriptions of execution traces. However, intermediate execution steps cannot be explicitly verified during SFT, so the training objective can reduce to merely matching teacher explanations. Moreover, training data is typically collected without explicit control over task difficulty. We introduce ExecVerify, which goes beyond text imitation by incorporating verifiable white-box rewards derived from execution traces, including next-statement prediction and variable value/type prediction. Our work first builds a dataset with multiple difficulty levels via constraint-based program synthesis. Then, we apply reinforcement learning (RL) to reward correct answers about both intermediate execution steps and final outputs, aligning the training objective with semantic correctness at each execution step. Finally, we adopt a two-stage training pipeline that first enhances execution reasoning and then transfers to code generation. Experiments demonstrate that a 7B model trained with ExecVerify achieves performance comparable to 32B models on code reasoning benchmarks and improves pass@1 by up to 5.9\% on code generation tasks over strong post-training baselines.

</details>


### [12] [Unveiling Practical Shortcomings of Patch Overfitting Detection Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11262)
*David Williams,Ioakim Avraam,Aldeida Aleti,Matias Martinez,Justyna Petke,Federica Sarro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 当前补丁过拟合检测工具在实际场景中效果有限，简单随机选择在71%-96%的情况下优于现有POD工具，需要新的检测技术和更实际的基准测试方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动程序修复（APR）可以减少开发人员调试时间，但自动生成的补丁可能通过测试却仍然不正确（补丁过拟合）。现有补丁过拟合检测技术的研究往往在孤立环境下评估，使用不反映实际APR工具生成补丁分布的数据集，因此需要了解这些技术在实际应用中的有效性。

Method: 1. 构建反映现实假设的数据集（即工具在相同实验条件下生成的补丁）；2. 使用这些数据对六种最先进的POD方法进行基准测试，涵盖静态分析、动态测试和学习方法；3. 与两种基于随机采样的基线方法进行比较（一种来自先前工作，一种本文提出）。

Result: 结果令人震惊：简单随机选择在71%到96%的情况下优于所有POD工具，具体比例取决于POD工具。这表明：1. 当前POD工具提供的实际效益有限；2. 任何POD工具必须在现实数据和随机采样基准上进行测试以证明其实际有效性。

Conclusion: 当前补丁过拟合检测工具在实际应用中效果不佳，需要开发新技术。APR社区应继续改进POD技术，并采用本文提出的实用基准测试方法。作者提供了数据和代码以促进采用。

Abstract: Automated Program Repair (APR) can reduce the time developers spend debugging, allowing them to focus on other aspects of software development. Automatically generated bug patches are typically validated through software testing. However, this method can lead to patch overfitting, i.e., generating patches that pass the given tests but are still incorrect.
  Patch correctness assessment (also known as overfitting detection) techniques have been proposed to identify patches that overfit. However, prior work often assessed the effectiveness of these techniques in isolation and on datasets that do not reflect the distribution of correct-to-overfitting patches that would be generated by APR tools in typical use; thus, we still do not know their effectiveness in practice.
  This work presents the first comprehensive benchmarking study of several patch overfitting detection (POD) methods in a practical scenario. To this end, we curate datasets that reflect realistic assumptions (i.e., patches produced by tools run under the same experimental conditions). Next, we use these data to benchmark six state-of-the-art POD approaches -- spanning static analysis, dynamic testing, and learning-based approaches -- against two baselines based on random sampling (one from prior work and one proposed herein).
  Our results are striking: Simple random selection outperforms all POD tools for 71% to 96% of cases, depending on the POD tool. This suggests two main takeaways: (1) current POD tools offer limited practical benefit, highlighting the need for novel techniques; (2) any POD tool must be benchmarked on realistic data and against random sampling to prove its practical effectiveness. To this end, we encourage the APR community to continue improving POD techniques and to adopt our proposed methodology for practical benchmarking; we make our data and code available to facilitate such adoption.

</details>


### [13] [Enhancing Requirements Traceability Link Recovery: A Novel Approach with T-SimCSE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11800)
*Ye Wang,Wenqing Wang,Kun Hu,Qiao Huang,Liping Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出基于SimCSE预训练语言模型的T-SimCSE方法，用于无监督的需求可追溯性链接恢复，通过相似度计算和特异性度量重排序来提高准确性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于预训练语言模型的需求可追溯性链接恢复方法准确性不足，且需要大量标注数据进行训练，而当前可用标注数据集非常有限

Method: 提出T-SimCSE方法：1) 使用SimCSE模型计算需求与目标工件之间的相似度；2) 引入新的特异性度量对目标工件进行重排序；3) 在需求与排名前K的目标工件之间创建追溯链接

Result: 在10个公共数据集上的评估表明，T-SimCSE在召回率和平均精度均值(MAP)方面优于其他方法

Conclusion: T-SimCSE是一种有效的无监督需求可追溯性链接恢复方法，不需要标注数据，具有广泛适用性和良好性能

Abstract: Requirements traceability plays an important role in ensuring software quality and responding to changes in requirements. Requirements trace links (such as the links between requirements and other software artifacts) underpin the modeling and implementation of requirements traceability. With the rapid development of artificial intelligence, more and more pre-trained language models (PLMs) techniques are applied to the automatic recovery of requirements trace links. However, the requirements traceability links recovered by these approaches are not accurate enough, and many approaches require a large labeled dataset for training. Currently, there are very few labeled datasets available. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a novel requirements traceability link recovery approach called T-SimCSE, which is based on a PLM -- SimCSE. SimCSE has the advantages of not requiring labeled data, having broad applicability, and performing well. T-SimCSE firstly uses the SimCSE model to calculate the similarity between requirements and target artifacts, and employs a new metric (i.e. specificity) to reorder those target artifacts. Finally, the trace links are created between the requirement and the top-K target artifacts. We have evaluated T-SimCSE on ten public datasets by comparing them with other approaches. The results show that T-SimCSE achieves superior performance in terms of recall and Mean Average Precision (MAP).

</details>


### [14] [QUARE: Multi-Agent Negotiation for Balancing Quality Attributes in Requirements Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11890)
*Haowei Cheng,Milhan Kim,Foutse Khomh,Teeradaj Racharak,Nobukazu Yoshioka,Naoyasu Ubayashi,Hironori Washizaki*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: QUARE是一个多智能体框架，通过五个质量专业智能体（安全性、效率、绿色、可信性、责任性）的结构化协商协议，将需求工程自动化，显著提升合规性覆盖和语义保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求工程对软件成功至关重要，但自动化面临挑战，因为需要平衡多个经常冲突的质量属性同时保持利益相关者意图。现有LLM方法主要依赖单体推理或隐式聚合，限制了系统性地发现和解决跨质量冲突的能力。

Method: 提出QUARE多智能体框架，将需求分析构建为五个质量专业智能体（安全性、效率、绿色、可信性、责任性）之间的结构化协商，由专用协调器协调。引入辩证协商协议，明确暴露质量间冲突并通过迭代提议、批评和综合解决。协商结果通过拓扑验证转换为结构合理的KAOS目标模型，并通过检索增强生成（RAG）验证是否符合行业标准。

Result: 在五个案例研究（来自MARE、iReDev基准和工业自动驾驶规范）中，QUARE实现了98.2%的合规性覆盖（比基线提高105%）、94.9%的语义保持（比最佳基线提高2.3个百分点）和高可验证性（4.96/5.0），同时比现有多智能体RE框架生成25-43%更多需求。

Conclusion: 有效的需求工程自动化更依赖于原则性的架构分解、明确的交互协议和自动化验证，而不是模型规模。QUARE通过结构化多智能体协商显著提升了需求工程的质量和合规性。

Abstract: Requirements engineering (RE) is critical to software success, yet automating it remains challenging because multiple, often conflicting quality attributes must be balanced while preserving stakeholder intent. Existing Large-Language-Model (LLM) approaches predominantly rely on monolithic reasoning or implicit aggregation, limiting their ability to systematically surface and resolve cross-quality conflicts. We present QUARE (Quality-Aware Requirements Engineering), a multi-agent framework that formulates requirements analysis as structured negotiation among five quality-specialized agents (Safety, Efficiency, Green, Trustworthiness, and Responsibility), coordinated by a dedicated orchestrator. QUARE introduces a dialectical negotiation protocol that explicitly exposes inter-quality conflicts and resolves them through iterative proposal, critique, and synthesis. Negotiated outcomes are transformed into structurally sound KAOS goal models via topology validation and verified against industry standards through retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). We evaluate QUARE on five case studies drawn from established RE benchmarks (MARE, iReDev) and an industrial autonomous-driving specification, spanning safety-critical, financial, and information-system domains. Results show that QUARE achieves 98.2% compliance coverage (+105% over both baselines), 94.9% semantic preservation (+2.3 percentage points over the best baseline), and high verifiability (4.96/5.0), while generating 25-43% more requirements than existing multi-agent RE frameworks. These findings suggest that effective RE automation depends less on model scale than on principled architectural decomposition, explicit interaction protocols, and automated verification.

</details>


### [15] [A Collaborative and Pattern-Based Training Approach to Knowledge Acquisition and Decision-Making During the Design of Software Architectures Courses: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11904)
*Wilson Libardo Pantoja Yepez,Luis Mariano Bibbo,Julio Ariel Hurtado Alegría*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 哥伦比亚和阿根廷两所大学合作开展软件架构教学实验，应用并评估培训模式，通过跨国团队协作项目验证教学效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决软件架构教学中面临的挑战：架构概念抽象、先修知识要求高、难以模拟真实项目环境、团队协作困难、资源限制等，同时满足行业对软件架构设计技能的需求。

Method: 1. 两所大学对比学习目标、课程设置和教学策略，寻找共同改进点；2. 选择并实施培训模式；3. 在课程最后阶段组织跨国团队进行全局开发迭代；4. 基于技术接受模型(TAM)评估培训模式的效果、感知有用性和易用性。

Result: 提出了一个培训模式目录，这些模式有助于模拟行业环境，结构化架构知识以实现渐进式学习。评估显示培训模式在提升学生能力方面有效，强调了通过多次项目经验逐步培养架构决策能力的重要性。

Conclusion: 软件架构决策能力需要时间和多个项目经验来培养，强调需要实践性、结构良好的培训项目。培训模式目录为在其他环境中复制高质量培训提供了有效框架。

Abstract: This article describes a collaborative learning experience on Software Architecture (SA) between Universidad del Cauca (UNICAUCA) in Colombia and Universidad Nacional de la Plata (UNPL) in Argentina. The goal was to apply and evaluate training patterns, identifying effective practices for replication in other contexts. During the planning phase, both universities compared learning objectives, curricula, and teaching strategies to find common ground for improving student training. Selected training patterns were implemented, and their impact on professors and students was measured. As an integrating activity, a global development experience was carried out in the final part of the course, merging the work teams of the two educational institutions in a development iteration. The evaluation of this experience focused on the competencies achieved through the training patterns, their perceived usefulness, and ease of use based on the Technology Acceptance Model (TAM). The training addressed industry needs for software architecture design skills despite challenges such as the abstract nature of architectures, prerequisite knowledge, difficulty in recreating realistic project environments, team collaboration challenges, and resource limitations. A catalog of training patterns was proposed to provide quality training. These patterns help simulate industry-like environments and structure architectural knowledge for incremental learning. The ability to make architectural decisions is developed over time and through multiple project experiences, emphasizing the need for practical, well-structured training programs.

</details>

### [16] [Interpreting Contrastive Embeddings in Specific Domains with Fuzzy Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12227)
*Javier Fumanal-Idocin,Mohammadreza Jamalifard,Javier Andreu-Perez*

Main category: cs.SC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合模糊规则分类系统和CLIP模型的方法，将自由文本转换为结构化表示，并在临床报告和电影评论两个领域进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实环境中（如法律程序和医疗记录）大量数据以自由文本形式存在，需要将其转换为结构化格式以便机器学习方法利用。虽然CLIP模型在文本向量表示方面表现良好，但在特定领域应用中仍存在问题。

Method: 使用模糊规则分类系统结合标准文本处理技术，将感兴趣的特征映射到CLIP模型创建的空间中，分析获得的规则、关联以及每个特征的重要性。

Result: 在临床报告和电影评论两个不同数据领域应用该方法，比较单独考虑每个领域和同时考虑两个领域时的结果，展示了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 讨论了该方法的局限性以及可能的改进方向，为特定领域文本结构化处理提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Free-style text is still one of the common ways in which data is registered in real environments, like legal procedures and medical records. Because of that, there have been significant efforts in the area of natural language processing to convert these texts into a structured format, which standard machine learning methods can then exploit. One of the most popular methods to embed text into a vectorial representation is the Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training model (CLIP), which was trained using both image and text. Although the representations computed by CLIP have been very successful in zero-show and few-shot learning problems, they still have problems when applied to a particular domain. In this work, we use a fuzzy rule-based classification system along with some standard text procedure techniques to map some of our features of interest to the space created by a CLIP model. Then, we discuss the rules and associations obtained and the importance of each feature considered. We apply this approach in two different data domains, clinical reports and film reviews, and compare the results obtained individually and when considering both. Finally, we discuss the limitations of this approach and how it could be further improved.

</details>

### [17] [Probabilistic Disjunctive Normal Forms in Temporal Logic and Automata Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11083)
*Alexander Kuznetsov*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: PDNFs将概率权重引入析取范式，通过向量空间代数组合证据，在时序逻辑和积分函数双重解释下形成Banach空间结构，连接逻辑、数值方法和连续概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逻辑系统缺乏对不确定性的表达能力，需要一种能同时处理逻辑结构和概率信息的统一框架，以桥接离散逻辑推理与连续概率分析。

Method: 提出概率析取范式(PDNFs)，为变量分配实数值权重；构建PDNF向量空间支持代数证据组合；将PDNF解释为时序逻辑构造上的概率分布和分区区间上的可积函数；建立Banach空间结构并应用泛函分析。

Result: 在指数参数化下，PDNF加法与贝叶斯证据融合一致；推导了从随机样本进行结果识别的界限；建立了逻辑、数值方法和连续概率之间的形式化桥梁。

Conclusion: PDNFs提供了一个统一的数学框架，能够同时表示逻辑结构和概率不确定性，支持代数证据组合，并为逻辑推理与概率分析的集成开辟了新途径。

Abstract: This article introduces probabilistic disjunctive normal forms (PDNFs) as a framework for representing and reasoning about uncertainty in logical systems. Unlike classical DNFs, PDNFs assign real-valued weights to variables, encoding probabilistic information about their presence, absence, or negation. Then we construct a vector space of PDNFs that allows algebraic evidence combination. PDNFs are interpreted as probability distributions over venjunctions (temporal logic constructs) and as integrable functions over partitioned intervals, where the integrals determine variable probabilities. This dual perspective allows for a Banach space structure and the application of functional analysis. We demonstrate that, under exponential parametrisation, PDNF addition aligns with Bayesian evidence fusion and derive bounds for outcome identification from random samples. The formalism thus bridges logic, numerical methods, and continuous probability.

</details>


### [18] [Witnesses for Fixpoint Games on Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11908)
*Barbara König,Karla Messing*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该论文构建了可用于推导定点博弈策略的见证，并证明了函数最小定点要么高于要么不低于给定边界。基于格论方法，通过伽罗瓦连接连接逻辑宇宙格和行为宇宙格，研究原始博弈和对偶博弈两种类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是开发一种通用框架，用于通过定点博弈验证系统属性。现有方法如标准互模拟和行为度量缺乏统一的见证构造理论，需要建立连接逻辑规范和行为语义的形式化桥梁，以支持更广泛的验证应用。

Method: 采用格论方法，通过伽罗瓦连接连接两个格：逻辑宇宙格（见证所在）和行为宇宙格（函数定义域）。研究两种定点博弈：原始博弈和对偶博弈，展示如何从见证推导博弈的获胜策略，以及从策略构造见证。方法基于格的基选择差异区分两种博弈类型。

Result: 建立了见证与定点博弈策略之间的双向对应关系：可以从见证推导获胜策略，也可以从策略构造见证。理论可实例化到已知案例：标准互模拟的区分公式构造、概率系统行为度量。作为新案例研究，应用于马尔可夫链终止概率的下界认证。

Conclusion: 提出了一个统一的格论框架，将定点博弈中的见证构造与策略推导形式化。该框架通过伽罗瓦连接桥接逻辑规范和行为语义，为系统验证提供了通用理论基础，并成功应用于互模拟、行为度量和马尔可夫链终止概率分析等多个领域。

Abstract: We construct witnesses that can be used to derive strategies in fixpoint games and provide proof that the least fixpoint of a function is either above or not below some given bound. We rely on a lattice-theoretical approach, including a Galois connection that connects a lattice representing the "logic universe", where the witness lives, with another lattice representing the "behaviour universe", over which the function is defined. In fact we consider two types of games -- primal and dual games -- and in both cases show how to derive winning strategies in the game from witnesses and construct witnesses from strategies. The two games differ wrt. their rules and the choice of basis of the lattice.
  The theory can be instantiated to well-known examples: in particular we compare with the construction of distinguishing formulas in standard bisimilarity and behavioural metrics for probabilistic systems. As a new case study we consider witnesses for certifying lower bounds for the termination probability for Markov chains.

</details>


### [19] [When do modal definability and preservation theorems transfer to the finite?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12171)
*Johan van Benthem,Balder ten Cate,Xi Yang*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 研究模态逻辑在有限结构上的可定义性和保持性结果，探讨哪些经典定理在有限域中仍然成立


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶逻辑的许多转移定理在有限结构上失效，因此需要研究模态逻辑的经典定义性和保持性结果在有限结构上的表现，以理解模态逻辑在有限域中的行为特征

Method: 通过分析模态公式类在有限结构上的语义特征，检验经典模态定理的有限版本，包括双模拟安全定理、Goldblatt-Thomason定理和模态对应理论的有限类比

Result: 多个模态公式类的语义特征在有限结构中得以保持，但许多基本框架操作的一阶保持定理在有限域中失效；主要正面结果是双模拟安全定理成功转移到有限结构；还讨论了可计算性方面的问题

Conclusion: 模态逻辑在有限结构上的行为与无限结构存在显著差异：虽然一些经典定理（如双模拟安全定理）仍然成立，但许多一阶保持定理在有限域中失效，这为有限模态逻辑理论提供了新的研究视角

Abstract: We study which classic modal definability and preservation results survive when attention is restricted to finite structures, where many first-order transfer theorems are known to break down. Several semantic characterizations for modal formula classes survive the passage to the finite, while a number of first-order preservation theorems for basic frame operations fail. Our main positive result is that the Bisimulation Safety Theorem does transfer to finite structures. We also discuss computability aspects, and analogues in the finite for the Goldblatt-Thomason theorem and for modal correspondence theory.

</details>


### [20] [Coalgebraic Path Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12204)
*Todd Schmid*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种称为"等式路径约束"的新方法，用于公理化余代数余簇，作为传统余方程方法的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的余代数余簇公理化方法（如余代数模态逻辑、模式避免规范和隐藏代数）不够直观，而传统的代数簇方程公理化方法在余代数中不适用。需要一种更易描述、行为良好的方法来定义余代数行为性质。

Method: 引入"等式路径约束"概念，基本思想是为余代数中的每条路径分配一对值，并要求这两个值相等。该方法将路径计算与等式约束相结合，在某些情况下构造相对于等式路径约束的最终余代数，并与余方程建立联系。

Result: 证明等式路径约束能够定义余簇，在具体案例中构造了相对于这些约束的最终余代数。当路径计算的值位于单子中时，建立了等式路径约束与余方程的联系，并计算了表达余方程所需颜色数量的上界。

Conclusion: 等式路径约束提供了一种代数风格的、相对容易描述的有限行为性质类，可作为余方程的替代方案。该方法在自动机理论、微分方程、双向无限流和框架条件等应用中有实际意义。

Abstract: Axiomatizing covarieties of coalgebras for an endofunctor is less intuitive than axiomatizing varieties of algebras via equations (Dahlqvist and Schmid, 2022). Existing techniques come from coalgebraic modal logic, pattern avoidance specifications, and hidden algebra. We introduce equational path constraints, a well-behaved and relatively easy to describe class of finitary behavioural properties that provide an algebra-flavoured alternative to coequations. The basic idea is to assign a pair of values to each path through a coalgebra and posit that the two values coincide. We show that equational path constraints define covarieties and construct final coalgebras relative to equational path constraints in some concrete cases. We connect equational path constraints to coequations when values computed from paths live in a monad, and we compute an upper bound on the number of colours needed to express the coequation. One of our constructions is reminiscent of the initial/terminal sequences of (Adámek, 1974) and (Barr, 1993). Motivating examples include commutativity conditions in automata theory, differential equations, bi-infinite streams, and frame conditions.

</details>

### [21] [Stable Spike: Dual Consistency Optimization via Bitwise AND Operations for Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11676)
*Yongqi Ding,Kunshan Yang,Linze Li,Yiyang Zhang,Mengmeng Jing,Lin Zuo*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 通过Stable Spike方法进行双重一致性优化，提高SNN的识别性能，在超低延迟下显著提升神经形态物体识别准确率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN的时间脉冲动态虽然具有低功耗时间模式捕获能力，但存在固有的不一致性，严重损害了表示能力。需要解决这种不一致性问题以提高SNN的识别性能。

Method: 采用Stable Spike方法进行双重一致性优化：1) 使用硬件友好的"AND"位操作从多时间步脉冲图中解耦稳定脉冲骨架，捕获关键语义同时减少可变噪声脉冲的不一致性；2) 强制不稳定脉冲图收敛到稳定脉冲骨架，提高时间步间固有一致性；3) 向稳定脉冲骨架注入幅度感知脉冲噪声，在保持一致语义的同时多样化表示，鼓励SNN产生扰动一致的预测以提升泛化能力。

Result: 在多种架构和数据集上的广泛实验验证了方法的有效性和通用性。特别是在超低延迟下的神经形态物体识别方面取得显著进展，准确率最高提升8.33%。

Conclusion: 该方法有助于释放SNN在功耗和速度方面的全部潜力，通过双重一致性优化有效解决了SNN固有的不一致性问题，显著提升了识别性能。

Abstract: Although the temporal spike dynamics of spiking neural networks (SNNs) enable low-power temporal pattern capture capabilities, they also incur inherent inconsistencies that severely compromise representation. In this paper, we perform dual consistency optimization via Stable Spike to mitigate this problem, thereby improving the recognition performance of SNNs. With the hardware-friendly ``AND" bit operation, we efficiently decouple the stable spike skeleton from the multi-timestep spike maps, thereby capturing critical semantics while reducing inconsistencies from variable noise spikes. Enforcing the unstable spike maps to converge to the stable spike skeleton significantly improves the inherent consistency across timesteps. Furthermore, we inject amplitude-aware spike noise into the stable spike skeleton to diversify the representations while preserving consistent semantics. The SNN is encouraged to produce perturbation-consistent predictions, thereby contributing to generalization. Extensive experiments across multiple architectures and datasets validate the effectiveness and versatility of our method. In particular, our method significantly advances neuromorphic object recognition under ultra-low latency, improving accuracy by up to 8.33\%. This will help unlock the full power consumption and speed potential of SNNs.

</details>

### [22] [BEACON: Budget-Aware Entity Matching Across Domains (Extended Technical Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11391)
*Nicholas Pulsone,Roee Shraga,Gregory Goren*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: BEACON是一个分布感知、预算感知的框架，用于跨领域的低资源实体匹配，通过有效选择跨域样本在有限领域监督下提升匹配性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实体匹配是数据集成中的核心任务，深度学习特别是预训练语言模型和大语言模型取得了新标准，但微调需要大量标注数据。在电子商务匹配中，不同领域的标注稀缺性差异很大，如何在有限标注数据下智能训练准确的领域特定实体匹配模型成为关键问题。

Method: BEACON框架假设用户对特定目标领域只有有限标注数据，但可以访问其他领域的标注数据。该框架利用成对候选匹配的嵌入表示来指导在有限领域监督下有效选择跨域样本，是一个分布感知、预算感知的框架。

Result: 在多个基于现有实体匹配基准构建的领域划分数据集上进行广泛实验，结果表明BEACON在不同训练预算下始终优于最先进的方法。

Conclusion: BEACON为跨领域的低资源实体匹配提供了一个有效的解决方案，能够在有限领域标注数据下通过智能选择跨域样本显著提升匹配性能。

Abstract: Entity Matching (EM)--the task of determining whether two data records refer to the same real-world entity--is a core task in data integration. Recent advances in deep learning have set a new standard for EM, particularly through fine-tuning Pretrained Language Models (PLMs) and, more recently, Large Language Models (LLMs). However, fine-tuning typically requires large amounts of labeled data, which are expensive and time-consuming to obtain. In the context of e-commerce matching, labeling scarcity varies widely across domains, raising the question of how to intelligently train accurate domain-specific EM models with limited labeled data. In this work we assume users have only limited amount of labels for a specific target domain but have access to labeled data from other domains. We introduce BEACON, a distribution-aware, budget-aware framework for low-resource EM across domains. BEACON leverages the insight that embedding representations of pairwise candidate matches can guide the effective selection of out-of-domain samples under limited in-domain supervision. We conduct extensive experiments across multiple domain-partitioned datasets derived from established EM benchmarks, demonstrating that BEACON consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods under different training budgets.

</details>


### [23] [Faster Relational Algorithms Using Geometric Data Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11402)
*Aryan Esmailpour,Stavros Sintos*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出RBBD树框架，在关系数据上实现高效几何算法，避免连接操作的高昂代价，将k中心/均值/中位数聚类运行时间提升k倍


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系数据上的优化任务（如聚类）面临连接操作的高昂代价问题，传统几何数据结构（如BBD树）在关系数据上的应用不明确，需要专门针对关系设置的设计

Method: 开发RBBD树（随机化BBD树变体），利用关系连接的高效采样和计数技术，实现按需扩展RBBD树，无需物化连接结果即可模拟几何查询过程

Result: 提出的算法将关系k-中心/均值/中位数聚类的运行时间提升了k倍，同时保持相同的近似保证，方法具有通用性

Conclusion: RBBD树框架成功将几何洞察应用于关系数据，避免了连接操作的高昂代价，为关系设置中的各种优化问题提供了通用解决方案

Abstract: Optimization tasks over relational data, such as clustering, often suffer from the prohibitive cost of join operations, which are necessary to access the full dataset. While geometric data structures like BBD trees yield fast approximation algorithms in the standard computational setting, their application to relational data remains unclear due to the size of the join output. In this paper, we introduce a framework that leverages geometric insights to design faster algorithms when the data is stored as the results of a join query in a relational database. Our core contribution is the development of the RBBD tree, a randomized variant of the BBD tree tailored for relational settings. Instead of completely constructing the RBBD tree, by leveraging efficient sampling and counting techniques over relational joins, we enable on-the-fly efficient expansion of the RBBD tree, maintaining only the necessary parts. This allows us to simulate geometric query procedures without materializing the join result. As an application, we present algorithms that improve the state-of-the-art for relational $k$-center/means/median clustering by a factor of $k$ in running time while maintaining the same approximation guarantees. Our method is general and can be applied to various optimization problems in the relational setting.

</details>


### [24] [LHGstore: An In-Memory Learned Graph Storage for Fast Updates and Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11596)
*Pengpeng Qiao,Zhiwei Zhang,Xinzhou Wang,Zhetao Li,Xiaochun Cao,Yang Cao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: LHGstore是一种面向内存动态图管理的学习型层次化图存储系统，通过两级层次结构和度感知的数据结构设计，在高度偏斜的度分布下平衡更新效率和遍历局部性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中的内存动态图需要同时支持频繁更新和低延迟分析，但在高度偏斜的度分布下，更新效率和遍历局部性之间存在权衡，现有系统难以同时满足这两个目标。

Method: LHGstore采用度感知的学习型层次化图存储设计：1) 两级层次结构分离顶点和边访问；2) 根据顶点度数自适应选择数据结构：低度顶点使用轻量级数组以最大化遍历局部性，高度顶点使用学习型索引以提高更新吞吐量。

Result: 实验表明，LHGstore相比最先进的内存图存储系统实现了5.9-28.2倍的吞吐量提升，同时显著加快了分析查询速度。

Conclusion: LHGstore首次将学习型索引集成到图管理中，通过度感知的层次化设计有效平衡了动态图的更新效率和遍历局部性，为内存图管理提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Various real-world applications rely on in-memory dynamic graphs that must efficiently handle frequent updates while supporting low-latency analytics on evolving structures. Achieving both objectives remains challenging due to the trade-off between update efficiency and traversal locality, particularly under highly skewed degree distributions. This motivates the design of graph indexing schemes optimized for in-memory graph management on modern multi-core CPUs. We present LHGstore, a degree-aware Learned Hierarchical Graph storage that, for the first time, integrates learned indexing into graph management. LHGstore designs a two-level hierarchy that decouples vertex and edge access and further organizes each vertex's edges using data structures adaptive to its degree. Lightweight arrays are used for low-degree vertices to maximize traversal locality, while learned indexes are applied to high-degree vertices to improve update throughput. Extensive experiments show that LHGstore achieves 5.9-28.2$\times$ higher throughput and significantly faster analytics than SOTA in-memory graph storage systems.

</details>


### [25] [Sema: A High-performance System for LLM-based Semantic Query Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11622)
*Kangkang Qi,Dongyang Xie,Wenbo Li,Hao Zhang,Yuanyuan Zhu,Jeffrey Xu Yu,Kangfei Zhao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Sema是一个基于DuckDB的高性能语义查询引擎，将LLM驱动的语义算子作为一等公民，通过SemaSQL声明式方言实现端到端优化，在保持结果质量的同时获得2-10倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统要么依赖缺乏高效执行基础设施的DataFrame原语，要么使用SQL UDFs将语义逻辑与查询优化器隔离并增加用户实现复杂度。LLM驱动的语义算子带来了新的挑战，包括高成本和不确定性，传统优化规则和成本模型无法适用。

Method: Sema在逻辑层面压缩自然语言表达式并从语义算子推导关系约束；在运行时采用自适应查询执行动态重排算子、融合语义操作和应用提示批处理，在精度约束下寻求令牌消耗和延迟的帕累托最优执行路径。

Result: 在分类、摘要和提取任务的20个语义查询评估中，Sema相比三个基线系统实现了2-10倍的加速，同时保持了竞争性的结果质量。

Conclusion: Sema通过将LLM驱动的语义算子作为一等公民，提供声明式SemaSQL方言和端到端优化，成功解决了现有系统在LLM集成到数据分析中的局限性，实现了高性能的语义查询处理。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into data analytics has unlocked powerful capabilities for reasoning over bulk structured and unstructured data. However, existing systems typically rely on either DataFrame primitives, which lack the efficient execution infrastructure of modern DBMSs, or SQL User-Defined Functions (UDFs), which isolate semantic logic from the query optimizer and burden users with implementation complexities. The LLM-powered semantic operators also bring new challenges due to the high cost and non-deterministic nature of LLM invocation, where conventional optimization rules and cost models are inapplicable for their optimization.
  To bridge these gaps, we present Sema, a high-performance semantic query engine built on DuckDB that treats LLM-powered semantic operators as first-class citizens. Sema introduces SemaSQL, a declarative dialect that allows users seamlessly inject natural language expressions into standard SQL clauses, enabling end-to-end optimization and execution. At the logical level, the optimizer of Sema compresses natural language expressions and deduces relational constraints from semantic operators. At runtime, Sema employs Adaptive Query Execution (AQE) to dynamically reorder operators, fuse semantic operations, and apply prompt batching. This approach seeks a Pareto-optimal execution path balancing token consumption and latency under accuracy constraints. We evaluate Sema on 20 semantic queries across classification, summarization, and extraction tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that Sema achieves $2-10 \times$ speedup against three baseline systems while achieving competitive result quality.

</details>


### [26] [OMNIA: Closing the Loop by Leveraging LLMs for Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11820)
*Frédéric Ieng,Soror Sahri,Mourad Ouzzani,Massinissa Hammaz,Salima Benbernou,Hanieh Khorashadizadeh,Sven Groppe,Farah Benamara*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: OMNIA：一种用于知识图谱补全的两阶段方法，通过聚类语义相关实体和关系生成候选三元组，再通过轻量级嵌入过滤和LLM语义验证，无需外部源，专门针对LLM生成图谱中的隐式语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱补全方法存在局限性：基于结构嵌入的方法忽略语义信息，而基于语言模型的方法忽视图结构且依赖外部源。LLM自动构建的知识图谱通常不完整且有噪声，需要一种能同时利用结构和语义信息、无需外部源的方法来补全隐式语义。

Method: OMNIA采用两阶段方法：1）候选生成阶段：在知识图谱内部聚类语义相关的实体和关系，生成候选三元组；2）验证阶段：先进行轻量级嵌入过滤，然后使用LLM进行语义验证。该方法专门针对LLM生成图谱中的隐式语义，完全基于内部KG，无需外部源。

Result: 在多个数据集上的广泛实验表明，OMNIA相比传统基于嵌入的模型显著提高了F1分数。聚类和过滤阶段减少了搜索空间和验证成本，同时保持了高质量的补全效果。

Conclusion: OMNIA有效桥接了结构和语义推理，通过两阶段方法实现了高效且高质量的知识图谱补全，特别适用于处理LLM生成图谱中的隐式语义，无需依赖外部源。

Abstract: Knowledge Graphs (KGs) are widely used to represent structured knowledge, yet their automatic construction, especially with Large Language Models (LLMs), often results in incomplete or noisy outputs. Knowledge Graph Completion (KGC) aims to infer and add missing triples, but most existing methods either rely on structural embeddings that overlook semantics or language models that ignore the graph's structure and depend on external sources. In this work, we present OMNIA, a two-stage approach that bridges structural and semantic reasoning for KGC. It first generates candidate triples by clustering semantically related entities and relations within the KG, then validates them through lightweight embedding filtering followed by LLM-based semantic validation. OMNIA performs on the internal KG, without external sources, and specifically targets implicit semantics that are most frequent in LLM-generated graphs. Extensive experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that OMNIA significantly improves F1-score compared to traditional embedding-based models. These results highlight OMNIA's effectiveness and efficiency, as its clustering and filtering stages reduce both search space and validation cost while maintaining high-quality completion.

</details>


### [27] [Numerical benchmark for damage identification in Structural Health Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12069)
*Francesca Marafini,Giacomo Zini,Alberto Barontini,Nuno Mendes,Alice Cicirello,Michele Betti,Gianni Bartoli*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个用于结构健康监测(SHM)验证的模拟数据集，包含动态和静态测量数据，考虑了环境变化、损伤、噪声等多种现实因素，并提供开源代码和数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工程领域缺乏用于验证数据驱动方法和混合物理-数据方法的公开数据集，数据所有权、安全性、访问权限和元数据可用性等问题阻碍了结构健康监测等领域的发展。

Method: 采用固定-固定钢梁结构作为数值基准，使用单自由度系统生成加速度记录，采用欧拉-伯努利梁理论模拟位移测量。通过并行计算生成包含环境变化、不同类型损伤、测量噪声和传感器故障的模拟数据。

Result: 创建了一个全面的SHM模拟数据集，包含动态加速度和静态位移测量，考虑了多种现实场景。提供开源代码和数据，确保可重复性、可重现性和可访问性。

Conclusion: 该数据集为研究人员开发和改进SHM技术提供了有价值的资源，通过高质量合成数据的可访问性促进了该领域的进步。

Abstract: The availability of a dataset for validation and verification purposes of novel data-driven strategies and/or hybrid physics-data approaches is currently one of the most pressing challenges in the engineering field. Data ownership, security, access and metadata handiness are currently hindering advances across many fields, particularly in Structural Health Monitoring (SHM) applications. This paper presents a simulated SHM dataset, comprised of dynamic and static measurements (i.e., acceleration and displacement), and includes the conceptual framework designed to generate it. The simulated measurements were generated to incorporate the effects of Environmental and Operational Variations (EOVs), different types of damage, measurement noise and sensor faults and malfunctions, in order to account for scenarios that may occur during real acquisitions. A fixed-fixed steel beam structure was chosen as reference for the numerical benchmark. The simulated monitoring was operated under the assumptions of a Single Degree of Freedom (SDOF) for generating acceleration records and of the Euler-Bernoulli beam for the simulated displacement measurements. The generation process involved the use of parallel computation, which is detailed within the provided open-source code. The generated data is also available open-source, thus ensuring reproducibility, repeatability and accessibility for further research. The comprehensive description of data types, formats, and collection methodologies makes this dataset a valuable resource for researchers aiming to develop or refine SHM techniques, fostering advancements in the field through accessible, high-quality synthetic data.

</details>


### [28] [Structure Selection for Fairness-Constrained Differentially Private Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12112)
*Naeim Ghahramanpour,Mostafa Milani*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: PrivCI：一种在差分隐私合成数据生成中强制执行条件独立约束的方法，通过CI感知的最小生成树算法改进公平性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准差分隐私合成数据生成器会保留数据中的所有依赖关系，包括敏感属性与结果之间的虚假相关性，这在公平性关键场景中会重现不希望的偏见。需要一种方法来强制执行条件独立性约束，以编码领域知识或法律要求。

Method: 提出PrivCI方法，在差分隐私合成数据生成的两个阶段中：1）测量阶段通过CI感知的贪婪MST算法在指数机制下整合可行性检查，强制执行条件独立性约束；2）重构阶段拟合与噪声边际一致的模型。

Result: 在标准公平性基准测试中，PrivCI相比现有基线方法实现了更强的保真度和预测准确性，同时满足指定的条件独立性约束。

Conclusion: PrivCI通过在差分隐私合成数据生成的测量阶段集成条件独立性约束，提供了一种在保护隐私的同时确保公平性的有效方法，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) enables safe data release, with synthetic data generation emerging as a common approach in recent years. Yet standard synthesizers preserve all dependencies in the data, including spurious correlations between sensitive attributes and outcomes. In fairness-critical settings, this reproduces unwanted bias. A principled remedy is to enforce conditional independence (CI) constraints, which encode domain knowledge or legal requirements that outcomes be independent of sensitive attributes once admissible factors are accounted for. DP synthesis typically proceeds in two phases: (i) a measure- ment step that privatizes selected marginals, often structured via minimum spanning trees (MSTs), and (ii) a reconstruction step that fits a probabilistic model consistent with the noisy marginals. We propose PrivCI, which enforces CI during the measurement step via a CI-aware greedy MST algorithm that integrates feasibility checks into Kruskal's construction under the exponential mechanism, improving accuracy over competing methods. Experiments on standard fairness benchmarks show that PrivCI achieves stronger fidelity and predictive accuracy than prior baselines while satisfying the specified CI constraints.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [29] [Comparison of Outlier Detection Algorithms on String Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11049)
*Philip Maus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文比较了两种字符串异常检测算法：基于编辑距离的局部离群因子算法变体和基于层次正则表达式学习的新算法，实验证明两者都能有效检测字符串异常，但各有适用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前异常检测研究主要集中在数值数据上，对字符串数据的异常检测研究较少。开发稳健的字符串异常检测算法有助于数据清洗和系统日志文件中的异常检测。

Method: 1. 提出局部离群因子算法的变体，使用Levenshtein编辑距离计算数据集密度，并引入考虑层次字符类别的加权Levenshtein度量；2. 提出基于层次左正则表达式学习的新算法，通过推断预期数据的正则表达式来检测异常。

Result: 实验表明两种算法都能在概念上发现字符串数据中的异常。基于正则表达式的算法在预期值具有明显结构且与异常结构差异足够大时表现优异；而局部离群因子算法在异常与预期数据的编辑距离与预期数据之间的编辑距离差异足够明显时效果最佳。

Conclusion: 两种字符串异常检测算法各有优势：正则表达式方法适用于结构差异明显的场景，而编辑距离方法适用于距离差异明显的场景。这为字符串异常检测提供了两种互补的技术路径。

Abstract: Outlier detection is a well-researched and crucial problem in machine learning. However, there is little research on string data outlier detection, as most literature focuses on outlier detection of numerical data. A robust string data outlier detection algorithm could assist with data cleaning or anomaly detection in system log files. In this thesis, we compare two string outlier detection algorithms. Firstly, we introduce a variant of the well-known local outlier factor algorithm, which we tailor to detect outliers on string data using the Levenshtein measure to calculate the density of the dataset. We present a differently weighted Levenshtein measure, which considers hierarchical character classes and can be used to tune the algorithm to a specific string dataset. Secondly, we introduce a new kind of outlier detection algorithm based on the hierarchical left regular expression learner, which infers a regular expression for the expected data. Using various datasets and parameters, we experimentally show that both algorithms can conceptually find outliers in string data. We show that the regular expression-based algorithm is especially good at finding outliers if the expected values have a distinct structure that is sufficiently different from the structure of the outliers. In contrast, the local outlier factor algorithms are best at finding outliers if their edit distance to the expected data is sufficiently distinct from the edit distance between the expected data.

</details>


### [30] [Fingerprinting Concepts in Data Streams with Supervised and Unsupervised Meta-Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11094)
*Ben Halstead,Yun Sing Koh,Patricia Riddle,Mykola Pechenizkiy,Albert Bifet,Russel Pears*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FiCSUM是一个用于数据流概念漂移检测的通用框架，通过构建包含多种元信息特征的概念指纹来更精确地表示概念，并使用动态加权策略学习哪些特征对特定数据集的概念漂移描述最有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据流处理中概念漂移是一个主要挑战，现有概念表示方法依赖少量元信息特征，往往无法区分不同概念，导致系统对概念漂移的脆弱性。需要更强大的概念表示方法来提高概念漂移检测的准确性。

Method: 提出FiCSUM框架：1) 构建概念指纹，即包含多种不同元信息特征的向量，能够唯一标识更多概念；2) 采用动态加权策略，学习哪些元信息特征在特定数据集中最能描述概念漂移；3) 支持监督和无监督行为的概念表示。

Result: 在11个真实世界和合成数据集上，FiCSUM在准确性和建模底层概念漂移方面均优于最先进的方法。

Conclusion: FiCSUM通过丰富的元信息特征概念表示和动态特征加权，显著提高了概念漂移检测能力，为数据流处理中的概念漂移适应提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Streaming sources of data are becoming more common as the ability to collect data in real-time grows. A major concern in dealing with data streams is concept drift, a change in the distribution of data over time, for example, due to changes in environmental conditions. Representing concepts (stationary periods featuring similar behaviour) is a key idea in adapting to concept drift. By testing the similarity of a concept representation to a window of observations, we can detect concept drift to a new or previously seen recurring concept. Concept representations are constructed using meta-information features, values describing aspects of concept behaviour. We find that previously proposed concept representations rely on small numbers of meta-information features. These representations often cannot distinguish concepts, leaving systems vulnerable to concept drift. We propose FiCSUM, a general framework to represent both supervised and unsupervised behaviours of a concept in a fingerprint, a vector of many distinct meta-information features able to uniquely identify more concepts. Our dynamic weighting strategy learns which meta-information features describe concept drift in a given dataset, allowing a diverse set of meta-information features to be used at once. FiCSUM outperforms state-of-the-art methods over a range of 11 real world and synthetic datasets in both accuracy and modeling underlying concept drift.

</details>


### [31] [Learning Tree-Based Models with Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11117)
*Sascha Marton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种通过梯度下降学习硬轴对齐决策树的新方法，使用直通算子的反向传播在密集DT表示上联合优化所有树参数，克服传统贪婪算法的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统决策树学习方法（如CART）存在两个主要问题：1）使用贪婪搜索过程，在每个节点做局部最优决策，限制了搜索空间并导致次优树结构；2）需要定制训练方法，无法无缝集成到依赖梯度下降的现代机器学习方法中。

Method: 提出基于梯度下降的决策树学习方法，使用带有直通算子的反向传播在密集决策树表示上进行训练，能够联合优化所有树参数，而不是顺序选择局部最优分割。

Result: 在多个领域取得最先进结果：小表格数据集的解释性决策树、复杂表格数据的高级模型、多模态学习、以及无信息损失的解释性强化学习。

Conclusion: 通过弥合决策树与基于梯度优化的差距，该方法显著提升了树模型在各种机器学习领域的性能和适用性，实现了与现有ML方法的无缝集成。

Abstract: Tree-based models are widely recognized for their interpretability and have proven effective in various application domains, particularly in high-stakes domains. However, learning decision trees (DTs) poses a significant challenge due to their combinatorial complexity and discrete, non-differentiable nature. As a result, traditional methods such as CART, which rely on greedy search procedures, remain the most widely used approaches. These methods make locally optimal decisions at each node, constraining the search space and often leading to suboptimal tree structures. Additionally, their demand for custom training methods precludes a seamless integration into modern machine learning (ML) approaches.
  In this thesis, we propose a novel method for learning hard, axis-aligned DTs through gradient descent. Our approach utilizes backpropagation with a straight-through operator on a dense DT representation, enabling the joint optimization of all tree parameters, thereby addressing the two primary limitations of traditional DT algorithms. First, gradient-based training is not constrained by the sequential selection of locally optimal splits but, instead, jointly optimizes all tree parameters. Second, by leveraging gradient descent for optimization, our approach seamlessly integrates into existing ML approaches e.g., for multimodal and reinforcement learning tasks, which inherently rely on gradient descent.
  These advancements allow us to achieve state-of-the-art results across multiple domains, including interpretable DTs rees for small tabular datasets, advanced models for complex tabular data, multimodal learning, and interpretable reinforcement learning without information loss. By bridging the gap between DTs and gradient-based optimization, our method significantly enhances the performance and applicability of tree-based models across various ML domains.

</details>


### [32] [Higher-Order Modular Attention: Fusing Pairwise and Triadic Interactions for Protein Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11133)
*Shirin Amiraslani,Xin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HOMA引入高阶模块化注意力，融合成对注意力与显式三元交互通路，通过块结构窗口化三元注意力处理长序列，在蛋白质序列预测任务中相比标准自注意力获得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer自注意力计算成对token交互，但蛋白质序列到表型关系常涉及三个或更多残基的协同依赖，这是点积注意力无法显式捕获的。需要设计能捕捉高阶交互的注意力机制。

Method: 提出高阶模块化注意力(HOMA)，统一融合成对注意力通路与显式三元交互通路。为在长序列上实用，采用块结构、窗口化的三元注意力，控制计算复杂度。

Result: 在TAPE基准测试的三个任务（二级结构、荧光、稳定性）上评估，HOMA相比标准自注意力及其高效变体（块状注意力和Linformer）在所有任务中获得一致改进。

Conclusion: 显式三元项为蛋白质序列预测提供了互补的表征能力，且计算成本可控增加。高阶交互捕获对蛋白质建模具有重要价值。

Abstract: Transformer self-attention computes pairwise token interactions, yet protein sequence to phenotype relationships often involve cooperative dependencies among three or more residues that dot product attention does not capture explicitly. We introduce Higher-Order Modular Attention, HOMA, a unified attention operator that fuses pairwise attention with an explicit triadic interaction pathway. To make triadic attention practical on long sequences, HOMA employs block-structured, windowed triadic attention. We evaluate on three TAPE benchmarks for Secondary Structure, Fluorescence, and Stability. Our attention mechanism yields consistent improvements across all tasks compared with standard self-attention and efficient variants including block-wise attention and Linformer. These results suggest that explicit triadic terms provide complementary representational capacity for protein sequence prediction at controllable additional computational cost.

</details>


### [33] [Scaling Reasoning Efficiently via Relaxed On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11137)
*Jongwoo Ko,Sara Abdali,Young Jin Kim,Tianyi Chen,Pashmina Cameron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REOPOLD是一种稳定的在线策略蒸馏框架，通过松弛严格的模仿约束，将教师-学生对数似然比作为token奖励，实现更高效的推理能力迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏对于将推理能力迁移到容量受限模型至关重要，但传统方法存在不稳定性和负迁移问题。作者发现在线策略蒸馏可被解释为一种策略优化形式，其中教师-学生对数似然比充当token奖励，这为改进方法提供了理论基础。

Method: 提出REOPOLD框架，通过三种关键技术松弛标准在线策略蒸馏的严格模仿约束：1) 基于混合的奖励裁剪，温和选择性地利用教师奖励；2) 基于熵的token级动态采样；3) 统一的探索到精炼训练策略。

Result: REOPOLD在数学、视觉和工具使用推理任务上超越基线方法，训练时具有更优的样本效率，推理时表现出更好的扩展性。具体而言，比近期RL方法提高6.7~12倍样本效率，使7B学生模型在视觉推理上匹配32B教师模型，推理速度提升约3.32倍。

Conclusion: REOPOLD通过将在线策略蒸馏重新解释为策略优化问题并松弛模仿约束，实现了更稳定高效的推理能力迁移，为容量受限模型获得强大推理能力提供了有效框架。

Abstract: On-policy distillation is pivotal for transferring reasoning capabilities to capacity-constrained models, yet remains prone to instability and negative transfer. We show that on-policy distillation can be interpreted, both theoretically and empirically, as a form of policy optimization, where the teacher-student log-likelihood ratio acts as a token reward. From this insight, we introduce REOPOLD (Relaxed On-Policy Distillation) a framework that stabilizes optimization by relaxing the strict imitation constraints of standard on-policy distillation. Specifically, REOPOLD temperately and selectively leverages rewards from the teacher through mixture-based reward clipping, entropy-based token-level dynamic sampling, and a unified exploration-to-refinement training strategy. Empirically, REOPOLD surpasses its baselines with superior sample efficiency during training and enhanced test-time scaling at inference, across mathematical, visual, and agentic tool-use reasoning tasks. Specifically, REOPOLD outperforms recent RL approaches achieving 6.7~12x greater sample efficiency and enables a 7B student to match a 32B teacher in visual reasoning with a ~3.32x inference speedup.

</details>


### [34] [H2LooP Spark Preview: Continual Pretraining of Large Language Models for Low-Level Embedded Systems Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11139)
*Amit Singh,Vedant Nipane,Pulkit Agrawal,Jatin Kishnani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过持续预训练将OLMo-3-7B模型适配到嵌入式系统领域，在8个NVIDIA H100 GPU上使用BF16 LoRA训练，在13个嵌入式领域代码生成任务中超越Claude Opus 4.6和Qwen3-Coder-30B


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在通用编程语言上表现出色，但在嵌入式系统等专业领域（涉及硬件寄存器操作、厂商特定SDK、实时操作系统API和硬件抽象层）表现有限，这些内容在标准预训练语料中代表性不足

Method: 采用持续预训练管道H2LooP Spark Preview，使用BF16 LoRA配合秩稳定缩放技术，在8个NVIDIA H100 GPU上训练。训练语料基于仓库-数据手册对构建，涵盖100B原始嵌入式系统数据，使用SpecMap提出的分层数据手册到代码映射方法处理，最终得到23.5B tokens的精选数据集，覆盖13个嵌入式领域

Result: 高秩LoRA（r=512）持续预训练带来显著提升：领域内困惑度降低70.4%，保留仓库困惑度降低66.1%。在13个嵌入式领域的生成式代码补全基准测试中，7B模型在8个类别上超越Claude Opus 4.6和Qwen3-Coder-30B的token准确率，表明针对性持续预训练能使小型开源模型在专业任务上匹敌前沿系统

Conclusion: 针对嵌入式系统领域的持续预训练能显著提升语言模型在该专业领域的性能，使较小的开源模型在特定技术任务上达到甚至超越更大规模前沿系统的水平。训练检查点已在Huggingface开源发布

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong code generation abilities in general-purpose programming languages but remain limited in specialized domains such as low-level embedded systems programming. This domain involves hardware register manipulation, vendor-specific SDKs, real-time operating system APIs, and hardware abstraction layers that are underrepresented in standard pretraining corpora. We introduce H2LooP Spark Preview, a continual pretraining (CPT) pipeline that adapts the OLMo-3-7B-a fully open language model to the embedded systems domain using BF16 LoRA with rank-stabilized scaling on 8 NVIDIA H100 GPUs. Our training corpus is constructed from repository-datasheet pairs covering 100B tokens of raw embedded systems data across 117 manufacturers, processed using the hierarchical datasheet-to-code mapping approach proposed in SpecMap (Nipane et al., 2026). The resulting curated dataset split contains 23.5B tokens across 13 embedded domains. Continual pretraining with high-rank LoRA (r=512) yields substantial gains, reducing in-domain perplexity by 70.4% and held-out repository perplexity by 66.1%. On generative code completion benchmarks spanning 13 embedded domains, our 7B model outperforms Claude Opus 4.6 and Qwen3-Coder-30B on 8 categories in token accuracy, showing that targeted continual pretraining enables smaller open-weight models to rival frontier systems on specialized technical tasks. We release the production training checkpoint on Huggingface as an open-source artifact.

</details>


### [35] [Procedural Fairness via Group Counterfactual Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11140)
*Gideon Popoola,John Sheppard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GCIG框架，通过正则化强制不同保护群体间的解释不变性，实现程序公平性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习公平性研究主要关注结果公平性（如Equalized Odds），而忽视了程序公平性。模型可能对不同保护群体产生不同的解释，这会侵蚀信任。需要一种方法来确保模型推理过程在不同群体间保持一致。

Method: 提出Group Counterfactual Integrated Gradients (GCIG)框架，这是一种处理中的正则化方法。GCIG为每个输入计算相对于多个群体条件基线的解释，并在训练过程中惩罚这些归因的跨群体变异。将程序公平性形式化为群体反事实解释稳定性。

Result: 与六种最先进方法相比，GCIG显著减少了跨群体解释差异，同时保持了竞争力的预测性能和准确性-公平性权衡。结果表明，对齐跨群体的模型推理为实现超越结果平等的公平性提供了原则性和实用的途径。

Conclusion: GCIG通过强制解释不变性来增强程序公平性，补充了仅约束预测的现有公平性目标。对齐模型推理过程为推进公平性研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Fairness in machine learning research has largely focused on outcome-oriented fairness criteria such as Equalized Odds, while comparatively less attention has been given to procedural-oriented fairness, which addresses how a model arrives at its predictions. Neglecting procedural fairness means it is possible for a model to generate different explanations for different protected groups, thereby eroding trust. In this work, we introduce Group Counterfactual Integrated Gradients (GCIG), an in-processing regularization framework that enforces explanation invariance across groups, conditioned on the true label. For each input, GCIG computes explanations relative to multiple Group Conditional baselines and penalizes cross-group variation in these attributions during training. GCIG formalizes procedural fairness as Group Counterfactual explanation stability and complements existing fairness objectives that constrain predictions alone. We compared GCIG empirically against six state-of-the-art methods, and the results show that GCIG substantially reduces cross-group explanation disparity while maintaining competitive predictive performance and accuracy-fairness trade-offs. Our results also show that aligning model reasoning across groups offers a principled and practical avenue for advancing fairness beyond outcome parity.

</details>


### [36] [Attention Gathers, MLPs Compose: A Causal Analysis of an Action-Outcome Circuit in VideoViT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11142)
*Sai V R Chereddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 视频分类模型通过内部电路计算"成功vs失败"信号，该信号通过注意力头收集证据、MLP块组合概念的分布式冗余电路实现，揭示了模型训练中产生的超越显式任务的"隐藏知识"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究视频分类模型如何表示不影响最终结果的细微隐藏语义信息，这是可信AI模型的关键挑战。探索模型内部是否发展出超越显式任务的"隐藏知识"，为构建真正可解释和可信的AI系统提供机制性监督依据。

Method: 使用可解释AI方法，特别是机制可解释性技术，在预训练视频视觉Transformer中逆向工程表示动作结果的内部电路。主要采用激活修补（activation patching）和消融实验进行因果分析。

Result: 发现"成功vs失败"信号通过独特的放大级联计算。从第0层观察到低级差异，但结果的抽象语义表示从第5到11层逐渐放大。注意力头作为"证据收集器"提供低级信息，MLP块作为稳健的"概念组合器"生成成功信号。这种分布式冗余电路解释了模型对简单消融的韧性。

Conclusion: 即使在仅进行简单分类训练的模型中，也存在表示复杂结果的复杂电路，突显了模型可能发展出超越显式任务的"隐藏知识"。这强调了构建真正可解释和可信AI系统需要机制性监督。

Abstract: The paper explores how video models trained for classification tasks represent nuanced, hidden semantic information that may not affect the final outcome, a key challenge for Trustworthy AI models. Through Explainable and Interpretable AI methods, specifically mechanistic interpretability techniques, the internal circuit responsible for representing the action's outcome is reverse-engineered in a pre-trained video vision transformer, revealing that the "Success vs Failure" signal is computed through a distinct amplification cascade. While there are low-level differences observed from layer 0, the abstract and semantic representation of the outcome is progressively amplified from layers 5 through 11. Causal analysis, primarily using activation patching supported by ablation results, reveals a clear division of labor: Attention Heads act as "evidence gatherers", providing necessary low-level information for partial signal recovery, while MLP Blocks function as robust "concept composers", each of which is the primary driver to generate the "success" signal. This distributed and redundant circuit in the model's internals explains its resilience to simple ablations, demonstrating a core computational pattern for processing human-action outcomes. Crucially, the existence of this sophisticated circuit for representing complex outcomes, even within a model trained only for simple classification, highlights the potential for models to develop forms of 'hidden knowledge' beyond their explicit task, underscoring the need for mechanistic oversight for building genuinely Explainable and Trustworthy AI systems intended for deployment.

</details>


### [37] [Systematic Scaling Analysis of Jailbreak Attacks in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11149)
*Xiangwen Wang,Ananth Balashankar,Varun Chandrasekaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个用于评估越狱攻击的扩展定律框架，通过将攻击视为计算受限的优化过程，在统一的FLOPs轴上衡量进展，发现基于提示的方法比基于优化的方法更高效，且攻击效果高度依赖于目标类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对大型语言模型越狱攻击的理解缺乏系统性，特别是关于攻击成功率如何随攻击者投入（计算资源）在不同方法、模型家族和危害类型中扩展。需要建立一个统一的框架来量化评估越狱攻击的效率和扩展特性。

Method: 提出了一个扩展定律框架，将越狱攻击视为计算受限的优化过程，在共享的FLOPs轴上衡量进展。系统评估了四种代表性越狱范式：基于优化的攻击、自我精炼提示、基于采样的选择和遗传优化。通过拟合简单的饱和指数函数来分析FLOPs与攻击成功率之间的关系，并从拟合曲线中推导出可比较的效率摘要。

Result: 基于提示的范式在计算效率上优于基于优化的方法；攻击在成功率-隐蔽性操作点上存在差异，基于提示的方法占据高成功率、高隐蔽性区域；漏洞性高度依赖于目标类型，涉及错误信息的危害通常比其他非错误信息危害更容易引发。

Conclusion: 该研究为越狱攻击提供了系统的扩展定律分析框架，揭示了不同攻击方法的效率差异和操作特性。基于提示的方法在计算效率和隐蔽性方面具有优势，而攻击成功率对目标类型有强烈依赖性，这为理解和防御越狱攻击提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Large language models remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks, yet we still lack a systematic understanding of how jailbreak success scales with attacker effort across methods, model families, and harm types. We initiate a scaling-law framework for jailbreaks by treating each attack as a compute-bounded optimization procedure and measuring progress on a shared FLOPs axis. Our systematic evaluation spans four representative jailbreak paradigms, covering optimization-based attacks, self-refinement prompting, sampling-based selection, and genetic optimization, across multiple model families and scales on a diverse set of harmful goals. We investigate scaling laws that relate attacker budget to attack success score by fitting a simple saturating exponential function to FLOPs--success trajectories, and we derive comparable efficiency summaries from the fitted curves. Empirically, prompting-based paradigms tend to be the most compute-efficient compared to optimization-based methods. To explain this gap, we cast prompt-based updates into an optimization view and show via a same-state comparison that prompt-based attacks more effectively optimize in prompt space. We also show that attacks occupy distinct success--stealthiness operating points with prompting-based methods occupying the high-success, high-stealth region. Finally, we find that vulnerability is strongly goal-dependent: harms involving misinformation are typically easier to elicit than other non-misinformation harms.

</details>


### [38] [Huntington Disease Automatic Speech Recognition with Biomarker Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11168)
*Charles L. Wang,Cady Chen,Ziwei Gong,Julia Hirschberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了针对亨廷顿病（HD）病理语音的自动语音识别（ASR），比较了多种ASR架构，发现Parakeet-TDT表现最佳，并通过HD特定适应将WER从6.99%降至4.95%，同时分析了错误模式随疾病严重程度的变化规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理语音的自动语音识别（ASR）研究不足，特别是亨廷顿病（HD）语音具有不规则时序、不稳定发声和发音扭曲等特征，对现有ASR模型构成挑战。研究旨在填补HD-ASR领域的空白，使用未用于端到端ASR训练的高质量临床语音语料库进行系统性研究。

Method: 使用高质量临床HD语音语料库进行端到端ASR训练；在统一评估框架下比较多种ASR架构家族；分析词错误率（WER）及替换、删除、插入错误模式；提出HD特定适应方法；引入基于生物标志物的辅助监督方法；分析错误行为随疾病严重程度的变化。

Result: HD语音引发架构特定的错误模式，Parakeet-TDT在多种ASR架构中表现最佳；HD特定适应将WER从6.99%降至4.95%；基于生物标志物的辅助监督方法重塑了错误行为模式，而非均匀改善WER；错误模式随疾病严重程度呈现特定变化规律。

Conclusion: 该研究为HD病理语音ASR提供了系统性评估框架，证明了架构选择和特定适应策略的重要性，揭示了错误模式与疾病严重程度的关联性，为病理语音识别研究提供了新见解。所有代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) for pathological speech remains underexplored, especially for Huntington's disease (HD), where irregular timing, unstable phonation, and articulatory distortion challenge current models. We present a systematic HD-ASR study using a high-fidelity clinical speech corpus not previously used for end-to-end ASR training. We compare multiple ASR families under a unified evaluation, analyzing WER as well as substitution, deletion, and insertion patterns. HD speech induces architecture-specific error regimes, with Parakeet-TDT outperforming encoder-decoder and CTC baselines. HD-specific adaptation reduces WER from 6.99% to 4.95% and we also propose a method for using biomarker-based auxiliary supervision and analyze how error behavior is reshaped in severity-dependent ways rather than uniformly improving WER.
