Through extensive _in silico_ evaluation on diverse protein communities from UNIREF and OAS, we demonstrate that EvoFlows capture protein sequence distributions with a quality comparable to leading masked language models commonly used in protein engineering, while showing improved ability to generate non-trivial yet natural-like mutants from a given template protein.

</details>


### [77] [Mitigating the Multiplicity Burden: The Role of Calibration in Reducing Predictive Multiplicity of Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11750)
*Mustafa Cavus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了机器学习模型在信贷风险评估中分类校准与预测多样性之间的相互作用，发现少数类别样本承担了不成比例的多样性负担，而事后校准方法（特别是Platt Scaling和Isotonic Regression）能有效降低预测多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习模型在高风险环境中部署，确保概率可靠性和预测稳定性变得至关重要。研究旨在探索分类校准与预测多样性（即Rashomon集合中多个近最优模型对同一申请者产生冲突信用结果的现象）之间的相互作用。

Method: 使用九个不同的信用风险基准数据集，研究预测多样性是否集中在低预测置信度区域，以及事后校准如何减轻算法任意性。实证分析比较了三种事后校准方法：Platt Scaling、Isotonic Regression和Temperature Scaling。

Result: 研究发现少数类别观察值承担了不成比例的多样性负担，预测多样性和预测置信度存在显著差异。事后校准方法与Rashomon集合中较低的模糊度相关，其中Platt Scaling和Isotonic Regression对降低预测多样性效果最稳健。

Conclusion: 校准可以作为共识强制执行层，通过减轻预测多样性来支持程序公平性。研究结果表明，在信贷风险评估等高风险应用中，实施校准技术有助于提高模型决策的一致性和公平性。

Abstract: As machine learning models are increasingly deployed in high-stakes environments, ensuring both probabilistic reliability and prediction stability has become critical. This paper examines the interplay between classification calibration and predictive multiplicity - the phenomenon in which multiple near-optimal models within the Rashomon set yield conflicting credit outcomes for the same applicant. Using nine diverse credit risk benchmark datasets, we investigate whether predictive multiplicity concentrates in regions of low predictive confidence and how post-hoc calibration can mitigate algorithmic arbitrariness. Our empirical analysis reveals that minority class observations bear a disproportionate multiplicity burden, as confirmed by significant disparities in predictive multiplicity and prediction confidence. Furthermore, our empirical comparisons indicate that applying post-hoc calibration methods - specifically Platt Scaling, Isotonic Regression, and Temperature Scaling - is associated with lower obscurity across the Rashomon set. Among the tested techniques, Platt Scaling and Isotonic Regression provide the most robust reduction in predictive multiplicity. These findings suggest that calibration can function as a consensus-enforcing layer and may support procedural fairness by mitigating predictive multiplicity.

</details>


### [78] [Exploiting Expertise of Non-Expert and Diverse Agents in Social Bandit Learning: A Free Energy Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11757)
*Erfan Mirzaei,Seyed Pooya Shariatpanahi,Alireza Tavakoli,Reshad Hosseini,Majid Nili Ahmadabadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于自由能量的社会多臂赌博机学习算法，使智能体能够通过观察其他智能体的行为（无需知道其奖励）来提升学习性能，同时保持对数遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习算法主要关注个体学习，未能充分利用人类和动物普遍具备的社会学习能力。社会学习能够整合个体经验和观察他人行为，为改善学习效果提供了机会。

Method: 提出了一种基于自由能量的社会多臂赌博机学习算法，在策略空间上进行操作。社会智能体无需依赖任何先知或社会规范，通过评估其他智能体的专业水平，将自身在环境中的直接经验与他人的估计策略进行整合。

Result: 理论证明了算法收敛到最优策略。实证评估验证了该方法在各种场景下优于其他替代方法。算法能够策略性地识别相关智能体（即使在存在随机或次优智能体的情况下），并巧妙利用其行为信息。在包含相关但非专家智能体的情况下，该方法显著提升了个体学习性能，而大多数相关方法在此情况下失败。重要的是，算法保持了对数遗憾。

Conclusion: 提出的基于自由能量的社会学习算法能够有效利用社会观察提升个体学习性能，在多种社会配置下表现出色，同时保持理论上的最优收敛性和对数遗憾保证。

Abstract: Personalized AI-based services involve a population of individual reinforcement learning agents. However, most reinforcement learning algorithms focus on harnessing individual learning and fail to leverage the social learning capabilities commonly exhibited by humans and animals. Social learning integrates individual experience with observing others' behavior, presenting opportunities for improved learning outcomes. In this study, we focus on a social bandit learning scenario where a social agent observes other agents' actions without knowledge of their rewards. The agents independently pursue their own policy without explicit motivation to teach each other. We propose a free energy-based social bandit learning algorithm over the policy space, where the social agent evaluates others' expertise levels without resorting to any oracle or social norms. Accordingly, the social agent integrates its direct experiences in the environment and others' estimated policies. The theoretical convergence of our algorithm to the optimal policy is proven. Empirical evaluations validate the superiority of our social learning method over alternative approaches in various scenarios. Our algorithm strategically identifies the relevant agents, even in the presence of random or suboptimal agents, and skillfully exploits their behavioral information. In addition to societies including expert agents, in the presence of relevant but non-expert agents, our algorithm significantly enhances individual learning performance, where most related methods fail. Importantly, it also maintains logarithmic regret.

</details>


### [79] [A Further Efficient Algorithm with Best-of-Both-Worlds Guarantees for $m$-Set Semi-Bandit Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11764)
*Botao Chen,Jongyeong Lee,Chansoo Kim,Junya Honda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FTPL在m-set半赌博问题中达到最优遗憾界，兼具对抗和随机环境的最优性，并通过条件几何重采样降低计算复杂度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FTPL作为对抗组合半赌博问题的高效算法候选，其最优性一直未知，而FTRL的最优性已在多种在线学习任务中得到证明。本文旨在研究FTPL在m-set半赌博问题中的最优性和计算复杂度。

Method: 将带几何重采样的FTPL分析扩展到m-set半赌博问题，使用特定参数的Fréchet和Pareto分布。提出条件几何重采样方法，将计算复杂度从O(d²)降低到O(md(log(d/m)+1))。

Result: 在对抗环境中，FTPL使用特定参数的Fréchet和Pareto分布实现了最优遗憾界O(√(mdT))。在随机环境中，FTPL实现了对数遗憾，证明了FTPL在m-set半赌博问题中的"两全其美"最优性。

Conclusion: FTPL在m-set半赌博问题中达到了最优遗憾性能，兼具对抗和随机环境的最优性，并通过条件几何重采样显著降低了计算复杂度，为实际应用提供了高效算法。

Abstract: This paper studies the optimality and complexity of Follow-the-Perturbed-Leader (FTPL) policy in $m$-set semi-bandit problems. FTPL has been studied extensively as a promising candidate of an efficient algorithm with favorable regret for adversarial combinatorial semi-bandits. Nevertheless, the optimality of FTPL has still been unknown unlike Follow-the-Regularized-Leader (FTRL) whose optimality has been proved for various tasks of online learning. In this paper, we extend the analysis of FTPL with geometric resampling (GR) to $m$-set semi-bandits, which is a special case of combinatorial semi-bandits, showing that FTPL with Fréchet and Pareto distributions with certain parameters achieves the best possible regret of $O(\sqrt{mdT})$ in adversarial setting. We also show that FTPL with Fréchet and Pareto distributions with a certain parameter achieves a logarithmic regret for stochastic setting, meaning the Best-of-Both-Worlds optimality of FTPL for $m$-set semi-bandit problems. Furthermore, we extend the conditional geometric resampling to $m$-set semi-bandits for efficient loss estimation in FTPL, reducing the computational complexity from $O(d^2)$ of the original geometric resampling to $O(md(\log(d/m)+1))$ without sacrificing the regret performance.

</details>


### [80] [Language Generation with Replay: A Learning-Theoretic View of Model Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11784)
*Giorgio Racca,Michal Valko,Amartya Sanyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文从学习理论角度研究模型崩溃问题，通过语言生成极限框架分析生成文本重新进入训练数据的影响，发现重放对最强生成概念无害但对较弱概念产生分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM训练数据需求增长，公开在线文本可能被耗尽，同时机器生成内容增加导致生成文本可能重新进入训练数据，引发模型崩溃风险。现有实践方法缺乏理论分析，需要从学习理论角度研究这一问题。

Method: 采用语言生成极限框架的理论视角，引入重放对手概念，将生成器过去输出作为示例流的一部分。通过理论分析研究重放对三种生成概念的影响：均匀生成、非均匀生成和极限生成。

Result: 重放对最强的均匀生成概念是良性的，但对较弱的非均匀生成和极限生成概念会产生理论分离。正面结果反映了实践中广泛使用的启发式方法（数据清洗、水印、输出过滤），而分离结果显示了这些方法可能失败的情况。

Conclusion: 模型崩溃问题需要从学习理论角度理解，重放对不同生成概念有不同影响。理论分析为实践方法提供了理论基础，同时揭示了现有启发式方法的局限性。

Abstract: As scaling laws push the training of frontier large language models (LLMs) toward ever-growing data requirements, training pipelines are approaching a regime where much of the publicly available online text may be consumed. At the same time, widespread LLM usage increases the volume of machine-generated content on the web; together, these trends raise the likelihood of generated text re-entering future training corpora, increasing the associated risk of performance degradation often called model collapse. In practice, model developers address this concern through data cleaning, watermarking, synthetic-data policies, or, in some cases, blissful ignorance. However, the problem of model collapse in generative models has not been examined from a learning-theoretic perspective: we study it through the theoretical lens of the language generation in the limit framework, introducing a replay adversary that augments the example stream with the generator's own past outputs. Our main contribution is a fine-grained learning-theoretic characterization of when replay fundamentally limits generation: while replay is benign for the strongest notion of uniform generation, it provably creates separations for the weaker notions of non-uniform generation and generation in the limit. Interestingly, our positive results mirror heuristics widely used in practice, such as data cleaning, watermarking, and output filtering, while our separations show when these ideas can fail.

</details>


### [81] [Disentangled Representation Learning through Unsupervised Symmetry Group Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11790)
*Dang-Nhu Barthélémy,Annabi Louis,Argentieri Sylvain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需先验知识即可自主发现动作空间群结构的方法，用于学习线性对称解耦表示


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于对称性的解耦表示学习方法需要强先验知识或对子群性质的限制性假设，限制了方法的通用性

Method: 通过智能体与环境无监督交互自主发现动作空间的群结构，提出两种算法：从交互数据中发现群分解的算法，以及学习线性对称解耦表示而不假设特定子群性质的算法

Result: 在三个具有不同群分解的环境中验证了方法有效性，性能优于现有LSBD方法

Conclusion: 该方法在最小假设下证明了真实对称群分解的可识别性，无需先验知识即可实现对称解耦表示学习

Abstract: Symmetry-based disentangled representation learning leverages the group structure of environment transformations to uncover the latent factors of variation. Prior approaches to symmetry-based disentanglement have required strong prior knowledge of the symmetry group's structure, or restrictive assumptions about the subgroup properties. In this work, we remove these constraints by proposing a method whereby an embodied agent autonomously discovers the group structure of its action space through unsupervised interaction with the environment. We prove the identifiability of the true symmetry group decomposition under minimal assumptions, and derive two algorithms: one for discovering the group decomposition from interaction data, and another for learning Linear Symmetry-Based Disentangled (LSBD) representations without assuming specific subgroup properties. Our method is validated on three environments exhibiting different group decompositions, where it outperforms existing LSBD approaches.

</details>


### [82] [On the Role of Reversible Instance Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11869)
*Gaspard Berthelier,Tahar Nabil,Etienne Le Naour,Richard Niamke,Samir Perlaza,Giovanni Neglia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视时间序列预测中的Reversible Instance Normalization (RevIN)，通过消融实验发现其多个组件冗余甚至有害，并提出改进RevIN鲁棒性和泛化能力的新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据归一化是深度学习模型的关键组件，但在时间序列预测中的作用尚未得到充分理解。本文识别了时间序列预测中归一化的三个核心挑战：时间输入分布偏移、空间输入分布偏移和条件输出分布偏移。

Method: 通过消融研究重新审视广泛使用的Reversible Instance Normalization (RevIN)，分析其各个组件的实际效果，识别冗余或有害的组件。

Result: 研究发现RevIN的多个组件是冗余的甚至对性能有负面影响，这为改进RevIN提供了实证基础。

Conclusion: 基于观察结果，提出了改进RevIN鲁棒性和泛化能力的新视角，为时间序列预测中的归一化方法提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Data normalization is a crucial component of deep learning models, yet its role in time series forecasting remains insufficiently understood. In this paper, we identify three central challenges for normalization in time series forecasting: temporal input distribution shift, spatial input distribution shift, and conditional output distribution shift. In this context, we revisit the widely used Reversible Instance Normalization (RevIN), by showing through ablation studies that several of its components are redundant or even detrimental. Based on these observations, we draw new perspectives to improve RevIN's robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [83] [FlexRec: Adapting LLM-based Recommenders for Flexible Needs via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11901)
*Yijun Pan,Weikang Qiu,Qiyao Ma,Mingxuan Ju,Tong Zhao,Neil Shah,Rex Ying*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlexRec：基于强化学习的后训练框架，通过因果基础的项目级奖励和不确定性感知缩放，解决推荐系统中序列级奖励稀疏和噪声问题，显著提升多场景推荐性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐系统需要适应动态、需求特定的目标，但传统推荐器通常针对单一静态目标优化，难以按需重新配置行为。大型语言模型通过强化学习后训练展现出强大的指令跟随和推理能力，为将其与复杂推荐目标对齐提供了原则性途径。然而，在固定候选集自回归排序中应用RL面临两个关键障碍：序列级奖励导致粗粒度信用分配，无法提供细粒度训练信号；交互反馈稀疏且噪声大，导致更新低效不稳定。

Method: 提出FlexRec后训练强化学习框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于剩余候选池内反事实交换的因果基础项目级奖励，提供细粒度训练信号；(2) 批评者引导的不确定性感知缩放，显式建模奖励不确定性，降低低置信度奖励的权重，在稀疏监督下稳定学习。该框架针对闭集自回归排序场景，其中LLM基于用户上下文和明确需求指令生成固定候选集的排列。

Result: 在多样推荐场景和目标下，FlexRec取得显著提升：在需求特定排序中，NDCG@5提升高达59%，Recall@5提升高达109.4%；在泛化设置下，Recall@5进一步提升高达24.1%。性能超越强大的传统推荐器和基于LLM的基线方法。

Conclusion: FlexRec通过因果基础的项目级奖励和不确定性感知缩放，有效解决了推荐系统中强化学习面临的信用分配稀疏和噪声问题，为LLM在动态、需求特定推荐场景中的对齐提供了有效框架，显著提升了推荐性能。

Abstract: Modern recommender systems must adapt to dynamic, need-specific objectives for diverse recommendation scenarios, yet most traditional recommenders are optimized for a single static target and struggle to reconfigure behavior on demand. Recent advances in reinforcement-learning-based post-training have unlocked strong instruction-following and reasoning capabilities in LLMs, suggesting a principled route for aligning them to complex recommendation goals. Motivated by this, we study closed-set autoregressive ranking, where an LLM generates a permutation over a fixed candidate set conditioned on user context and an explicit need instruction. However, applying RL to this setting faces two key obstacles: (i) sequence-level rewards yield coarse credit assignment that fails to provide fine-grained training signals, and (ii) interaction feedback is sparse and noisy, which together lead to inefficient and unstable updates. We propose FlexRec, a post-training RL framework that addresses both issues with (1) a causally grounded item-level reward based on counterfactual swaps within the remaining candidate pool, and (2) critic-guided, uncertainty-aware scaling that explicitly models reward uncertainty and down-weights low-confidence rewards to stabilize learning under sparse supervision. Across diverse recommendation scenarios and objectives, FlexRec achieves substantial gains: it improves NDCG@5 by up to \textbf{59\%} and Recall@5 by up to \textbf{109.4\%} in need-specific ranking, and further achieves up to \textbf{24.1\%} Recall@5 improvement under generalization settings, outperforming strong traditional recommenders and LLM-based baselines.

</details>


### [84] [Causal Representation Learning with Optimal Compression under Complex Treatments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11907)
*Wanting Liang,Haoang Chi,Zhiheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多治疗场景下个体治疗效果估计的新方法，解决平衡权重超参数选择困境和计算可扩展性的维度诅咒问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多治疗场景下的个体治疗效果估计面临两个关键挑战：平衡权重的超参数选择困境和计算可扩展性的维度诅咒问题。现有方法需要昂贵的启发式调参，且随着治疗空间扩展计算复杂度急剧增加。

Method: 1) 推导多治疗泛化边界并提出理论估计器确定最优平衡权重α，消除启发式调参；2) 研究三种平衡策略：Pairwise、One-vs-All和Treatment Aggregation；3) 扩展为生成式架构Multi-Treatment CausalEGM，保持治疗流形的Wasserstein测地线结构

Result: 在低维设置下OVA策略精度最优，但提出的Treatment Aggregation策略在治疗空间扩展时同时保证精度和O(1)可扩展性。在半合成和图像数据集上的实验表明，该方法在估计精度和效率上显著优于传统模型，尤其在大规模干预场景中

Conclusion: 通过理论推导最优平衡权重和提出可扩展的平衡策略，解决了多治疗ITE估计的关键挑战。Treatment Aggregation策略在保证精度的同时实现了计算可扩展性，为大规模干预场景提供了有效的解决方案

Abstract: Estimating Individual Treatment Effects (ITE) in multi-treatment scenarios faces two critical challenges: the Hyperparameter Selection Dilemma for balancing weights and the Curse of Dimensionality in computational scalability. This paper derives a novel multi-treatment generalization bound and proposes a theoretical estimator for the optimal balancing weight $α$, eliminating expensive heuristic tuning. We investigate three balancing strategies: Pairwise, One-vs-All (OVA), and Treatment Aggregation. While OVA achieves superior precision in low-dimensional settings, our proposed Treatment Aggregation ensures both accuracy and O(1) scalability as the treatment space expands. Furthermore, we extend our framework to a generative architecture, Multi-Treatment CausalEGM, which preserves the Wasserstein geodesic structure of the treatment manifold. Experiments on semi-synthetic and image datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms traditional models in estimation accuracy and efficiency, particularly in large-scale intervention scenarios.

</details>


### [85] [Chem4DLLM: 4D Multimodal LLMs for Chemical Dynamics Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11924)
*Xinyu Li,Zhen Zhang,Qi Chen,Anton van den Hengel,Lina Yao,Javen Qinfeng Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出化学动力学理解任务ChemDU，将4D分子轨迹转化为自然语言解释，构建首个数据集Chem4DBench，并开发统一模型Chem4DLLM


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有化学理解任务主要依赖静态分子表示，无法建模化学反应的动态现象（如键断裂、构象变化），这些动态过程对理解化学反应机制至关重要

Method: 1. 提出ChemDU任务：将4D分子轨迹转化为可解释的自然语言描述；2. 构建Chem4DBench数据集：包含4D分子轨迹与专家撰写的解释；3. 开发Chem4DLLM模型：结合等变图编码器和预训练大语言模型，显式捕捉分子几何结构和旋转动力学

Result: 建立了首个化学动力学理解基准，包含数据集和统一模型框架，为动态化学理解和多模态科学推理研究提供了基础

Conclusion: ChemDU任务、Chem4DBench数据集和Chem4DLLM模型将推动动态化学理解和多模态科学推理的进一步研究，填补了静态分子表示在建模动态化学现象方面的不足

Abstract: Existing chemical understanding tasks primarily rely on static molecular representations, limiting their ability to model inherently dynamic phenomena such as bond breaking or conformational changes, which are essential for a chemist to understand chemical reactions. To address this gap, we introduce Chemical Dynamics Understanding (ChemDU), a new task that translates 4D molecular trajectories into interpretable natural-language explanations. ChemDU focuses on fundamental dynamic scenarios, including gas-phase and catalytic reactions, and requires models to reason about key events along molecular trajectories, such as bond formation and dissociation, and to generate coherent, mechanistically grounded narratives. To benchmark this capability, we construct Chem4DBench, the first dataset pairing 4D molecular trajectories with expert-authored explanations across these settings. We further propose Chem4DLLM, a unified model that integrates an equivariant graph encoder with a pretrained large language model to explicitly capture molecular geometry and rotational dynamics. We hope that ChemDU, together with Chem4DBench and Chem4DLLM, will stimulate further research in dynamic chemical understanding and multimodal scientific reasoning.

</details>


### [86] [MobileKernelBench: Can LLMs Write Efficient Kernels for Mobile Devices?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11935)
*Xingze Zou,Jing Wang,Yuhua Zheng,Xueyi Chen,Haolei Bai,Lingcheng Kong,Syed A. R. Abu-Bakar,Zhaode Wang,Chengfei Lv,Haoji Hu,Huan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLMs在移动设备内核生成方面表现不佳，编译失败率高且性能提升有限；提出的MoKA多智能体系统显著改善了编译成功率和性能优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLMs在代码生成方面表现出色，但针对移动设备的高效内核生成能力尚未得到充分探索。移动设备具有独特的工程复杂性和数据稀缺性挑战，需要专门的研究框架来评估LLMs在这一领域的实际能力。

Method: 1. 引入MobileKernelBench评估框架，包含注重算子多样性和跨框架互操作性的基准测试，以及用于设备验证的自动化流水线。2. 在MNN CPU后端进行广泛评估。3. 提出Mobile Kernel Agent (MoKA)多智能体系统，采用仓库感知推理和计划-执行范式来克服LLMs的局限性。

Result: 1. 当前LLMs在移动内核生成方面表现不佳：标准模型和微调变体的编译失败率超过54%，性能提升可忽略不计，主要由于幻觉问题和缺乏领域特定知识。2. MoKA系统显著改善性能：编译成功率提升至93.7%，27.4%的生成内核能够提供可测量的加速效果，优于原生库。

Conclusion: LLMs在移动设备内核生成方面面临工程复杂性和数据稀缺的挑战，但通过专门设计的MoKA多智能体系统，可以显著提升编译成功率和性能优化能力，为移动设备的高效内核生成提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in code generation, yet their potential for generating kernels specifically for mobile de- vices remains largely unexplored. In this work, we extend the scope of automated kernel generation to the mobile domain to investigate the central question: Can LLMs write efficient kernels for mobile devices? To enable systematic investigation, we introduce MobileKernelBench, a comprehensive evaluation framework comprising a benchmark prioritizing operator diversity and cross-framework interoperability, coupled with an automated pipeline that bridges the host-device gap for on-device verification. Leveraging this framework, we conduct extensive evaluation on the CPU backend of Mobile Neural Network (MNN), revealing that current LLMs struggle with the engineering complexity and data scarcity inher-ent to mobile frameworks; standard models and even fine-tuned variants exhibit high compilation failure rates (over 54%) and negligible performance gains due to hallucinations and a lack of domain-specific grounding. To overcome these limitations, we propose the Mobile K ernel A gent (MoKA), a multi-agent system equipped with repository-aware reasoning and a plan-and-execute paradigm.Validated on MobileKernelBench, MoKA achieves state-of-the-art performance, boosting compilation success to 93.7% and enabling 27.4% of generated kernelsto deliver measurable speedups over native libraries.

</details>


### [87] [Exhaustive Circuit Mapping of a Single-Cell Foundation Model Reveals Massive Redundancy, Heavy-Tailed Hub Architecture, and Layer-Dependent Differentiation Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11940)
*Ihor Kendiukhov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过三种实验方法改进了生物基础模型的机制可解释性：1）穷举电路追踪揭示特征连接性的重尾分布和注释偏差；2）高阶组合消融显示模型架构在所有测试阶数上都是次可加的；3）轨迹引导特征操控建立了层位置与分化方向性之间的因果联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生物基础模型的机制可解释性方法（选择性特征采样、成对交互测试、观测轨迹分析）存在系统性偏差，需要更全面、因果性的分析方法来理解模型内部工作机制。

Method: 采用三种实验方法：1）对Geneformer模型第5层所有4065个活跃稀疏自编码器特征进行穷举电路追踪；2）对8个特征三元组进行三阶组合消融分析；3）通过轨迹引导的特征操控分析不同层特征对细胞分化方向的影响。

Result: 1）穷举追踪发现1,393,850个显著下游连接，比选择性采样扩大27倍，揭示1.8%的特征具有不成比例连接性，40%的top20枢纽缺乏生物注释；2）三阶组合消融显示冗余度随交互阶数单调加深（三阶比0.59 vs 成对比0.74），无协同效应；3）晚期层（L17）特征始终推动细胞状态向成熟分化，早期和中期层（L0和L11）特征主要推动远离成熟。

Conclusion: 该研究从相关性证据转向因果证据，证明模型层位置对细胞状态控制具有依赖性，揭示了先前选择性分析中的系统性注释偏差，并确认模型架构在所有测试阶数上都是次可加的。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability of biological foundation models has relied on selective feature sampling, pairwise interaction testing, and observational trajectory analysis. Each of these can introduce systematic bias. Here we present three experiments that address these limitations through exhaustive circuit tracing, higher order combinatorial ablation, and causal trajectory steering in Geneformer, a transformer based single cell foundation model. First, exhaustive tracing of all 4065 active sparse autoencoder features at layer 5 yields 1393850 significant downstream edges, a 27 fold expansion over selective sampling. This reveals a heavy tailed hub distribution in which 1.8 percent of features account for disproportionate connectivity and 40 percent of the top 20 hubs lack biological annotation. These results indicate systematic annotation bias in prior selective analyses. Second, three way combinatorial ablation across 8 feature triplets shows that redundancy deepens monotonically with interaction order, with a three way ratio of 0.59 versus a pairwise ratio of 0.74, and with zero synergy. This confirms that the model architecture is subadditive at all tested orders. Third, trajectory guided feature steering establishes a causal link between layer position and differentiation directionality. Late layer features at L17 consistently push cell states toward maturity, with fraction positive equal to 1.0. Early and mid layer features at L0 and L11 mostly push away from maturity, with fraction positive ranging from 0.00 to 0.58. Together these results move from correlation toward causal evidence for layer dependent control of cell state.

</details>


### [88] [Causal Matrix Completion under Multiple Treatments via Mixed Synthetic Nearest Neighbors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11942)
*Minrui Luo,Zhiheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MSNN扩展了SNN方法，通过跨治疗水平整合信息来解决多治疗或复杂治疗场景下数据不足的问题，保持理论保证的同时扩大有效样本量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN方法在MNAR下的因果矩阵补全中表现良好，但要求每个治疗水平都有足够数据，这在多治疗或复杂治疗场景中往往不成立，导致估计效果受限。

Method: 提出Mixed Synthetic Nearest Neighbors (MSNN)，一种新的逐项因果识别估计器，通过跨治疗水平整合信息来利用局部低秩结构，扩大可用于估计的有效样本量。

Result: MSNN保持了SNN的有限样本误差界和渐近正态性保证，同时在合成和真实数据集上表现出更好的性能，特别是在数据稀缺的治疗水平下效果显著。

Conclusion: MSNN是SNN的有效扩展，通过跨治疗水平信息整合解决了数据稀缺问题，在多治疗或复杂治疗场景下具有更好的实用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Synthetic Nearest Neighbors (SNN) provides a principled solution to causal matrix completion under missing-not-at-random (MNAR) by exploiting local low-rank structure through fully observed anchor submatrices. However, its effectiveness critically relies on sufficient data availability within each treatment level, a condition that often fails in settings with multiple or complex treatments. In this work, we propose Mixed Synthetic Nearest Neighbors (MSNN), a new entry-wise causal identification estimator that integrates information across treatment levels. We show that MSNN retains the finite-sample error bounds and asymptotic normality guarantees of SNN, while enlarging the effective sample size available for estimation. Empirical results on synthetic and real-world datasets illustrate the efficacy of the proposed approach, especially under data-scarce treatment levels.

</details>


### [89] [Geometry-Aware Probabilistic Circuits via Voronoi Tessellations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11946)
*Sahil Sidheekh,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出将Voronoi剖分融入概率电路以捕获数据几何结构，解决了由此带来的可处理性问题，并开发了近似推理框架和结构条件来保证推理的精确性与可处理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率电路使用数据无关的混合权重，限制了其捕捉数据流形局部几何结构的能力。需要一种方法来将几何结构直接融入概率电路的求和节点中。

Method: 提出使用Voronoi剖分将几何结构融入概率电路，开发了两种解决方案：1) 提供推理保证上下界的近似推理框架；2) 恢复精确可处理推理的VT结构条件；并引入了可微VT松弛以实现基于梯度的学习。

Result: 在标准密度估计任务上对提出的方法进行了实证验证，展示了将几何结构融入概率电路的有效性。

Conclusion: Voronoi剖分是向概率电路注入几何结构的自然方式，通过适当的近似框架和结构条件，可以在保持推理可处理性的同时增强模型对数据几何结构的捕捉能力。

Abstract: Probabilistic circuits (PCs) enable exact and tractable inference but employ data independent mixture weights that limit their ability to capture local geometry of the data manifold. We propose Voronoi tessellations (VT) as a natural way to incorporate geometric structure directly into the sum nodes of a PC. However, naïvely introducing such structure breaks tractability. We formalize this incompatibility and develop two complementary solutions: (1) an approximate inference framework that provides guaranteed lower and upper bounds for inference, and (2) a structural condition for VT under which exact tractable inference is recovered. Finally, we introduce a differentiable relaxation for VT that enables gradient-based learning and empirically validate the resulting approach on standard density estimation tasks.

</details>


### [90] [Statistical and structural identifiability in representation learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11970)
*Walter Nelson,Marco Fumero,Theofanis Karaletsos,Francesco Locatello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将表示学习的稳定性形式化为统计可识别性和结构可识别性两个概念，提出ε-近可识别性定义，证明非线性解码器模型的统计近可识别性，并展示ICA后处理可实现解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表示学习模型内部表示表现出惊人的稳定性，但现有研究将其视为单一属性。本文旨在将这种稳定性形式化为两个不同概念：统计可识别性（跨运行的一致性）和结构可识别性（与未观测真实情况的对应），并建立更实用的近可识别性理论。

Method: 1) 提出统计和结构ε-近可识别性的模型无关定义；2) 证明非线性解码器模型的统计近可识别性定理；3) 使用独立成分分析(ICA)解决剩余的线性模糊性；4) 在合成基准和细胞显微镜基础模型MAE上进行实证验证。

Result: 1) 理论证明非线性解码器模型（包括MAE和监督学习器）的中间表示具有统计近可识别性；2) ICA后处理在合成基准上实现最先进的解耦性能；3) 在细胞显微镜MAE模型中，成功分离生物变异和技术批次效应，显著提升下游泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过区分统计和结构可识别性并引入近可识别性概念，本文为表示学习稳定性提供了更精确的理论框架。ICA后处理为解耦提供了一种简单实用的方法，在合成数据和真实世界基础模型中都取得了显著效果，为理解表示学习模型的内部工作机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: Representation learning models exhibit a surprising stability in their internal representations. Whereas most prior work treats this stability as a single property, we formalize it as two distinct concepts: statistical identifiability (consistency of representations across runs) and structural identifiability (alignment of representations with some unobserved ground truth). Recognizing that perfect pointwise identifiability is generally unrealistic for modern representation learning models, we propose new model-agnostic definitions of statistical and structural near-identifiability of representations up to some error tolerance $ε$. Leveraging these definitions, we prove a statistical $ε$-near-identifiability result for the representations of models with nonlinear decoders, generalizing existing identifiability theory beyond last-layer representations in e.g. generative pre-trained transformers (GPTs) to near-identifiability of the intermediate representations of a broad class of models including (masked) autoencoders (MAEs) and supervised learners. Although these weaker assumptions confer weaker identifiability, we show that independent components analysis (ICA) can resolve much of the remaining linear ambiguity for this class of models, and validate and measure our near-identifiability claims empirically. With additional assumptions on the data-generating process, statistical identifiability extends to structural identifiability, yielding a simple and practical recipe for disentanglement: ICA post-processing of latent representations. On synthetic benchmarks, this approach achieves state-of-the-art disentanglement using a vanilla autoencoder. With a foundation model-scale MAE for cell microscopy, it disentangles biological variation from technical batch effects, substantially improving downstream generalization.

</details>


### [91] [On-Average Stability of Multipass Preconditioned SGD and Effective Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11989)
*Simon Vary,Tyler Farghly,Ilja Kuzborskij,Patrick Rebeschini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究多轮预条件随机梯度下降（PSGD）中总体风险曲率、噪声几何和预条件对泛化能力的权衡关系，提出新的平均算法稳定性分析框架，推导出依赖有效维度的超额风险界


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际优化启发式方法隐含地处理总体风险曲率、梯度噪声几何和预条件之间的权衡关系，当这些几何结构不匹配时，激进的选择可能导致次优的统计行为，需要理论分析这种权衡对泛化的影响

Method: 采用平均算法稳定性方法，开发新的多轮SGD稳定性分析框架处理数据重用引起的相关性，推导依赖有效维度的超额风险上界，并补充匹配的实例依赖下界

Result: 建立了PSGD泛化能力与有效维度之间的理论联系，表明不当选择的预条件器可能导致优化和泛化中次优的有效维度依赖关系

Conclusion: 总体风险曲率、梯度噪声几何和预条件之间的权衡对PSGD的泛化能力至关重要，不当的预条件选择会恶化有效维度依赖，新的稳定性分析框架为多轮SGD提供了理论保证

Abstract: We study trade-offs between the population risk curvature, geometry of the noise, and preconditioning on the generalisation ability of the multipass Preconditioned Stochastic Gradient Descent (PSGD). Many practical optimisation heuristics implicitly navigate this trade-off in different ways -- for instance, some aim to whiten gradient noise, while others aim to align updates with expected loss curvature. When the geometry of the population risk curvature and the geometry of the gradient noise do not match, an aggressive choice that improves one aspect can amplify instability along the other, leading to suboptimal statistical behavior. In this paper we employ on-average algorithmic stability to connect generalisation of PSGD to the effective dimension that depends on these sources of curvature. While existing techniques for on-average stability of SGD are limited to a single pass, as first contribution we develop a new on-average stability analysis for multipass SGD that handles the correlations induced by data reuse. This allows us to derive excess risk bounds that depend on the effective dimension. In particular, we show that an improperly chosen preconditioner can yield suboptimal effective dimension dependence in both optimisation and generalisation. Finally, we complement our upper bounds with matching, instance-dependent lower bounds.

</details>


### [92] [Slow-Fast Inference: Training-Free Inference Acceleration via Within-Sentence Support Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12038)
*Xingyu Xie,Zhaochen Yu,Yue Liao,Tao Wang,Kim-Chuan Toh,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SFI是一种无需训练的推理框架，通过将解码过程分解为频繁的低成本快速步骤和偶尔的密集注意力慢速步骤，实现长上下文自回归解码的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文自回归解码成本高昂，因为每个解码步骤都需要重复处理不断增长的历史。研究发现，在句子内或语义连贯的短跨度内，主导注意力支持通常保持相对稳定。

Method: 提出Slow-Fast Inference (SFI)框架：快速步骤重用紧凑的稀疏内存进行高效解码；慢速步骤在语义边界附近触发，重新审视更广泛的上下文并使用选择器刷新后续快速步骤的内存。

Result: 在评估的上下文长度范围内，SFI提供约1.6倍到14.4倍更高的解码吞吐量，同时在长上下文和长链式思维设置中保持与完整KV基线相当的质量。

Conclusion: SFI是一种无需训练、可直接应用于现有检查点的实用方法，为当代自回归推理模型在长上下文、长视野和智能体工作负载中降低推理成本提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Long-context autoregressive decoding remains expensive because each decoding step must repeatedly process a growing history. We observe a consistent pattern during decoding: within a sentence, and more generally within a short semantically coherent span, the dominant attention support often remains largely stable. Motivated by this observation, we propose Slow-Fast Inference (SFI), a training-free decoding framework that decouples generation into frequent low-cost fast steps and occasional dense-attention slow steps. Fast steps reuse a compact sparse memory for efficient decoding. Slow steps are triggered near semantic boundaries. At slow steps, the model revisits the broader context and uses the Selector to refresh the selected memory for subsequent fast steps. Across the evaluated context lengths, SFI delivers approximately $1.6\times$--$14.4\times$ higher decoding throughput while generally maintaining quality on par with the full-KV baseline across long-context and long-CoT settings. Because SFI is training-free and applies directly to existing checkpoints, it offers a practical path to reducing inference cost for contemporary autoregressive reasoning models in long-context, long-horizon, and agentic workloads.

</details>


### [93] [Cross-Domain Policy Optimization via Bellman Consistency and Hybrid Critics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12087)
*Ming-Hong Chen,Kuan-Chen Pan,You-De Huang,Xi Liu,Ping-Chun Hsieh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QAvatar：通过跨域贝尔曼一致性和混合批评器解决跨域强化学习中的状态/动作空间差异和负迁移问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨域强化学习面临两个核心挑战：1) 源域和目标域可能具有不同的状态空间或动作空间，需要复杂的域间映射；2) 源域模型的迁移性难以先验确定，容易产生负迁移效应

Method: 提出QAvatar方法：1) 引入跨域贝尔曼一致性概念来衡量源域模型的迁移性；2) 设计混合批评器，将源域和目标域的Q函数通过自适应无超参数权重函数结合

Result: QAvatar在各种RL基准任务（包括运动控制和机器人手臂操作）中实现了有利的迁移性能，证明了其有效利用源域Q函数进行知识迁移的能力

Conclusion: 通过跨域贝尔曼一致性和混合批评器的联合设计，QAvatar能够可靠地解决跨域强化学习中的空间差异和负迁移问题，实现高效的知识迁移

Abstract: Cross-domain reinforcement learning (CDRL) is meant to improve the data efficiency of RL by leveraging the data samples collected from a source domain to facilitate the learning in a similar target domain. Despite its potential, cross-domain transfer in RL is known to have two fundamental and intertwined challenges: (i) The source and target domains can have distinct state space or action space, and this makes direct transfer infeasible and thereby requires more sophisticated inter-domain mappings; (ii) The transferability of a source-domain model in RL is not easily identifiable a priori, and hence CDRL can be prone to negative effect during transfer. In this paper, we propose to jointly tackle these two challenges through the lens of \textit{cross-domain Bellman consistency} and \textit{hybrid critic}. Specifically, we first introduce the notion of cross-domain Bellman consistency as a way to measure transferability of a source-domain model. Then, we propose $Q$Avatar, which combines the Q functions from both the source and target domains with an adaptive hyperparameter-free weight function. Through this design, we characterize the convergence behavior of $Q$Avatar and show that $Q$Avatar achieves reliable transfer in the sense that it effectively leverages a source-domain Q function for knowledge transfer to the target domain. Through experiments, we demonstrate that $Q$Avatar achieves favorable transferability across various RL benchmark tasks, including locomotion and robot arm manipulation. Our code is available at https://rl-bandits-lab.github.io/Cross-Domain-RL/.

</details>


### [94] [Resource-Efficient Iterative LLM-Based NAS with Feedback Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12091)
*Xiaojie Gu,Dmitry Ignatov,Radu Timofte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于大语言模型的闭环神经架构搜索方法，在单张消费级GPU上实现无需LLM微调的迭代架构生成、评估和优化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经架构搜索方法需要大量计算资源，而现有LLM优化器往往丢弃失败轨迹，无法有效利用历史反馈信息

Method: 采用基于马尔可夫链启发的历史反馈记忆机制（滑动窗口K=5），使用结构化诊断三元组记录问题、修改和结果；采用双LLM专业化：代码生成器负责生成PyTorch架构，提示改进器处理诊断推理；在有限VRAM约束下进行搜索

Result: 在CIFAR-10上，DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B从28.2%提升到69.2%，Qwen2.5-7B从50.0%提升到71.5%，GLM-5从43.2%提升到62.0%；2000次迭代搜索在单张RTX 4090上约需18小时

Conclusion: 建立了一种低预算、可复现、硬件感知的LLM驱动NAS范式，无需云基础设施，适合边缘部署的紧凑高效模型设计

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) automates network design, but conventional methods demand substantial computational resources. We propose a closed-loop pipeline leveraging large language models (LLMs) to iteratively generate, evaluate, and refine convolutional neural network architectures for image classification on a single consumer-grade GPU without LLM fine-tuning. Central to our approach is a historical feedback memory inspired by Markov chains: a sliding window of $K{=}5$ recent improvement attempts keeps context size constant while providing sufficient signal for iterative learning. Unlike prior LLM optimizers that discard failure trajectories, each history entry is a structured diagnostic triple -- recording the identified problem, suggested modification, and resulting outcome -- treating code execution failures as first-class learning signals. A dual-LLM specialization reduces per-call cognitive load: a Code Generator produces executable PyTorch architectures while a Prompt Improver handles diagnostic reasoning. Since both the LLM and architecture training share limited VRAM, the search implicitly favors compact, hardware-efficient models suited to edge deployment. We evaluate three frozen instruction-tuned LLMs (${\leq}7$B parameters) across up to 2000 iterations in an unconstrained open code space, using one-epoch proxy accuracy on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and ImageNette as a fast ranking signal. On CIFAR-10, DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B improves from 28.2% to 69.2%, Qwen2.5-7B from 50.0% to 71.5%, and GLM-5 from 43.2% to 62.0%. A full 2000-iteration search completes in ${\approx}18$ GPU hours on a single RTX~4090, establishing a low-budget, reproducible, and hardware-aware paradigm for LLM-driven NAS without cloud infrastructure.

</details>


### [95] [Taming the Adversary: Stable Minimax Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient via Fractional Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12110)
*Taeho Lee,Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MMDDPG框架，通过极小极大优化学习连续控制任务中的抗干扰策略，在用户策略和对抗性干扰策略之间建立平衡，提高RL智能体在外部扰动和模型不确定性下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在控制决策任务中取得了显著成功，但智能体在面对意外外部干扰和模型不确定性时经常表现出不稳定或性能下降。确保在这种条件下的可靠性能仍然是一个关键挑战。

Method: 提出极小极大深度确定性策略梯度（MMDDPG）框架，将训练过程表述为用户策略和对抗性干扰策略之间的极小极大优化问题。用户学习最小化目标函数的鲁棒策略，而对抗者生成最大化目标函数的干扰。引入分数目标函数来平衡任务性能和干扰幅度，防止过度激进的干扰并促进鲁棒学习。

Result: 在MuJoCo环境中的实验评估表明，提出的MMDDPG在对抗外部力扰动和模型参数变化方面实现了显著改进的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MMDDPG框架通过极小极大优化有效提高了强化学习智能体在存在外部干扰和模型不确定性环境中的鲁棒性能，为解决RL在实际部署中的稳定性问题提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has achieved remarkable success in a wide range of control and decision-making tasks. However, RL agents often exhibit unstable or degraded performance when deployed in environments subject to unexpected external disturbances and model uncertainties. Consequently, ensuring reliable performance under such conditions remains a critical challenge. In this paper, we propose minimax deep deterministic policy gradient (MMDDPG), a framework for learning disturbance-resilient policies in continuous control tasks. The training process is formulated as a minimax optimization problem between a user policy and an adversarial disturbance policy. In this problem, the user learns a robust policy that minimizes the objective function, while the adversary generates disturbances that maximize it. To stabilize this interaction, we introduce a fractional objective that balances task performance and disturbance magnitude. This objective prevents excessively aggressive disturbances and promotes robust learning. Experimental evaluations in MuJoCo environments demonstrate that the proposed MMDDPG achieves significantly improved robustness against both external force perturbations and model parameter variations.

</details>


### [96] [Cornserve: A Distributed Serving System for Any-to-Any Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12118)
*Jae-Won Chung,Jeff J. Ma,Jisang Ahn,Yizhuo Liang,Akshay Jajoo,Myungjin Lee,Mosharaf Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Cornserve是一个用于Any-to-Any多模态模型的分布式服务系统，通过组件解耦和独立扩展实现高效服务，相比现有方案提供最高3.81倍吞吐量和5.79倍更低尾部延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Any-to-Any多模态模型（接受文本、图像、视频、音频等组合输入并生成相应输出）的服务面临挑战：不同请求的输入输出模态组合不同，导致计算图路径不同，且模型各组件具有不同的扩展特性，需要灵活的分布式服务解决方案。

Method: Cornserve提供灵活的任务抽象来表达Any-to-Any模型计算图，支持组件解耦和独立扩展。分布式运行时通过高效的记录-重放执行模型调度计算到数据平面，跟踪数据依赖，并直接在生产者与消费者之间转发张量数据。系统基于Kubernetes构建，使用约23K行Python代码实现。

Result: Cornserve支持多样化的Any-to-Any模型，相比现有方案提供最高3.81倍的吞吐量提升和5.79倍的尾部延迟降低。系统已开源，演示视频可在YouTube上获取。

Conclusion: Cornserve为Any-to-Any多模态模型提供了一个高效、灵活的分布式服务系统，通过组件解耦、独立扩展和高效的执行模型解决了多模态模型服务的核心挑战，显著提升了服务性能。

Abstract: Any-to-Any models are an emerging class of multimodal models that accept combinations of multimodal data (e.g., text, image, video, audio) as input and generate them as output. Serving these models are challenging; different requests with different input and output modalities traverse different paths through the model computation graph, and each component of the model have different scaling characteristics.
  We present Cornserve, a distributed serving system for generic Any-to-Any models. Cornserve provides a flexible task abstraction for expressing Any-to-Any model computation graphs, enabling component disaggregation and independent scaling. The distributed runtime dispatches compute to the data plane via an efficient record-and-replay execution model that keeps track of data dependencies, and forwards tensor data between components directly from the producer to the consumer. Built on Kubernetes with approximately 23K new lines of Python, Cornserve supports diverse Any-to-Any models and delivers up to 3.81$\times$ higher throughput and 5.79$\times$ lower tail latency. Cornserve is open-source, and the demo video is available on YouTube.

</details>


### [97] [IsoCompute Playbook: Optimally Scaling Sampling Compute for LLM RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12151)
*Zhoujun Cheng,Yutao Xie,Yuxiao Qu,Amrith Setlur,Shibo Hao,Varad Pimpalkhute,Tongtong Liang,Feng Yao,Zhengzhong Liu,Eric Xing,Virginia Smith,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,Zhiting Hu,Taylor Killian,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探索了大型语言模型强化学习后训练的计算最优分配策略，发现并行rollout数量随计算预算增加而可预测地增长并最终饱和，这一规律在不同难度问题上由不同机制驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然扩展定律指导了LLM预训练的计算分配，但针对大型语言模型强化学习后训练的类似指导原则仍然缺乏理解。研究者希望建立RL后训练的计算最优分配规则，为高效计算使用提供指导。

Method: 研究者将RL扩展问题构建为计算约束下的优化问题，考虑三个关键资源：每个问题的并行rollout数量、每批处理的问题数量、更新步骤数量。通过系统实验分析这些参数在不同计算预算下的最优配置。

Result: 研究发现：1）每个问题的并行rollout数量随计算预算增加而可预测地增长并最终饱和；2）这一趋势在不同难度问题上由不同机制驱动：简单问题上是解决方案锐化，困难问题上是覆盖扩展；3）增加并行rollout数量能减轻问题间的干扰，而每批处理的问题数量主要影响训练稳定性且可在较宽范围内选择。

Conclusion: 该研究将RL扩展定律重新构建为规定性分配规则，为计算高效的LLM RL后训练提供了实用指导。这些发现在不同基础模型和数据分布上得到验证，为RL后训练的计算资源分配提供了系统性框架。

Abstract: While scaling laws guide compute allocation for LLM pre-training, analogous prescriptions for reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of large language models (LLMs) remain poorly understood. We study the compute-optimal allocation of sampling compute for on-policy RL methods in LLMs, framing scaling as a compute-constrained optimization over three resources: parallel rollouts per problem, number of problems per batch, and number of update steps. We find that the compute-optimal number of parallel rollouts per problem increases predictably with compute budget and then saturates. This trend holds across both easy and hard problems, though driven by different mechanisms: solution sharpening on easy problems and coverage expansion on hard problems. We further show that increasing the number of parallel rollouts mitigates interference across problems, while the number of problems per batch primarily affects training stability and can be chosen within a broad range. Validated across base models and data distributions, our results recast RL scaling laws as prescriptive allocation rules and provide practical guidance for compute-efficient LLM RL post-training.

</details>


### [98] [A Quantitative Characterization of Forgetting in Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12163)
*Krishnakumar Balasubramanian,Shiva Prasad Kasiviswanathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在双模态混合模型框架下，从理论上分析了生成模型持续后训练中的遗忘现象，区分了质量遗忘和旧分量漂移两种形式，证明了不同KL散度目标对遗忘的不同影响，并分析了回放机制和近期方法的保留条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型的持续后训练被广泛使用，但对于遗忘何时发生、为何发生的原理性理解仍然有限。本文旨在建立理论框架来精确量化遗忘现象，理解不同训练目标、几何行为重叠、采样机制和过去行为可见性之间的相互作用。

Method: 基于Chen等人（2025）提出的双模态混合抽象（代表新旧任务），在等协方差高斯模态假设下，分析前向KL和后向KL目标对遗忘的影响。形式化两种遗忘形式：质量遗忘（旧混合权重坍缩为零）和旧分量漂移（已正确的旧分量在训练中偏移）。进一步分析回放机制与这些目标的交互作用，并通过相同理论视角分析三种近期提出的近策略后训练方法。

Result: 证明前向KL目标会驱动旧权重归零，而后向KL目标收敛到真实目标（避免质量遗忘），旧均值漂移受重叠门控的误分配概率控制，由Bhattacharyya系数决定，漂移随模态分离呈指数衰减。回放机制对前向KL需要修改训练分布，对后向KL则通过有界重要性权重防止旧模态饥饿。分析了SDFT、TTT-Discover和OAPL方法，推导出每种方法保留旧质量和展示重叠控制漂移的明确条件。

Conclusion: 遗忘现象可以基于散度方向、几何行为重叠、采样机制和训练中过去行为可见性之间的相互作用进行精确量化。后向KL目标在避免质量遗忘方面具有优势，而漂移受模态重叠控制。回放机制在不同目标下作用不同，近期方法在特定条件下能够保留旧知识。

Abstract: Continual post-training of generative models is widely used, yet a principled understanding of when and why forgetting occurs remains limited. We develop theoretical results under a two-mode mixture abstraction (representing old and new tasks), proposed by Chen et al. (2025) (arXiv:2510.18874), and formalize forgetting in two forms: (i) mass forgetting, where the old mixture weight collapses to zero, and (ii) old-component drift, where an already-correct old component shifts during training. For equal-covariance Gaussian modes, we prove that forward-KL objectives trained on data from the new distribution drive the old weight to zero, while reverse-KL objectives converge to the true target (thereby avoiding mass forgetting) and perturb the old mean only through overlap-gated misassignment probabilities controlled by the Bhattacharyya coefficient, yielding drift that decays exponentially with mode separation and a locally well-conditioned geometry with exponential convergence. We further quantify how replay interacts with these objectives. For forward-KL, replay must modify the training distribution to change the population optimum; for reverse-KL, replay leaves the population objective unchanged but prevents finite-batch old-mode starvation through bounded importance weighting. Finally, we analyze three recently proposed near-on-policy post-training methods, SDFT (arxiv:2601.19897), TTT-Discover (arxiv:2601.16175), and OAPL (arxiv:2602.19362), via the same lens and derive explicit conditions under which each retains old mass and exhibits overlap-controlled drift. Overall, our results show that forgetting can by precisely quantified based on the interaction between divergence direction, geometric behavioral overlap, sampling regime, and the visibility of past behavior during training.

</details>


### [99] [Neural Thickets: Diverse Task Experts Are Dense Around Pretrained Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12228)
*Yulu Gan,Phillip Isola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出将预训练结果视为参数向量分布而非单一向量，发现大模型中任务专家密度显著增加，并提出基于随机采样和多数投票的并行后训练方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将预训练参数向量视为后续适应的起点，本文认为预训练结果应被视为参数向量分布，其中已包含任务特定专家，大模型中这些专家密度显著增加

Method: 提出完全并行的后训练方法：随机采样N个参数扰动，选择表现最好的K个，通过多数投票集成预测结果

Result: 该方法在当代大规模模型中与PPO、GRPO、ES等标准后训练方法具有竞争力，尽管方法简单但效果显著

Conclusion: 预训练参数分布中已包含丰富任务专家，大模型中专家密度显著增加，基于随机采样和集成的简单方法可有效利用这一特性

Abstract: Pretraining produces a learned parameter vector that is typically treated as a starting point for further iterative adaptation. In this work, we instead view the outcome of pretraining as a distribution over parameter vectors, whose support already contains task-specific experts. We show that in small models such expert solutions occupy a negligible fraction of the volume of this distribution, making their discovery reliant on structured optimization methods such as gradient descent. In contrast, in large, well-pretrained models the density of task-experts increases dramatically, so that diverse, task-improving specialists populate a substantial fraction of the neighborhood around the pretrained weights. Motivated by this perspective, we explore a simple, fully parallel post-training method that samples $N$ parameter perturbations at random, selects the top $K$, and ensembles predictions via majority vote. Despite its simplicity, this approach is competitive with standard post-training methods such as PPO, GRPO, and ES for contemporary large-scale models.

</details>


### [100] [Temporal Straightening for Latent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12231)
*Ying Wang,Oumayma Bounou,Gaoyue Zhou,Randall Balestriero,Tim G. J. Rudner,Yann LeCun,Mengye Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出时间平直化方法，通过曲率正则化器改善世界模型中的潜在规划表示学习，使潜在空间中的欧氏距离更好地近似测地距离，提升梯度规划稳定性和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的视觉编码器虽然能产生强语义视觉特征，但这些特征并非为规划任务量身定制，可能包含与规划无关甚至有害的信息。受人类视觉处理中感知平直化假设的启发，需要改进潜在规划的表示学习。

Method: 引入时间平直化方法，使用曲率正则化器鼓励局部平直的潜在轨迹，联合学习编码器和预测器。通过减少曲率，使潜在空间中的欧氏距离更好地近似测地距离，并改善规划目标的调节性。

Result: 实验证明，时间平直化使基于梯度的规划更加稳定，在一系列目标达成任务中显著提高了成功率。

Conclusion: 时间平直化是一种有效的表示学习方法，通过曲率正则化改善潜在空间的几何结构，使欧氏距离更好地反映真实动态距离，从而提升潜在规划的性能和稳定性。

Abstract: Learning good representations is essential for latent planning with world models. While pretrained visual encoders produce strong semantic visual features, they are not tailored to planning and contain information irrelevant -- or even detrimental -- to planning. Inspired by the perceptual straightening hypothesis in human visual processing, we introduce temporal straightening to improve representation learning for latent planning. Using a curvature regularizer that encourages locally straightened latent trajectories, we jointly learn an encoder and a predictor. We show that reducing curvature this way makes the Euclidean distance in latent space a better proxy for the geodesic distance and improves the conditioning of the planning objective. We demonstrate empirically that temporal straightening makes gradient-based planning more stable and yields significantly higher success rates across a suite of goal-reaching tasks.

</details>


### [101] [Separable neural architectures as a primitive for unified predictive and generative intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12244)
*Reza T. Batley,Apurba Sarker,Rajib Mostakim,Andrew Klichine,Sourav Saha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出可分离神经架构（SNA），通过约束交互阶数和张量秩，将高维映射分解为低元组件，为跨物理、语言和感知的智能系统提供统一的因子化建模框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能系统（物理、语言、感知）常表现出可因子化结构，但现有神经架构多为整体式设计，未能显式利用这种结构。需要一种能统一加性、二次和张量分解模型的表示类，通过结构归纳偏置实现高效建模。

Method: 提出可分离神经架构（SNA），通过约束交互阶数和张量秩实现因子化。该方法采用坐标感知公式化，揭示混沌时空动力学与语言自回归之间的结构类比，将连续物理状态视为平滑可分离嵌入，支持确定性算子和分布建模的统一处理。

Result: 在四个领域验证了SNA的有效性：1）通过强化学习实现自主航点导航；2）多功能微结构的逆向生成；3）湍流的分布建模；4）神经语言建模。结果表明SNA能缓解确定性算子的非物理漂移特性，同时适用于离散序列。

Conclusion: 可分离神经架构作为领域无关的预测和生成智能原语，能够统一确定性和分布表示。该方法通过显式利用因子化结构，为跨物理、语言和感知的复杂系统建模提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Intelligent systems across physics, language and perception often exhibit factorisable structure, yet are typically modelled by monolithic neural architectures that do not explicitly exploit this structure. The separable neural architecture (SNA) addresses this by formalising a representational class that unifies additive, quadratic and tensor-decomposed neural models. By constraining interaction order and tensor rank, SNAs impose a structural inductive bias that factorises high-dimensional mappings into low-arity components. Separability need not be a property of the system itself: it often emerges in the coordinates or representations through which the system is expressed. Crucially, this coordinate-aware formulation reveals a structural analogy between chaotic spatiotemporal dynamics and linguistic autoregression. By treating continuous physical states as smooth, separable embeddings, SNAs enable distributional modelling of chaotic systems. This approach mitigates the nonphysical drift characteristics of deterministic operators whilst remaining applicable to discrete sequences. The compositional versatility of this approach is demonstrated across four domains: autonomous waypoint navigation via reinforcement learning, inverse generation of multifunctional microstructures, distributional modelling of turbulent flow and neural language modelling. These results establish the separable neural architecture as a domain-agnostic primitive for predictive and generative intelligence, capable of unifying both deterministic and distributional representations.

</details>


### [102] [Matching Features, Not Tokens: Energy-Based Fine-Tuning of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12248)
*Samy Jelassi,Mujin Kwun,Rosie Zhao,Yuanzhi Li,Nicolo Fusi,Yilun Du,Sham M. Kakade,Carles Domingo-Enrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EBFT：一种基于能量模型的微调方法，通过序列级特征匹配优化语言模型，在保持低交叉熵的同时提升下游任务性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交叉熵训练虽然提供密集监督，但仅优化教师强制下的下一个token预测，而非模型rollout下的序列级行为。需要一种能提供密集语义反馈但无需任务特定验证器或偏好模型的方法。

Method: 提出能量微调（EBFT）：1）使用跨步块并行采样从嵌套前缀并发生成多个rollout；2）在这些rollout上批量提取特征；3）使用得到的嵌入执行on-policy策略梯度更新。理论连接EBFT与KL正则化特征匹配和能量建模。

Result: 在问答编码、非结构化编码和翻译任务上，EBFT匹配RLVR并优于SFT的下游准确率，同时验证交叉熵低于两种方法。

Conclusion: EBFT通过序列级特征匹配提供密集语义监督，在保持低交叉熵的同时提升语言模型的下游任务性能，为语言模型微调提供了新范式。

Abstract: Cross-entropy (CE) training provides dense and scalable supervision for language models, but it optimizes next-token prediction under teacher forcing rather than sequence-level behavior under model rollouts. We introduce a feature-matching objective for language-model fine-tuning that targets sequence-level statistics of the completion distribution, providing dense semantic feedback without requiring a task-specific verifier or preference model. To optimize this objective efficiently, we propose energy-based fine-tuning (EBFT), which uses strided block-parallel sampling to generate multiple rollouts from nested prefixes concurrently, batches feature extraction over these rollouts, and uses the resulting embeddings to perform an on-policy policy-gradient update. We present a theoretical perspective connecting EBFT to KL-regularized feature-matching and energy-based modeling. Empirically, across Q&A coding, unstructured coding, and translation, EBFT matches RLVR and outperforms SFT on downstream accuracy while achieving a lower validation cross-entropy than both methods.

</details>


### [103] [The Latent Color Subspace: Emergent Order in High-Dimensional Chaos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12261)
*Mateusz Pach,Jessica Bader,Quentin Bouniot,Serge Belongie,Zeynep Akata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种解释FLUX.1变分自编码器潜在空间中颜色表示的方法，揭示了反映色调、饱和度和亮度的结构，并基于此开发了无需训练的显式颜色控制方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像生成模型发展迅速，但实现细粒度控制仍然困难，主要原因是缺乏对语义信息编码方式的理解。本文旨在解释FLUX.1变分自编码器潜在空间中的颜色表示，以更好地理解和控制生成图像的颜色属性。

Method: 开发了潜在颜色子空间（LCS）解释方法，揭示了FLUX.1变分自编码器潜在空间中反映色调、饱和度和亮度的结构。基于此提出了完全无需训练的显式颜色控制方法，仅通过闭式潜在空间操作实现。

Result: 验证了LCS解释的有效性，证明其既能预测颜色，又能显式控制颜色。该方法在FLUX模型中实现了基于潜在空间操作的颜色控制，无需额外训练。

Conclusion: 成功解释了FLUX.1变分自编码器潜在空间中的颜色表示结构，并开发了相应的显式颜色控制方法，为文本到图像生成的细粒度控制提供了新途径。

Abstract: Text-to-image generation models have advanced rapidly, yet achieving fine-grained control over generated images remains difficult, largely due to limited understanding of how semantic information is encoded. We develop an interpretation of the color representation in the Variational Autoencoder latent space of FLUX.1 [Dev], revealing a structure reflecting Hue, Saturation, and Lightness. We verify our Latent Color Subspace (LCS) interpretation by demonstrating that it can both predict and explicitly control color, introducing a fully training-free method in FLUX based solely on closed-form latent-space manipulation. Code is available at https://github.com/ExplainableML/LCS.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [The Laziness of the Crowd: Effort Aversion Among Raters Risks Undermining the Efficacy of X's Community Notes Program](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11120)
*Morgan Wack,Patrick Warren,Mustafa Alam*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究发现众包内容审核系统（如Twitter/X的社区笔记）存在"难度惩罚"现象：需要更多认知努力来验证的虚假信息（更可信的虚假信息）获得公开标注的可能性显著降低，导致系统可能无法有效处理最可能欺骗用户的"可信虚假信息"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究众包内容审核系统（如Twitter/X的社区笔记）在应对虚假信息时可能存在的系统性偏差。虽然先前研究关注了这类系统在减少虚假内容参与度方面的效果及其易受党派偏见影响的脆弱性，但本研究识别了一个先前未测试的机制：事实核查难度与审核者系统性不参与之间的联系。

Method: 使用18个月的疫苗相关社区笔记数据（2,250条帖子）和382名调查参与者的评分。通过额外分析和利用LLM流水线的事实核查流程来排除替代解释。研究假设需要较少认知努力评估的虚假信息（明显虚假且易于反驳）更可能获得公开标注。

Result: 研究发现，被感知为更难事实核查的虚假信息获得"有帮助"/公开状态标注的可能性显著降低。这种模式与审核者不愿意投入所需心理努力来评估和评分更可信的虚假信息相一致。众包审核可能系统性地无法处理最可能欺骗用户的"可信虚假信息"形式。

Conclusion: 众包内容审核系统存在"难度惩罚"问题，导致需要更多认知努力验证的虚假信息获得标注的可能性降低。这揭示了众包审核在应对最危险的可信虚假信息方面的系统性缺陷。研究讨论了平台设计的影响，并提出了减轻众包内容审核系统中这种难度惩罚的机制。

Abstract: Crowdsourced moderation systems like Twitter/X's Community Notes program have been proposed as scalable alternatives to professional fact-checkers for combating online misinformation. While prior research has examined the effectiveness of such systems in reducing engagement with false content and their vulnerability to partisan bias, we identify a previously untested mechanism linking fact-check difficulty to systematic non-participation by crowdsourced raters. We hypothesize that claims requiring less cognitive effort to evaluate, specifically, those that are obviously false and easy to refute, are more likely to receive public notes than claims that are more plausible and require greater effort to debunk. Using eighteen months of vaccine-related Community Notes data (2,250 posts) and ratings from 382 survey participants, we show that claims perceived as more difficult to fact-check are significantly less likely to receive notes that achieve ``helpful''/public status. Following the conduct of additional analyses and a fact-checking process utilizing an LLM pipeline to help rule out alternative explanations, we interpret this pattern as consistent with an unwillingness among raters to invest the mental effort required to evaluate and rate notes for more plausible misinformation. These findings suggest that crowdsourced moderation may systematically fail to address the forms of plausible misinformation which are most likely to deceive. We discuss implications for platform design and propose mechanisms to mitigate this difficulty penalty in crowdsourced content moderation systems.

</details>


### [105] [LLMs in social services: How does chatbot accuracy affect human accuracy?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11213)
*Jennah Gosciak,Eric Giannella,Zhaowen Guo,Michael Chen,Allison Koenecke*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 评估LLM聊天机器人建议对非营利组织个案工作者提供准确社会服务指导的影响，发现高质量聊天机器人能显著提升工作者准确性，但错误建议会严重降低表现，存在"AI依赖不足平台"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会服务项目（如SNAP食品券）的资格规则复杂难懂，非营利组织个案工作者需要同时处理十多个复杂项目。LLM聊天机器人可能为处理不常见情况的客户提供更好、更快的帮助，但需要评估其对个案工作者提供准确指导的潜在影响。

Method: 1) 创建包含770个困难但现实问题的多选题基准数据集；2) 在洛杉矶非营利外展组织中招募个案工作者进行随机实验；3) 控制组看不到聊天机器人建议，实验组看到人工设定不同准确率（53%-100%）的聊天机器人建议；4) 分析聊天机器人质量对个案工作者准确性的影响。

Result: 控制组平均准确率为49%。高质量聊天机器人（96-100%准确）使个案工作者准确性提高27个百分点。在问题层面，错误的聊天机器人建议会显著降低个案工作者准确性，在控制组表现最好的简单问题上降低三分之二。随着聊天机器人准确性提高，个案工作者准确性提升趋于平缓，出现"AI依赖不足平台"现象。

Conclusion: LLM聊天机器人能显著提升个案工作者提供准确指导的能力，但错误建议的危害很大。实际部署中需关注"AI依赖不足平台"现象，强调评估人机协作工具时需结合真实用户进行测试的重要性。

Abstract: Social service programs like the Supplemental Nutrition Assistance Program (SNAP, or food stamps) have eligibility rules that can be challenging to understand. For nonprofit caseworkers who often support clients in navigating a dozen or more complex programs, LLM-based chatbots may offer a means to provide better, faster help to clients whose situations may be less common. In this paper, we measure the potential effects of LLM-based chatbot suggestions on caseworkers' ability to provide accurate guidance. We first created a 770-question multiple-choice benchmark dataset of difficult, but realistic questions that a caseworker might receive. Next, using these benchmark questions and corresponding expert-verified answers, we conducted a randomized experiment with caseworkers recruited from nonprofit outreach organizations in Los Angeles. Caseworkers in the control condition did not see chatbot suggestions and had a mean accuracy of 49%. Caseworkers in the treatment condition saw chatbot suggestions that we artificially varied to range in aggregate accuracy from low (53%) to high (100%). Caseworker performance significantly improves as chatbot quality improves: high-quality chatbots (96-100% accurate) improved caseworker accuracy by 27 percentage points. At the question-level, incorrect chatbot suggestions substantially reduce caseworker accuracy, with a two-thirds reduction on easy questions where the control group performed best (without chatbot suggestions). Finally, improvements in caseworker accuracy level off as chatbot accuracy increases, a phenomenon that we call the "AI underreliance plateau," which is a concern for real-world deployment and highlights the importance of evaluating human-in-the-loop tools with their users.

</details>


### [106] [Understanding User Perceptions of Human-centered AI-Enhanced Support Group Formation in Online Healthcare Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11237)
*Pronob Kumar Barman,James R. Foulds,Tera L. Reynolds*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究评估了在线健康社区中算法个性化支持小组的价值、特征偏好和接受条件，发现用户对个性化支持小组有高需求，但需要解决信任、隐私和算法治理问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线健康社区规模庞大，患者和照护者难以找到最相关的同伴和内容，需要算法个性化支持小组来改善同伴匹配效率。

Method: 采用两阶段混合方法调查（N=165），研究在线健康社区参与模式、个性化优先级，以及模拟个性化支持小组的接受度。

Result: 模拟支持小组感知价值高（平均4.55/5；62.8%评5/5），91.5%愿意加入。同伴匹配重要性与感知价值强相关（ρ=0.764, p<0.001）。定性发现显示有条件接受：用户要求安全性、透明度、人工监督和数据控制。

Conclusion: 个性化支持小组有需求，但除非解决信任、隐私和算法治理问题，否则不会被采纳。需要关注安全性、透明度、人工监督和用户数据控制。

Abstract: Peer support is critical to managing chronic health conditions. Online health communities (OHCs) enable patients and caregivers to connect with similar others, yet their large scale makes it challenging to find the most relevant peers and content. This study assessed perceived value, preferred features, and acceptance conditions for algorithmically personalized support group formation within OHCs. A two-phase, mixed-methods survey (N=165) examined OHC participation patterns, personalization priorities, and acceptance of a simulated personalized support group. Perceived value of the simulated support group was high (mean 4.55/5; 62.8% rated 5/5) and 91.5% would join this group. The importance participants placed on peer matching strongly correlated with perceived value (\r{ho}=0.764, p<0.001). Qualitative findings revealed conditional acceptance: participants demand security, transparency, human oversight, and user control over data. Personalized support groups may be desired, but they will not be adopted unless trust, privacy, and algorithmic governance concerns are addressed.

</details>


### [107] ["I followed what felt right, not what I was told": Autonomy, Coaching, and Recognizing Bias Through AI-Mediated Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11274)
*Atieh Taheri,Hamza El Alaoui,Patrick Carrington,Jeffrey P. Bigham*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究测试AI中介对话如何影响对残障歧视微侵犯的识别，比较四种干预条件：偏见导向、包容导向、自我导向对话和纯文本阅读


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残障歧视微侵犯在日常互动中普遍存在，但帮助人们识别这些微侵犯的干预措施有限，需要探索AI中介对话在提升识别能力方面的潜力

Method: 160名参与者完成前测、干预和后测，分为四个条件：AI引导偏向偏见、AI引导包容、无引导对话和纯文本阅读，使用标准化社会体验和情感影响评分以及质性反思

Result: 对话条件比纯文本阅读产生更强的识别能力，但轨迹不同：偏见导向改善偏见与中性的区分但增加总体负面性；包容或无引导更平衡；纯文本阅读改善较弱甚至下降

Conclusion: AI中介对话能有效提升残障歧视识别，但引导方向产生不同效果：偏见导向可能被拒绝，包容导向被采纳为支架，为整合偏见相关引导的对话系统设计提供启示

Abstract: Ableist microaggressions remain pervasive in everyday interactions, yet interventions to help people recognize them are limited. We present an experiment testing how AI-mediated dialogue influences recognition of ableism. 160 participants completed a pre-test, intervention, and a post-test across four conditions: AI nudges toward bias (Bias-Directed), inclusion (Neutral-Directed), unguided dialogue (Self-Directed), and a text-only non-dialogue (Reading). Participants rated scenarios on standardness of social experience and emotional impact; those in dialogue-based conditions also provided qualitative reflections. Quantitative results showed dialogue-based conditions produced stronger recognition than Reading, though trajectories diverged: biased nudges improved differentiation of bias from neutrality but increased overall negativity. Inclusive or no nudges remained more balanced, while Reading participants showed weaker gains and even declines. Qualitative findings revealed biased nudges were often rejected, while inclusive nudges were adopted as scaffolding. We contribute a validated vignette corpus, an AI-mediated intervention platform, and design implications highlighting trade-offs conversational systems face when integrating bias-related nudges.

</details>


### [108] [Bridging the Cognitive Gap: Co-Designing and Evaluating a Voice-Enabled Community Chatbot for Older Adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11303)
*Feng Chen,Luna Xingyu Li,Ray-Yuan Chung,Wenyu Zeng,Yein Jeon,Yizhou Hu,Oleg Zaslavsky*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究评估了在退休社区部署语音大语言模型聊天机器人，采用"玻璃盒"方法结合多模态可访问性和AI教育，改善了老年人对AI的理解和信任，但80岁以上用户的可用性显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 退休社区的数字门户为老年人设置了物理和认知障碍，导致数字回避。生成式AI通过自然语言交互提供了解决方案，但其"黑盒"特性和可用性挑战阻碍了采用。

Method: 在太平洋西北地区的持续护理退休社区评估语音大语言模型聊天机器人，采用混合方法的共同设计和素养工作坊（N=25），应用"玻璃盒"方法结合多模态可访问性和有意的AI教育。

Result: 干预显著提高了参与者的技术理解（p=0.004）和感知透明度（p=0.001），将他们的交互模式从盲目信任转变为优先考虑可验证证据的知情依赖。语音输入降低了认知负荷，但80岁及以上用户的可用性评分显著下降（r=-0.50）。

Conclusion: 真正包容年龄的AI必须超越基于触摸的界面，向零触摸导航发展。语音交互虽然降低了认知负荷，但对高龄用户仍存在可用性挑战，需要进一步优化以实现真正的年龄包容性。

Abstract: Digital portals in retirement communities often create physical and cognitive barriers for older adults, leading to digital avoidance. Generative AI offers a solution by enabling natural language interaction, yet its adoption is hindered by the opaque, "Black Box" nature of these systems and lingering usability challenges. To address this, we evaluated a voice-enabled Large Language Model (LLM) chatbot at a continuing care retirement community in the Pacific Northwest. Through a mixed-methods Co-Design and Literacy Workshop (N=25), we applied a "Glass Box" approach combining multimodal accessibility with intentional AI education. The intervention significantly improved participants' technical understanding (p=0.004) and perceived transparency (p=0.001), shifting their interaction model from blind trust to informed reliance prioritizing verifiable evidence. While voice input reduced cognitive load, usability scores dropped significantly for users aged 80 and older (r=-0.50), indicating that truly age-inclusive AI must evolve beyond touch-based interfaces toward zero-touch navigation.

</details>


### [109] [To Believe or Not To Believe: Comparing Supporting Information Tools to Aid Human Judgments of AI Veracity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11393)
*Jessica Irons,Patrick Cooper,Necva Bolucu,Roelien Timmer,Huichen Yang,Changhyun Lee,Brian Jin,Andreas Duenser,Stephen Wan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨了在生成式AI数据提取工具中，不同类型支持信息（完整源文本、段落检索、LLM解释）如何影响用户真实性评估行为，发现段落检索在准确性和速度间提供合理平衡，而LLM解释虽快速但导致不当依赖和信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着对生成式AI幻觉风险意识的增强，用户需要工具来评估AI生成答案的真实性。虽然已有多种系统支持方式，但缺乏关于用户如何实际将这些信息纳入决策过程的实证证据。本研究旨在填补这一知识空白。

Method: 在生成式AI数据提取工具背景下，通过用户研究比较三种支持信息类型：完整源文本、段落检索和LLM解释。从效率、有效性、依赖性和信任四个维度分析用户行为，并考察信息需求复杂度的影响。

Result: 段落检索在准确性和速度间提供合理平衡，真实性判断与使用完整源文本相当。LLM解释虽能快速评估，但导致不当依赖和信任，用户更不易检测错误。初步证据表明，对于复杂答案，不当依赖问题更严重。

Conclusion: 通过严格的用户评估，可以更好地开发支持有效和负责任人类自主性的系统，用于真实性评估过程。段落检索是实用折中方案，而LLM解释需要谨慎设计以避免不当依赖。

Abstract: With increasing awareness of the hallucination risks of generative artificial intelligence (AI), we see a growing shift toward providing information tooling to help users determine the veracity of AI-generated answers for themselves. User responsibility for assessing veracity is particularly critical for certain sectors that rely on on-demand, AI-generated data extraction, such as biomedical research and the legal sector. While prior work offers us a variety of ways in which systems can provide such support, there is a lack of empirical evidence on how this information is actually incorporated into the user's decision-making process. Our user study takes a step toward filling this knowledge gap. In the context of a generative AI data extraction tool, we examine the relationship between the type of supporting information (full source text, passage retrieval, and Large Language Model (LLM) explanations) and user behavior in the veracity assessment process, examined through the lens of efficiency, effectiveness, reliance and trust. We find that passage retrieval offers a reasonable compromise between accuracy and speed, with judgments of veracity comparable to using the full source text. LLM explanations, while also enabling rapid assessments, fostered inappropriate reliance and trust on the data extraction AI, such that participants were less likely to detect errors. In additiona, we analyzed the impacts of the complexity of the information need, finding preliminary evidence that inappropriate reliance is worse for complex answers. We demonstrate how, through rigorous user evaluation, we can better develop systems that allow for effective and responsible human agency in veracity assessment processes.

</details>


### [110] [Evaluation format, not model capability, drives triage failure in the assessment of consumer health AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11413)
*David Fraile Navarro,Farah Magrabi,Enrico Coiera*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究发现，先前关于ChatGPT健康分诊低估51.6%急诊的研究使用了与真实使用场景不符的考试式评估方法。在更自然的患者式交互条件下，前沿LLMs的分诊准确率提高了6.4个百分点，某些疾病的分诊准确率从48%提升到80%。强制选择题格式是主要失败机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑Ramaswamy等人在《自然医学》上发表的关于ChatGPT健康分诊低估51.6%急诊的研究结论，认为其使用的考试式评估协议（强制A/B/C/D输出、知识抑制、禁止澄清问题）与消费者实际使用健康聊天机器人的方式存在根本差异，可能导致对AI分诊安全风险的错误评估。

Method: 测试了五个前沿LLMs（GPT-5.2、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Claude Opus 4.6、Gemini 3 Flash、Gemini 3.1 Pro），在17个场景的部分复制库上进行对比研究。采用两种条件：1）受约束的考试式条件（1,275次试验）；2）自然主义的患者式消息条件（850次试验）。进行了针对性消融实验，并使用作者发布的提示进行提示忠实性检查。

Result: 自然主义交互使分诊准确率提高了6.4个百分点（p=0.015）。糖尿病酮症酸中毒在所有模型和条件下均达到100%正确分诊。哮喘分诊从48%提高到80%。强制A/B/C/D格式是主要失败机制：三个模型在强制选择下得分0-24%，但在自由文本下达到100%（所有p<10^{-8}）。这些模型用自己的语言一致推荐急诊护理，但强制选择格式却记录为低估分诊。

Conclusion: 先前报道的51.6%低估分诊率高度依赖于评估格式，不应被解释为部署分诊行为的稳定估计。消费者健康AI的有效评估需要在反映实际使用情况的条件下进行测试。评估方法的选择对AI分诊性能的评估结果有决定性影响。

Abstract: Ramaswamy et al. reported in \textit{Nature Medicine} that ChatGPT Health under-triages 51.6\% of emergencies, concluding that consumer-facing AI triage poses safety risks. However, their evaluation used an exam-style protocol -- forced A/B/C/D output, knowledge suppression, and suppression of clarifying questions -- that differs fundamentally from how consumers use health chatbots. We tested five frontier LLMs (GPT-5.2, Claude Sonnet 4.6, Claude Opus 4.6, Gemini 3 Flash, Gemini 3.1 Pro) on a 17-scenario partial replication bank under constrained (exam-style, 1,275 trials) and naturalistic (patient-style messages, 850 trials) conditions, with targeted ablations and prompt-faithful checks using the authors' released prompts. Naturalistic interaction improved triage accuracy by 6.4 percentage points ($p = 0.015$). Diabetic ketoacidosis was correctly triaged in 100\% of trials across all models and conditions. Asthma triage improved from 48\% to 80\%. The forced A/B/C/D format was the dominant failure mechanism: three models scored 0--24\% with forced choice but 100\% with free text (all $p < 10^{-8}$), consistently recommending emergency care in their own words while the forced-choice format registered under-triage. Prompt-faithful checks on the authors' exact released prompts confirmed the scaffold produces model-dependent, case-dependent results. The headline under-triage rate is highly contingent on evaluation format and should not be interpreted as a stable estimate of deployed triage behavior. Valid evaluation of consumer health AI requires testing under conditions that reflect actual use.

</details>


### [111] [From Pen Strokes to Sleep States: Detecting Low-Recovery Days Using Sigma-Lognormal Handwriting Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11512)
*Chisa Tanaka,Andrew Vargo,Anna Scius-Bertrand,Andreas Fischer,Koichi Kise*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究探索了从日常在线手写动态中检测健康个体生理恢复状态波动的可行性，发现可以通过手写特征有效识别低恢复状态的日子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手写研究主要关注字符识别和疾病分类，但手写动态反映健康个体日常生理波动的潜力尚未被探索。本研究旨在探究是否可以从在线手写动态中推断与睡眠相关的日常恢复状态变化。

Method: 采用个性化二元分类框架，使用Sigma-Lognormal模型提取手写特征，该模型捕捉笔划的神经运动生成过程。在为期28天的野外研究中，13名大学生每天记录三次手写数据，同时使用可穿戴戒指测量夜间心脏指标。将四种睡眠相关指标（HRV、最低心率、平均心率、总睡眠时长）的最低（或最高）四分位数定义为正类（低恢复状态）。采用留出一天交叉验证进行评估。

Result: 经过FDR校正后，所有四个变量的PR-AUC均显著超过基线（0.25），其中与心脏相关的变量表现最强。重要的是，分类性能在不同任务类型或记录时间上没有显著差异，表明恢复相关信号嵌入在一般运动动态中。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，个体内部细微的自主神经恢复波动可以从日常手写中检测出来，这为非侵入性、设备独立的健康监测开辟了新方向。

Abstract: While handwriting has traditionally been studied for character recognition and disease classification, its potential to reflect day-to-day physiological fluctuations in healthy individuals remains unexplored. This study examines whether daily variations in sleep-related recovery states can be inferred from online handwriting dynamics. %
We propose a personalized binary classification framework that detects low-recovery days using features derived from the Sigma-Lognormal model, which captures the neuromotor generation process of pen strokes. In a 28-day in-the-wild study involving 13 university students, handwriting was recorded three times daily, and nocturnal cardiac indicators were measured using a wearable ring. For each participant, the lowest (or highest) quartile of four sleep-related metrics -- HRV, lowest heart rate, average heart rate, and total sleep duration -- defined the positive class. Leave-One-Day-Out cross-validation showed that PR-AUC significantly exceeded the baseline (0.25) for all four variables after FDR correction, with the strongest performance observed for cardiac-related variables. Importantly, classification performance did not differ significantly across task types or recording timings, indicating that recovery-related signals are embedded in general movement dynamics. These results demonstrate that subtle within-person autonomic recovery fluctuations can be detected from everyday handwriting, opening a new direction for non-invasive, device-independent health monitoring.

</details>


### [112] [Prediction of Grade, Gender, and Academic Performance of Children and Teenagers from Handwriting Using the Sigma-Lognormal Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11519)
*Adrian Iste,Kazuki Nishizawa,Chisa Tanaka,Andrew Vargo,Anna Scius-Bertrand,Andreas Fischer,Koichi Kise*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究利用大规模日本学生数字笔迹数据，分析笔迹动力学特征与学生特征（年级、性别、学业表现）的关系，发现笔迹动力学包含可测量的发育阶段和个体差异信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字笔迹采集能够捕捉反映书写行为背后运动过程的详细时间和运动学信号。虽然笔迹分析在临床或成人群体中已得到广泛探索，但其在研究儿童发展和教育特征方面的潜力仍未得到充分研究。

Method: 使用日本小学到初中学生的大规模在线数据集，系统比较三类笔迹衍生特征：运动学信号的基本统计描述符、基于熵的变异性度量、以及从sigma-lognormal模型获得的参数。特征在学生层面聚合，使用线性/逻辑回归和随机森林模型在一致的实验设置下评估三个预测任务：年级预测、性别分类和学业表现分类。

Result: 结果显示笔迹动力学包含与发育阶段和个体差异相关的可测量信号，特别是在年级预测任务中表现显著。这些发现突显了运动学笔迹分析的潜力，并证实随着儿童发展，他们的笔迹朝着对数正态运动组织演化。

Conclusion: 笔迹动力学分析能够捕捉儿童发展和个体差异的重要信息，为教育评估和儿童发展研究提供了新的量化工具，同时验证了儿童笔迹运动组织向对数正态模式发展的理论。

Abstract: Digital handwriting acquisition enables the capture of detailed temporal and kinematic signals reflecting the motor processes underlying writing behavior. While handwriting analysis has been extensively explored in clinical or adult populations, its potential for studying developmental and educational characteristics in children remains less investigated. In this work, we examine whether handwriting dynamics encode information related to student characteristics using a large-scale online dataset collected from Japanese students from elementary school to junior high school. We systematically compare three families of handwriting-derived features: basic statistical descriptors of kinematic signals, entropy-based measures of variability, and parameters obtained from the sigma-lognormal model. Although the dataset contains dense stroke-level recordings, features are aggregated at the student level to enable a controlled comparison between representations. These features are evaluated across three prediction tasks: grade prediction, gender classification, and academic performance classification, using Linear or Logistic Regression and Random Forest models under consistent experimental settings. The results show that handwriting dynamics contain measurable signals related to developmental stage and individual differences, especially for the grade prediction task. These findings highlight the potential of kinematic handwriting analysis and confirm that through their development, children's handwriting evolves toward a lognormal motor organization.

</details>


### [113] [Shadowless Projection Mapping for Tabletop Workspaces with Synthetic Aperture Projector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11551)
*Takahiro Okamoto,Masaki Takeuchi,Masataka Sawayama,Daisuke Iwai*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出合成孔径投影映射系统，使用大量密集排列的投影仪实现无延迟、无阴影的桌面投影，无需计算补偿，并开发了离线的模糊补偿方法和最小化"投影感"的设计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统投影映射系统存在投影阴影问题（用户遮挡光路），而采用多个分布式投影仪的现有方法虽然能补偿遮挡，但存在计算处理导致的延迟，影响用户体验。

Method: 1. 合成孔径投影映射系统：使用大量密集排列的投影仪，实现无延迟、无阴影的桌面投影；2. 离线模糊补偿方法：解决叠加投影中子像素错位导致的空间分辨率下降问题，计算时间与投影仪数量无关；3. PM设计框架：基于用户研究建立最小化"投影感"的设计框架。

Result: 实现了无延迟、无阴影的投影映射系统，解决了传统方法的遮挡问题；开发的模糊补偿方法有效处理了分辨率下降问题；通过用户研究建立了最小化"投影感"的设计框架，证明无阴影投影在改变材料属性而不产生投影印象方面发挥关键作用。

Conclusion: 合成孔径投影映射系统通过大量密集排列的投影仪实现了无延迟、无阴影的桌面投影，结合离线模糊补偿方法和最小化投影感的设计框架，为投影映射技术提供了新的解决方案，特别是在多用户交互和增强现实应用中具有重要价值。

Abstract: Projection mapping (PM) enables augmented reality (AR) experiences without requiring users to wear head-mounted displays and supports multi-user interaction. It is regarded as a promising technology for a variety of applications in which users interact with content superimposed onto augmented objects in tabletop workspaces, including remote collaboration, healthcare, industrial design, urban planning, artwork creation, and office work. However, conventional PM systems often suffer from projection shadows when users occlude the light path. Prior approaches employing multiple distributed projectors can compensate for occlusion, but suffer from latency due to computational processing, degrading the user experience. In this research, we introduce a synthetic-aperture PM system that uses a significantly larger number of projectors, arranged densely in the environment, to achieve delay-free, shadowless projection for tabletop workspaces without requiring computational compensation. To address spatial resolution degradation caused by subpixel misalignment among overlaid projections, we develop and validate an offline blur compensation method whose computation time remains independent of the number of projectors. Furthermore, we demonstrate that our shadowless PM plays a critical role in achieving a fundamental goal of PM: altering material properties without evoking projection-like impression. Specifically, we define this perceptual impression as ``sense of projection (SoP)'' and establish a PM design framework to minimize the SoP based on user studies.

</details>


### [114] [High-Contrast Projection Mapping under Light Field Illumination with LED Display and Aperiodic Lens Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11573)
*Kotaro Fujimura,Hiroki Kusuyama,Masaki Takeuchi,Daisuke Iwai*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一种新型目标排除照明方法，通过LED显示面板和非周期性透镜阵列实现光场照明，解决投影映射在明亮环境下的暗室限制问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投影映射技术需要暗环境才能保证高质量的投影效果，这种"暗室限制"限制了其实用性，需要克服这一限制以在明亮环境中实现高对比度投影

Method: 结合LED显示面板和优化的非周期性透镜阵列实现光场照明，选择性照亮周围环境而避开投影目标；开发计算技术优化非周期性透镜布局以抑制串扰导致的暗斑，以及计算LED亮度模式的高效方法

Result: 原型系统实验表明，该方法在明亮环境中实现了高对比度的投影映射，紧凑的尺寸提供了大有效光源面积，再现了与典型照明相当的自然柔和阴影，同时保持了精确避开目标所需的空间可控性

Conclusion: 提出的目标排除照明方法成功克服了投影映射的暗室限制，通过在明亮环境中实现高对比度投影，为投影映射的实际应用开辟了新可能性
