Abstract: Projection Mapping (PM) is a technology that projects images onto the surfaces of physical objects, allowing multiple users to share an augmented reality experience without special devices. However, its practical use has been constrained by the need for dark environments to ensure high-quality projection. To overcome this ``dark-room constraint,'' we propose a novel target-excluding lighting method that selectively illuminates the surrounding environment while avoiding the PM target. Our system achieves light-field illumination by combining an LED display panel with an optimized aperiodic lens array. The key contributions include a compact form factor that provides a large effective light source area, reproducing natural soft shadows comparable to typical lighting, while maintaining the spatial controllability needed to precisely avoid the target. We also introduce a computational technique for optimizing aperiodic lens placement to suppress undesired dark spots caused by crosstalk, and efficient methods for computing LED luminance patterns that enable dynamic PM. Experiments with a prototype system demonstrate that our approach achieves high-contrast PM even in bright environments.

</details>


### [115] [From Pets to Robots: MojiKit as a Data-Informed Toolkit for Affective HRI Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11632)
*Liwen He,Pingting Chen,Ziheng Tang,Yixiao Liu,Jihong Jeung,Teng Han,Xin Tong*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: MojiKit：一个基于宠物互动分析的系统化工具包，用于设计动物启发社交机器人的情感交互行为


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前设计动物启发社交机器人的情感行为主要依赖直觉和个人经验，导致设计结果零散且缺乏系统性。需要更系统化的指导方法来帮助设计师创建更丰富、更多样的情感交互模式。

Method: 1. 编码分析人-宠物互动视频，结合文献和访谈验证见解
2. 创建结构化参考卡片，映射宠物启发情感交互的设计空间
3. 开发MojiKit工具包：包含参考卡片、仿生机器人原型(MomoBot)和行为控制工作室
4. 通过18名参与者的共创工作坊进行评估

Result: 1. MojiKit帮助参与者设计了35种超越个人宠物经验的情感交互模式
2. 无代码工作室降低了技术门槛，增强了创作自主性
3. 参与者能够系统化地创建更丰富、更多样的机器人情感行为

Conclusion: MojiKit提供了一个数据驱动的结构化资源，将参考材料与动手原型制作相结合，通过实证证据表明该工具包能够赋能用户系统化地设计更丰富、更多样的情感机器人行为，解决了依赖直觉和经验的设计局限性。

Abstract: Designing affective behaviors for animal-inspired social robots often relies on intuition and personal experience, leading to fragmented outcomes. To provide more systematic guidance, we first coded and analyzed human-pet interaction videos, validated insights through literature and interviews, and created structured reference cards that map the design space of pet-inspired affective interactions. Building on this, we developed MojiKit, a toolkit combining reference cards, a zoomorphic robot prototype (MomoBot), and a behavior control studio. We evaluated MojiKit in co-creation workshops with 18 participants, finding that MojiKit helped them design 35 affective interaction patterns beyond their own pet experiences, while the code-free studio lowered the technical barrier and enhanced creative agency. Our contributions include the data-informed structured resource for pet-inspired affective HRI design, an integrated toolkit that bridges reference materials with hands-on prototyping, and empirical evidence showing how MojiKit empowers users to systematically create richer, more diverse affective robot behaviors.

</details>


### [116] [From Control to Foresight: Simulation as a New Paradigm for Human-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11677)
*Gaole He,Brian Y. Lim*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出"模拟在环"交互范式，让用户和智能体在决策前探索模拟的未来轨迹，将干预从被动猜测转变为知情探索


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM驱动的自主智能体与人类交互是点状和反应式的，用户只能批准或纠正单个动作来降低即时风险，但缺乏对后续后果的可见性。这迫使用户在心理上模拟长期影响，这是一个认知要求高且通常不准确的过程。用户对单个步骤有控制权，但缺乏做出知情决策的远见。

Method: 提出"模拟在环"交互范式，使人类用户和智能体能够在决策前探索模拟的未来轨迹。通过模拟将干预从反应式猜测转变为知情探索，同时帮助用户在过程中发现潜在的约束和偏好。

Result: 这是一篇视角论文，主要贡献在于：1) 描述当前范式的局限性；2) 引入基于模拟的协作概念框架；3) 通过具体的人机协作场景说明其潜力。没有提供具体的实验结果，而是提出了一个新的交互范式。

Conclusion: 有效的协作需要远见而不仅仅是控制。"模拟在环"范式通过让用户和智能体在决策前探索模拟的未来轨迹，将干预从被动猜测转变为知情探索，帮助用户发现潜在的约束和偏好，从而改善人机协作。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to power autonomous agents for complex, multi-step tasks. However, human-agent interaction remains pointwise and reactive: users approve or correct individual actions to mitigate immediate risks, without visibility into subsequent consequences. This forces users to mentally simulate long-term effects, a cognitively demanding and often inaccurate process. Users have control over individual steps but lack the foresight to make informed decisions. We argue that effective collaboration requires foresight, not just control. We propose simulation-in-the-loop, an interaction paradigm that enables users and agents to explore simulated future trajectories before committing to decisions. Simulation transforms intervention from reactive guesswork into informed exploration, while helping users discover latent constraints and preferences along the way. This perspective paper characterizes the limitations of current paradigms, introduces a conceptual framework for simulation-based collaboration, and illustrates its potential through concrete human-agent collaboration scenarios.

</details>


### [117] [PhiPlot: A Web-Based Interactive EDA Environment for Atmospherically Relevant Molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11751)
*Matias Loukojärvi,Ananth Mahadevan,Katsiaryna Haitsiukevich,Kai Puolamäki*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: PhiPlot：用于大气化学高维分子数据集交互式探索和知识引导降维的Web平台


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算化学进展产生了大量大气相关分子的高维数据集，但缺乏有效探索工具，特别是用于研究大气气溶胶形成过程

Method: 开发基于Web的交互式探索环境，集成可视化、聚类和领域知识引导的嵌入优化，连接到现有分子数据库集合

Result: PhiPlot能够发现数据中的模式并支持假设生成，为大气化学数据驱动研究提供可访问界面

Conclusion: PhiPlot为大气化学高维数据集探索提供了有效工具，特别适用于气溶胶形成研究，通过知识引导降维增强数据洞察

Abstract: Advances in computational chemistry have produced high-dimensional datasets on atmospherically relevant molecules. To aid exploration of such datasets, particularly for the study of atmospheric aerosol formation, we introduce PhiPlot: a web-based environment for interactive exploration and knowledge-based dimensionality reduction. The integration of visualisation, clustering, and domain knowledge-guided embedding refinement enables the discovery of patterns in the data and supports hypothesis generation. The application connects to an existing, evolving collection of molecular databases, offering an accessible interface for data-driven research in atmospheric chemistry.

</details>


### [118] [Modeling Trial-and-Error Navigation With a Sequential Decision Model of Information Scent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11759)
*Xiaofu Jin,Yunpeng Bai,Antti Oulasvirta*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 将信息觅食理论扩展为考虑记忆约束的序列决策问题，解释用户在导航中的过早选择、错误转向和回溯恢复行为


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户在信息架构中定位项目时经常遇到困难，特别是当链接模糊或深度嵌套时。传统的信息觅食理论假设用户会查看所有可用链接后再做决定，但实际上用户经常过快选择链接、忽略相关线索，并在出错时依赖回溯。

Method: 将导航框架化为记忆约束下的序列决策问题，假设用户不会扫描整个页面，而是策略性地检查"刚好足够"的内容以在时间预算内找到目标。用户选择下一个检查项目时同时考虑局部（当前页面）和全局（站点）信息线索，但两者都受记忆限制。

Result: 与经验数据的比较显示，该模型能够复现关键的导航行为：过早选择、错误转向和通过回溯恢复。模型表明，当考虑到导航问题的序列性和有限性特征时，试错行为可以通过信息觅食理论得到很好解释。

Conclusion: 试错行为可以通过信息觅食理论得到很好解释，前提是考虑到导航问题的序列性和有限性特征。用户不是扫描整个页面，而是策略性地检查"刚好足够"的内容，在局部和全局信息线索的约束下做出决策，这解释了过早选择和错误转向等常见行为。

Abstract: Users often struggle to locate an item within an information architecture, particularly when links are ambiguous or deeply nested in hierarchies. Information scent has been used to explain why users select incorrect links, but this concept assumes that users see all available links before deciding. In practice, users frequently select a link too quickly, overlook relevant cues, and then rely on backtracking when errors occur. We extend the concept of information scent by framing navigation as a sequential decision-making problem under memory constraints. Specifically, we assume that users do not scan entire pages but instead inspect strategically, looking "just enough" to find the target given their time budget. To choose which item to inspect next, they consider both local (this page) and global (site) scent; however, both are constrained by memory. Trying to avoid wasting time, they occasionally choose the wrong links without inspecting everything on a page. Comparisons with empirical data show that our model replicates key navigation behaviors: premature selections, wrong turns, and recovery from backtracking. We conclude that trial-and-error behavior is well explained by information scent when accounting for the sequential and bounded characteristics of the navigation problem.

</details>


### [119] [HiSync: Spatio-Temporally Aligning Hand Motion from Wearable IMU and On-Robot Camera for Command Source Identification in Long-Range HRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11809)
*Chengwen Zhang,Chun Yu,Borong Zhuang,Haopeng Jin,Qingyang Wan,Zhuojun Li,Zhe He,Zhoutong Ye,Yu Mei,Chang Liu,Weinan Shi,Yuanchun Shi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: HiSync是一个光学-惯性融合框架，通过将机器人摄像头的光流与手戴IMU信号对齐，实现远距离多人人机交互中的命令源识别，在34米距离内达到92.32%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远距离人机交互(HRI)研究不足，其中的命令源识别(CSI)因多用户和距离导致的传感器模糊而特别具有挑战性。现有方法难以在远距离多人场景中准确识别谁发出了命令。

Method: HiSync采用光学-惯性融合框架，将手部运动作为绑定线索。首先收集用户定义的手势集和多模态命令手势数据集，然后从摄像头和IMU数据中提取频域手部运动特征。使用学习的CSINet对IMU读数去噪，时间对齐多模态数据，并进行距离感知的多窗口融合来计算细微自然手势的跨模态相似度。

Result: 在三人场景中，HiSync在34米距离内达到92.32%的CSI准确率，比先前最先进方法提升48.44%。系统在真实机器人部署中得到验证，实现了可靠自然的命令源识别。

Conclusion: HiSync通过使命令源识别可靠且自然，为公共空间人机交互提供了实用的原语和设计指导，解决了远距离多人交互中的关键挑战。

Abstract: Long-range Human-Robot Interaction (HRI) remains underexplored. Within it, Command Source Identification (CSI) - determining who issued a command - is especially challenging due to multi-user and distance-induced sensor ambiguity. We introduce HiSync, an optical-inertial fusion framework that treats hand motion as binding cues by aligning robot-mounted camera optical flow with hand-worn IMU signals. We first elicit a user-defined (N=12) gesture set and collect a multimodal command gesture dataset (N=38) in long-range multi-user HRI scenarios. Next, HiSync extracts frequency-domain hand motion features from both camera and IMU data, and a learned CSINet denoises IMU readings, temporally aligns modalities, and performs distance-aware multi-window fusion to compute cross-modal similarity of subtle, natural gestures, enabling robust CSI. In three-person scenes up to 34m, HiSync achieves 92.32% CSI accuracy, outperforming the prior SOTA by 48.44%. HiSync is also validated on real-robot deployment. By making CSI reliable and natural, HiSync provides a practical primitive and design guidance for public-space HRI.

</details>


### [120] [Stuck on Suggestions: Automation Bias, the Anchoring Effect, and the Factors That Shape Them in Computational Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11821)
*Emely Rosbach,Jonas Ammeling,Jonathan Ganz,Christof Albert Bertram,Thomas Conrad,Andreas Riener,Marc Aubreville*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AI辅助病理诊断可提高准确性但引入认知偏差，时间压力加剧偏差严重性，专业经验降低AI依赖


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究AI驱动决策支持系统在计算病理学中可能引入的认知偏差问题，特别是自动化偏差和锚定偏差，这些偏差在时间压力下可能被放大，影响诊断准确性

Method: 在线实验设计，28名病理专家独立估计肿瘤细胞百分比，然后在AI支持下重复估计；部分实验在时间压力下进行；使用线性混合效应模型分析AI建议的影响和自动化依赖

Result: AI辅助整体提高诊断性能，但引入7%自动化偏差率；时间压力未增加偏差频率但加剧其严重性；AI建议产生显著锚定效应，时间压力下更明显；专业经验和自我效能感降低AI依赖，AI辅助决策中更高信心增加AI依赖

Conclusion: AI集成临床工作流程具有双重性：提高性能的同时引入偏差驱动诊断错误的风险；需要针对性策略减轻认知偏差，特别是时间压力下

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI)-driven decision support systems can improve diagnostic accuracy and efficiency in computational pathology. However, collaboration between human experts and AI may introduce cognitive biases such as automation and anchoring bias, where users adopt system predictions blindly or are disproportionately influenced by AI advice, even when inaccurate. These effects may be amplified under time pressure, common in routine pathology, or shaped by individual user characteristics. We conducted an online experiment in which pathology experts (n = 28) estimated tumor cell percentages: once independently and once with AI support. A subset of estimations in each condition was performed under time strain. Overall, AI assistance improved diagnostic performance but introduced a 7% automation bias rate, defined as accepted negative consultations where previously correct independent judgments were overturned by incorrect AI advice. While time pressure did not increase the frequency of automation bias, it appeared to intensify its severity, reflected in stronger performance declines associated with increased AI reliance under cognitive load. A linear mixed-effects model (LMM) simulating weighted averaging showed a statistically significant positive coefficient for AI advice, indicating moderate anchoring on system output. This effect increased under time pressure, suggesting anchoring bias becomes more pronounced when cognitive resources are limited. A second LMM assessing automation reliance, a proxy for automation and anchoring bias, showed that professional experience and self-efficacy were associated with lower dependence on AI, whereas higher confidence during AI-assisted decisions was tied to increased AI reliance. These findings highlight the dual nature of AI integration in clinical workflows: improving performance while introducing risks of bias-driven diagnostic errors.

</details>


### [121] [Design Exploration of Lightweight Interactions for Awareness-Supporting Technologies in Hybrid Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11977)
*Lu Liu,Harm van Essen,Berry Eggen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究探索了在混合工作环境中通过轻量级交互和信息显示技术促进非正式沟通的设计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合工作环境缺乏传统办公室中自然产生的偶遇和同事活动环境感知，这可能阻碍团队协作，因此需要探索如何通过技术支持非正式沟通。

Method: 采用体验式设计方法，关注信息感知和处理而非显式内容交换；通过头脑风暴、推测和原型设计探索小型混合团队的设计空间；通过标注和分析设计概念、推测场景和原型来开发框架。

Result: 开发了一个框架，识别了轻量级交互的设计选项以及将其与信息显示集成的方法。

Conclusion: 通过关注信息感知而非内容交换的体验式设计方法，可以为混合工作环境开发有效的轻量级交互和信息显示集成方案，促进非正式沟通。

Abstract: Hybrid work settings often lack the informal communication that naturally emerges from spontaneous encounters and ambient awareness of coworkers' activities, potentially hindering team collaboration. To address this challenge, we explored how lightweight interactions can be integrated into awareness-supporting technologies for fostering informal communication. Our experiential design approach focused on how information is perceived and processed rather than explicit content exchange. Through brainstorming, speculating, and prototyping, we explored the design space for small hybrid teams. By annotating and analyzing design concepts, speculative scenarios, and prototypes, we developed a framework that identified design options for lightweight interactions and methods for integrating them with information displays.

</details>


### [122] [ConvScale: Conversational Interviews for Scale-Aligned Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11988)
*Peinuan Qin,Jingzhu Chen,Yitian Yang,Han Meng,Zicheng Zhu,Yi-Chieh Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: ConvScale：一种将心理测量量表转化为自然对话访谈的AI支持方法，用于从访谈数据中预测项目级分数并生成量表评估


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话访谈通常用于补充结构化调查，获取丰富的情境化回答，但通常只进行定性分析。其在定量测量方面的潜力尚未得到充分探索。本研究旨在探索如何通过对话访谈支持定量测量。

Method: 提出ConvScale方法，将心理测量量表转化为自然对话访谈，同时保留原始测量结构。基于访谈数据，预测项目级分数并聚合生成量表评估。通过18名参与者的组内研究进行验证。

Result: ConvScale生成的分数在项目水平和构念水平上与参与者自我报告分数高度一致，同时保持中等内部信度。但结构效度不足。

Conclusion: 讨论了通过访谈支持定量测量的潜力，并为未来设计提出了启示。虽然ConvScale在分数一致性方面表现良好，但结构效度问题需要进一步改进。

Abstract: Conversational interviews are commonly used to complement structured surveys by eliciting rich and contextualized responses, which are typically analyzed qualitatively. However, their potential contribution to quantitative measurement remains underexplored. In this paper, we introduce ConvScale, an AI-supported approach that transforms psychometric scales into natural conversational interviews while preserving the original measurement structure. Based on interview data, ConvScale predicts item-level scores and aggregates them to derive scale-based assessments. In a within-subjects study with 18 participants, our results show that ConvScale-derived scores align closely with participants' self-report scores at both the item and construct levels, while maintaining moderate internal reliability; however, the structural validity was inadequate. In light of this, we discussed the potential of supporting quantitative measurement through interviews and proposed implications for future designs.

</details>


### [123] [Human-Centred LLM Privacy Audits: Findings and Frictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12094)
*Dimitri Staufer,Kirsten Morehouse,David Hartmann,Bettina Berendt*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: LMP2：基于浏览器的LLM自我审计工具，用于检查语言模型与个人姓名的关联，研究发现GPT-4o能预测普通人11/50特征，用户希望控制模型生成的关联，但并非所有输出都被视为隐私侵犯


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型从海量训练数据和用户交互中学习统计关联，部署的系统可能暴露或推断个人信息，但个人缺乏实用方法来检查模型与其姓名的关联

Method: 开发LMP2浏览器自审计工具，进行两项用户研究（N=458），使用GPT-4o预测普通人特征，评估八个LLM在公众人物和不存在姓名上的表现，验证探测方法

Result: GPT-4o对普通人50个特征中的11个预测准确率≥60%；参与者希望控制LLM生成的关联，但并非所有输出都被视为隐私侵犯；在公众人物和不存在姓名上观察到稳定的姓名条件关联与模型默认值之间的明显分离

Conclusion: 研究揭示了生成式AI评估危机：当输出是概率性、上下文依赖且通过提示调节时，模型-个体关联的定义不明确；为推进可靠、可操作的人本LLM隐私审计，识别了研究中出现的九个摩擦点并提供了未来工作建议

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) learn statistical associations from massive training corpora and user interactions, and deployed systems can surface or infer information about individuals. Yet people lack practical ways to inspect what a model associates with their name. We report interim findings from an ongoing study and introduce LMP2, a browser-based self-audit tool. In two user studies ($N_{total}{=}458$), GPT-4o predicts 11 of 50 features for everyday people with $\ge$60\% accuracy, and participants report wanting control over LLM-generated associations despite not considering all outputs privacy violations. To validate our probing method, we evaluate eight LLMs on public figures and non-existent names, observing clear separation between stable name-conditioned associations and model defaults. Our findings also contribute to exposing a broader generative AI evaluation crisis: when outputs are probabilistic, context-dependent, and user-mediated through elicitation, what model--individual associations even include is under-specified and operationalisation relies on crafting probes and metrics that are hard to validate or compare. To move towards reliable, actionable human-centred LLM privacy audits, we identify nine frictions that emerged in our study and offer recommendations for future work and the design of human-centred LLM privacy audits.

</details>


### [124] [UniMotion: Self-Supervised Learning for Cross-Domain IMU Motion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12218)
*Prerna Khanna,Tanmay Srivastava,Shubham Jain,Aruna Balasubramanian*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: UniMotion是一个通用的IMU手势识别框架，通过两阶段方法（无标签活动数据预训练+少量标签数据微调）实现跨设备和用户群体的手势识别，仅需10%标签数据即可达到85%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于IMU的手势识别算法通常针对特定设备（如智能手表vs耳机）或特定用户群体（如盲人vs视力正常者）进行优化，缺乏通用性。同时，收集大规模标注训练数据成本高昂，限制了手势识别系统的广泛应用。

Method: UniMotion采用两阶段框架：1) 使用丰富的无标签人类活动数据预训练运动表示模型，采用基于token的策略和嵌入来识别和关注时间数据中的关键运动特征；2) 使用少量标注手势数据进行微调，设计文本引导分类器来可靠地区分时间或语义上相似的手势。

Result: 在智能手表（手部手势）和耳机（耳机手势）两种设备上，使用盲人和视力正常用户的数据进行评估。UniMotion在平均13个手势类别上达到85%的准确率，仅使用10%的标注数据进行训练。显著优于最先进的自监督学习方法和专门的手势识别模型。

Conclusion: UniMotion证明了通过利用无标签活动数据和少量标注数据，可以构建通用的跨设备和用户群体的IMU手势识别框架。该方法解决了数据稀缺和泛化性问题，为可访问和直观的手势界面提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: IMU-based gesture interfaces are being increasingly adopted as efficient, accessible, and intuitive alternatives to traditional input methods, such as touchscreens and voice. However, current gesture recognition algorithms are tailored to work for specific devices (e.g., smartwatches vs. earbuds) or user populations (e.g., blind vs. sighted users), limiting their generalizability. In this paper, we design UniMotion, a generalized IMU-based gesture recognition framework that works across devices and populations with minimal training samples. To overcome the challenges and high cost of collecting large-scale labeled training data, UniMotion leverages readily available unlabeled human activity data. The UniMotion pipeline comprises two stages: (1) pre-training a motion representation model using abundant unlabeled human activity data, and (2) fine-tuning it with a small amount of labeled gesture data. For pre-training, we introduce a token-based strategy and embeddings that learn to identify and focus attention on the key motion signatures in the temporal data For fine-tuning, we design a text-guided classifier that can reliably differentiate between temporally or semantically similar gestures. We evaluate UniMotion across both hand gestures (captured through a smartwatch) and earbud gestures (captured through earbuds), using data collected from blind and sighted users. Across these diverse devices and user populations, UniMotion achieves an accuracy of 85\%, across an average of 13 gesture classes using only 10\% of labeled data for training. UniMotion significantly outperforms state-of-the-art self-supervised learning approaches and specialized gesture recognition models.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Radio Radiance Field: The New Frontier of Spatial Wireless Channel Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11588)
*Haijian Sun,Feng Ye*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 论文提出"无线电辐射场(RRF)"概念，用于捕获无线传播的空间分布和方向性，从而推导出全面的空间信道状态信息(Spatial-CSI)，为大规模MIMO和智能反射表面等下一代无线系统提供更精确的信道表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模MIMO系统中的信道估计方法存在局限性，主要关注聚合信道增益和延迟，缺乏完整的空间特性表征（包括极化信息），限制了高级大规模MIMO技术的有效性。需要一种能够全面捕获无线传播空间特性的新方法。

Method: 提出无线电辐射场(RRF)概念，该概念捕获无线电传播的空间分布和方向性。从RRF可以推导出称为Spatial-CSI的全面空间无线信道表示。RRF包含完整的几何和无线电信息，可直接用于波束赋形、延迟对齐调制等技术，并可作为数字无线电孪生体。

Result: RRF提供了一种全面的空间信道表征方法，能够捕获传统方法缺失的空间特性（包括极化信息）。它可直接应用于大规模MIMO和智能反射表面的各种技术实现，并为无线系统实时仿真和优化提供数字孪生基础。

Conclusion: 无线电辐射场(RRF)概念为下一代无线通信系统提供了一种全面的空间信道表征方法，克服了现有信道估计技术的局限性，为从通信到感知的各种应用铺平了道路，有望显著提升大规模MIMO系统的性能。

Abstract: Massive MIMO, among other ground-breaking technologies, is being developed for the next-generation wireless systems to support requirements in terms of data rates, reliability, latency, intelligence, security and energy efficiency. Accurate channel estimation remains a key challenge in fully exploiting massive MIMO. While recent research has explored aspects such as near-field effects, spatial non-stationarity, and channel sparsity, many practical estimation and modeling techniques still provide limited CSI, often dominated by aggregate channel gain and delay, without full spatial characteristics. Although wideband models and phased-array techniques can capture delay and angular information, many practical estimation methods still lack comprehensive spatial resolution, including polarization, which limits their effectiveness for advanced massive MIMO techniques. This article introduces the concept of radio radiance field (RRF), which captures the spatial distribution and directionality of radio propagation. From RRF, a comprehensive spatial representation of the wireless channel, referred to as Spatial-CSI, can be derived. Owing to the comprehensive geometric and radio information, RRF can be implemented directly for beamforming, delay-alignment modulation, and many other techniques in massive MIMO and reflective intelligent surface implementations. An RRF can also serve as a digital radio twin, which is a virtual representation of the radio environment that includes both geometric structure and radio propagation characteristics, enabling real-time simulation and optimization of wireless systems. It paves the way for various applications from communications to sensing in the next-generation wireless communication systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [DRAFTO: Decoupled Reduced-space and Adaptive Feasibility-repair Trajectory Optimization for Robotic Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11074)
*Yichang Feng,Xiao Liang,Minghui Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DRAFTO是一种新的轨迹优化算法，通过解耦降维空间优化和自适应可行性修复，在保证约束满足的同时提高计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹优化方法在处理关节限制等约束时，往往需要进行大量重复的约束优化，计算效率较低。需要一种既能高效处理约束又能保证全局收敛性的新方法。

Method: 首先构建包含平滑性、安全性、关节限制和任务要求的约束目标函数。将轨迹参数化为基函数集的系数进行优化。采用解耦策略：主迭代使用降维空间高斯牛顿下降，初始化和终端可行性修复使用约束二次规划。采用两阶段接受规则和非单调策略，使用铰链平方惩罚处理不等式约束以确保全局收敛性。

Result: 基准测试显示DRAFTO在效率、可靠性和成功率方面优于CHOMP、TrajOpt、GPMP2、FACTO等优化型规划器以及RRT-Connect、RRT*、PRM等采样型规划器。在从抽屉抓取物体的实验中展示了在复杂操作任务中的实施潜力。

Conclusion: DRAFTO通过解耦优化策略有效平衡了计算效率和约束满足，为复杂操作任务提供了高效可靠的轨迹优化解决方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces a new algorithm for trajectory optimization, Decoupled Reduced-space and Adaptive Feasibility-repair Trajectory Optimization (DRAFTO). It first constructs a constrained objective that accounts for smoothness, safety, joint limits, and task requirements. Then, it optimizes the coefficients, which are the coordinates of a set of basis functions for trajectory parameterization. To reduce the number of repeated constrained optimizations while handling joint-limit feasibility, the optimization is decoupled into a reduced-space Gauss-Newton (GN) descent for the main iterations and constrained quadratic programming for initialization and terminal feasibility repair. The two-phase acceptance rule with a non-monotone policy is applied to the GN model, which uses a hinge-squared penalty for inequality constraints, to ensure globalizability. The results of our benchmark tests against optimization-based planners, such as CHOMP, TrajOpt, GPMP2, and FACTO, and sampling-based planners, such as RRT-Connect, RRT*, and PRM, validate the high efficiency and reliability across diverse scenarios and tasks. The experiment involving grabbing an object from a drawer further demonstrates the potential for implementation in complex manipulation tasks. The supplemental video is available at https://youtu.be/XisFI37YyTQ.

</details>


### [127] [OA-NBV: Occlusion-Aware Next-Best-View Planning for Human-Centered Active Perception on Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11072)
*Boxun Hu,Chang Chang,Jiawei Ge,Man Namgung,Xiaomin Lin,Axel Krieger,Tinoosh Mohsenin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OA-NBV是一种用于移动机器人的遮挡感知最佳视点规划方法，专门针对部分遮挡的人类目标，通过选择可行机器人位姿来获得更完整的人类观察视图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人类中心操作（如搜索、分类和灾难响应）中，杂乱环境和部分可见性经常降低下游感知性能。现有NBV方法主要优化通用探索或长时程覆盖，未明确针对在真实运动约束下获取部分遮挡人类的单一可用观察这一即时目标。

Method: OA-NBV集成了感知和运动规划，通过目标中心可见性模型对候选视点进行评分，该模型考虑了遮挡、目标尺度和目标完整性，同时将候选视点限制在可行的机器人位姿。

Result: OA-NBV在仿真和真实世界试验中均达到超过90%的成功率，而基线NBV方法在遮挡条件下性能急剧下降。此外，OA-NBV提高了观察质量：与最强基线相比，在不同设置下将归一化目标面积至少增加81%，关键点可见性至少提高58%。

Conclusion: OA-NBV是一种即插即用的视点选择模块，适用于多样化的人类中心下游任务，能够有效解决部分遮挡人类目标的观察问题。

Abstract: We naturally step sideways or lean to see around the obstacle when our view is blocked, and recover a more informative observation. Enabling robots to make the same kind of viewpoint choice is critical for human-centered operations, including search, triage, and disaster response, where cluttered environments and partial visibility frequently degrade downstream perception. However, many Next-Best-View (NBV) methods primarily optimize generic exploration or long-horizon coverage, and do not explicitly target the immediate goal of obtaining a single usable observation of a partially occluded person under real motion constraints. We present Occlusion-Aware Next-Best-View Planning for Human-Centered Active Perception on Mobile Robots (OA-NBV), an occlusion-aware NBV pipeline that autonomously selects the next traversable viewpoint to obtain a more complete view of an occluded human. OA-NBV integrates perception and motion planning by scoring candidate viewpoints using a target-centric visibility model that accounts for occlusion, target scale, and target completeness, while restricting candidates to feasible robot poses. OA-NBV achieves over 90% success rate in both simulation and real-world trials, while baseline NBV methods degrade sharply under occlusion. Beyond success rate, OA-NBV improves observation quality: compared to the strongest baseline, it increases normalized target area by at least 81% and keypoint visibility by at least 58% across settings, making it a drop-in view-selection module for diverse human-centered downstream tasks.

</details>


### [128] [Flight through Narrow Gaps with Morphing-Wing Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12059)
*Julius Wanner,Hoang-Vu Phan,Charbel Toumieh,Dario Floreano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种可变翼展无人机，通过飞行中临时收拢机翼来穿越小于翼展宽度的狭窄缝隙，解决了低空速穿越时升力突然损失和精确时机控制的技术挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定翼无人机穿越狭窄缝隙的能力受限于其翼展宽度，无法通过小于翼展的缝隙。受鸟类飞行启发，需要开发能够临时收拢机翼以减小翼展的变形翼无人机，实现亚翼展宽度缝隙的穿越。

Method: 1) 开发了包含低飞行速度和失速后攻角的一般翼展变形无人机气动模型；2) 将纵向无人机动力学集成到具有运行时自适应成本和约束的最优参考轨迹生成和非线性模型预测控制框架中；3) 在130克翼展变形无人机上进行验证。

Result: 在5-7 m/s的前飞速度下，穿越狭窄缝隙时的平均高度误差为5厘米，能够在可变阈值距离内强制机翼在缝隙附近完全收拢。轨迹分析显示无人机可以通过在缝隙前加速和上仰来补偿穿越时的升力损失，补偿程度因参考轨迹优化目标而异。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了变形翼无人机穿越亚翼展宽度缝隙的能力，在保持恒定前飞速度参考和接近恒定高度的同时，能够在硬件上实现精确的缝隙穿越，为无人机在复杂环境中的导航提供了新方案。

Abstract: The size of a narrow gap traversable by a fixed-wing drone is limited by its wingspan. Inspired by birds, here, we enable the traversal of a gap of sub-wingspan width and height using a morphing-wing drone capable of temporarily sweeping in its wings mid-flight. This maneuver poses control challenges due to sudden lift loss during gap-passage at low flight speeds and the need for precisely timed wing-sweep actuation ahead of the gap. To address these challenges, we first develop an aerodynamic model for general wing-sweep morphing drone flight including low flight speeds and post-stall angles of attack. We integrate longitudinal drone dynamics into an optimal reference trajectory generation and Nonlinear Model Predictive Control framework with runtime adaptive costs and constraints. Validated on a 130 g wing-sweep-morphing drone, our method achieves an average altitude error of 5 cm during narrow-gap passage at forward speeds between 5 and 7 m/s, whilst enforcing fully swept wings near the gap across variable threshold distances. Trajectory analysis shows that the drone can compensate for lift loss during gap-passage by accelerating and pitching upwards ahead of the gap to an extent that differs between reference trajectory optimization objectives. We show that our strategy also allows for accurate gap passage on hardware whilst maintaining a constant forward flight speed reference and near-constant altitude.

</details>


### [129] [Edge-Assisted Multi-Robot Visual-Inertial SLAM with Efficient Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11085)
*Xin Liu,Shuhuan Wen,Jing Zhao,Tony Z. Qiu,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于机器人-边缘-云分层架构的集中式多机器人SLAM系统，通过光流跟踪和金字塔IMU预测降低计算成本，实现有限带宽下的实时协同SLAM


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云边协同是多机器人SLAM实现全局一致性和实时性的有效途径，但终端设备与云之间的有限带宽和长通信链路会导致性能下降，需要解决计算资源有限和执行效率低的问题

Method: 1) 提出基于金字塔IMU预测的光流跟踪轻量化SLAM方法；2) 构建机器人-边缘-云分层架构的集中式多机器人SLAM系统；3) 仅传输特征点和关键帧描述子，并进行无损编码压缩

Result: 在EuRoC数据集上验证：相比当前最先进的局部特征压缩方法，实现更低数据量的特征传输；相比先进集中式多机器人SLAM方案，在低计算负载下达到相同或更好的定位精度

Conclusion: 提出的分层架构和轻量化方法有效解决了多机器人SLAM中的带宽限制和计算资源约束问题，实现了实时协同SLAM而不损失精度

Abstract: The integration of cloud computing and edge computing is an effective way to achieve global consistent and real-time multi-robot Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM). Cloud computing effectively solves the problem of limited computing, communication and storage capacity of terminal equipment. However, limited bandwidth and extremely long communication links between terminal devices and the cloud result in serious performance degradation of multi-robot SLAM systems. To reduce the computational cost of feature tracking and improve the real-time performance of the robot, a lightweight SLAM method of optical flow tracking based on pyramid IMU prediction is proposed. On this basis, a centralized multi-robot SLAM system based on a robot-edge-cloud layered architecture is proposed to realize real-time collaborative SLAM. It avoids the problems of limited on-board computing resources and low execution efficiency of single robot. In this framework, only the feature points and keyframe descriptors are transmitted and lossless encoding and compression are carried out to realize real-time remote information transmission with limited bandwidth resources. This design reduces the actual bandwidth occupied in the process of data transmission, and does not cause the loss of SLAM accuracy caused by data compression. Through experimental verification on the EuRoC dataset, compared with the current most advanced local feature compression method, our method can achieve lower data volume feature transmission, and compared with the current advanced centralized multi-robot SLAM scheme, it can achieve the same or better positioning accuracy under low computational load.

</details>


### [130] [Decentralized Cooperative Localization for Multi-Robot Systems with Asynchronous Sensor Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12075)
*Nivand Khosravi,Niusha Khosravi,Mohammad Bozorg,Masoud S. Bahraini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种去中心化协同定位框架，用于GPS拒止环境中的非完整移动机器人，通过扩展卡尔曼滤波、异步传感器数据处理、自动坐标系对齐和双路标评估机制，在仿真和真实环境中相比集中式方法显著提升了定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GPS拒止环境（如封闭空间、水下、特征稀疏地形）中，传统定位方法失效，需要可靠的协同定位方案。现有方法通常需要预对齐坐标系或面临特征稀疏环境下的鲁棒性问题，因此需要开发一种能够处理异步传感器数据、自动坐标系对齐并在特征稀疏环境下保持鲁棒性的去中心化协同定位框架。

Method: 1. 采用去中心化架构，每个机器人本地运行扩展卡尔曼滤波器进行定位；2. 仅在通信链路可用且通过LiDAR成功检测到同伴机器人时共享测量信息；3. 处理异步传感器数据，支持异构采样率，适应动态机动中的加速度；4. 允许机器人以任意参考系方向初始化，通过预测和更新阶段的变换矩阵实现自动坐标系对齐；5. 引入双路标评估框架，同时利用静态环境特征和移动机器人作为动态路标；6. 在急转弯时实现可靠检测和特征提取，通过相互观测的信息共享提高预测精度。

Result: 1. 在Gazebo仿真和真实地下室环境中验证了框架有效性；2. 去中心化协同定位相比集中式协同定位实现了34%的RMSE降低；3. 双路标变体进一步实现了56%的改进；4. 证明了框架在封闭空间、水下环境和特征稀疏地形等挑战性领域的适用性。

Conclusion: 提出的去中心化协同定位框架通过本地扩展卡尔曼滤波、自动坐标系对齐和双路标评估机制，在GPS拒止环境中实现了优于集中式方法的定位性能。该框架能够处理异步传感器数据、适应动态机动，并在特征稀疏环境下保持鲁棒性，为封闭空间、水下环境和特征稀疏地形等挑战性应用提供了有效的定位解决方案。

Abstract: Decentralized cooperative localization (DCL) is a promising approach for nonholonomic mobile robots operating in GPS-denied environments with limited communication infrastructure. This paper presents a DCL framework in which each robot performs localization locally using an Extended Kalman Filter, while sharing measurement information during update stages only when communication links are available and companion robots are successfully detected by LiDAR. The framework preserves cross-correlation consistency among robot state estimates while handling asynchronous sensor data with heterogeneous sampling rates and accommodating accelerations during dynamic maneuvers. Unlike methods that require pre-aligned coordinate systems, the proposed approach allows robots to initialize with arbitrary reference-frame orientations and achieves automatic alignment through transformation matrices in both the prediction and update stages. To improve robustness in feature-sparse environments, we introduce a dual-landmark evaluation framework that exploits both static environmental features and mobile robots as dynamic landmarks. The proposed framework enables reliable detection and feature extraction during sharp turns, while prediction accuracy is improved through information sharing from mutual observations. Experimental results in both Gazebo simulation and real-world basement environments show that DCL outperforms centralized cooperative localization (CCL), achieving a 34% reduction in RMSE, while the dual-landmark variant yields an improvement of 56%. These results demonstrate the applicability of DCL to challenging domains such as enclosed spaces, underwater environments, and feature-sparse terrains where conventional localization methods are ineffective.

</details>


### [131] [ResWM: Residual-Action World Model for Visual RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11110)
*Jseen Zhang,Gabriel Adineera,Jinzhou Tan,Jinoh Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ResWM提出了一种基于残差动作的世界模型框架，将控制变量从绝对动作改为相对于前一步的增量调整，从而稳定长时程规划并提升控制平滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于模型的强化学习框架直接基于绝对动作进行未来预测，导致优化不稳定：最优动作分布是任务依赖的、先验未知的，常导致振荡或低效控制。需要一种更符合真实世界控制平滑性的框架。

Method: 提出残差动作世界模型(ResWM)，将控制变量从绝对动作改为残差动作（相对于前一步的增量调整）。引入观测差异编码器显式建模相邻帧之间的变化，生成与残差动作自然耦合的紧凑潜在动态。在Dreamer风格的潜在动态模型基础上进行最小修改，无需额外超参数。

Result: 在DeepMind Control Suite上的实验表明，ResWM在样本效率、渐进回报和控制平滑性方面均取得一致改进，显著超越Dreamer和TD-MPC等强基线。生成更稳定、能量效率更高的动作轨迹。

Conclusion: 残差动作建模为连接RL算法进展与机器人实际需求提供了一个简单而强大的原则，特别适用于需要稳定和能量高效控制的实际机器人系统部署。

Abstract: Learning predictive world models from raw visual observations is a central challenge in reinforcement learning (RL), especially for robotics and continuous control. Conventional model-based RL frameworks directly condition future predictions on absolute actions, which makes optimization unstable: the optimal action distributions are task-dependent, unknown a priori, and often lead to oscillatory or inefficient control. To address this, we introduce the Residual-Action World Model (ResWM), a new framework that reformulates the control variable from absolute actions to residual actions -- incremental adjustments relative to the previous step. This design aligns with the inherent smoothness of real-world control, reduces the effective search space, and stabilizes long-horizon planning. To further strengthen the representation, we propose an Observation Difference Encoder that explicitly models the changes between adjacent frames, yielding compact latent dynamics that are naturally coupled with residual actions. ResWM is integrated into a Dreamer-style latent dynamics model with minimal modifications and no extra hyperparameters. Both imagination rollouts and policy optimization are conducted in the residual-action space, enabling smoother exploration, lower control variance, and more reliable planning. Empirical results on the DeepMind Control Suite demonstrate that ResWM achieves consistent improvements in sample efficiency, asymptotic returns, and control smoothness, significantly surpassing strong baselines such as Dreamer and TD-MPC. Beyond performance, ResWM produces more stable and energy-efficient action trajectories, a property critical for robotic systems deployed in real-world environments. These findings suggest that residual action modeling provides a simple yet powerful principle for bridging algorithmic advances in RL with the practical requirements of robotics.

</details>


### [132] [Robust Co-design Optimisation for Agile Fixed-Wing UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11130)
*Adrian Andrei Buda,Xavier Chen,Nicolò Botteghi,Urban Fasel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用于敏捷固定翼无人机的鲁棒协同设计框架，通过双层优化将参数不确定性和风扰动纳入协同优化过程，在三个敏捷飞行任务中优于确定性基线


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主系统协同设计框架往往忽略非结构化真实环境所需的鲁棒性，导致敏捷无人机设计对扰动和模型失配敏感，需要将不确定性和扰动直接纳入优化过程

Method: 采用双层方法：高层循环优化物理设计，通过约束轨迹规划器发现标称解，使用反馈LQR控制通过随机蒙特卡洛集合评估性能，将参数不确定性和风扰动整合到协同优化中

Result: 在三个敏捷飞行任务中验证，鲁棒协同设计策略始终优于确定性基线，能够自动调整机翼位置和展弦比等气动特征，实现任务性能与扰动抑制的最佳权衡

Conclusion: 提出的鲁棒协同设计框架能够有效处理敏捷固定翼无人机在非结构化环境中的不确定性和扰动，通过联合优化物理设计和控制策略实现鲁棒性能提升

Abstract: Co-design optimisation of autonomous systems has emerged as a powerful alternative to sequential approaches by jointly optimising physical design and control strategies. However, existing frameworks often neglect the robustness required for autonomous systems navigating unstructured, real-world environments. For agile Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) operating at the edge of the flight envelope, this lack of robustness yields designs that are sensitive to perturbations and model mismatch. To address this, we propose a robust co-design framework for agile fixed-wing UAVs that integrates parametric uncertainty and wind disturbances directly into the concurrent optimisation process. Our bi-level approach optimises physical design in a high-level loop while discovering nominal solutions via a constrained trajectory planner and evaluating performance across a stochastic Monte Carlo ensemble using feedback LQR control. Validated across three agile flight missions, our strategy consistently outperforms deterministic baselines. The results demonstrate that our robust co-design strategy inherently tailors aerodynamic features, such as wing placement and aspect ratio, to achieve an optimal trade-off between mission performance and disturbance rejection.

</details>


### [133] [ADMM-based Continuous Trajectory Optimization in Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11335)
*Lukas Pries,Jon Arrizabalaga,Zachary Manchester,Markus Ryll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于ADMM的数值求解器，用于非凸环境中连续轨迹计算，通过多项式参数化和时空分配图实现联合优化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非凸环境中连续轨迹计算问题，克服现有解耦方法搜索空间有限和复杂预热初始化瓶颈

Method: 定制化ADMM实现：1) 多项式参数化轨迹，原始更新可闭式求解为最小控制力问题；2) 基于混合整数规划的时空分配图，松弛更新转化为最短路径搜索

Result: 相比现有技术具有明显优势：联合优化离散空间和连续时间域，访问更大搜索空间；结构鲁棒性确保从简单初始化可靠收敛

Conclusion: 该方法通过多项式参数化和时空分配图的ADMM实现，有效解决了非凸环境中的轨迹优化问题，克服了现有方法的局限性

Abstract: This paper presents a numerical solver for computing continuous trajectories in non-convex environments. Our approach relies on a customized implementation of the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) built upon two key components: First, we parameterize trajectories as polynomials, allowing the primal update to be computed in closed form as a minimum-control-effort problem. Second, we introduce the concept of a spatio-temporal allocation graph based on a mixed-integer formulation and pose the slack update as a shortest-path search. The combination of these ingredients results in a solver with several distinct advantages over the state of the art. By jointly optimizing over both discrete spatial and continuous temporal domains, our method accesses a larger search space than existing decoupled approaches, enabling the discovery of superior trajectories. Additionally, the solver's structural robustness ensures reliable convergence from naive initializations, removing the bottleneck of complex warm starting in non-convex environments.

</details>


### [134] [Novelty Adaptation Through Hybrid Large Language Model (LLM)-Symbolic Planning and LLM-guided Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11351)
*Hong Lu,Pierrick Lorang,Timothy R. Duggan,Jivko Sinapov,Matthias Scheutz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种神经符号架构，结合符号规划、强化学习和大语言模型来处理动态开放世界中的新颖对象


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态开放世界环境中，自主智能体常遇到新颖对象，导致传统符号规划器因缺乏相应操作符而无法生成计划。需要解决机器人规划域中缺少与新颖对象交互的操作符的问题。

Method: 提出神经符号架构，整合符号规划、强化学习和LLM。利用LLM的常识推理能力识别缺失操作符，用符号AI规划器生成计划，并编写奖励函数指导强化学习智能体学习新识别操作符的控制策略。

Result: 该方法在操作符发现和连续机器人域中的操作符学习方面优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 神经符号架构通过整合LLM的常识推理、符号规划和强化学习，有效解决了动态开放世界中处理新颖对象的问题，在操作符发现和学习方面表现出优越性能。

Abstract: In dynamic open-world environments, autonomous agents often encounter novelties that hinder their ability to find plans to achieve their goals. Specifically, traditional symbolic planners fail to generate plans when the robot's planning domain lacks the operators that enable it to interact appropriately with novel objects in the environment. We propose a neuro-symbolic architecture that integrates symbolic planning, reinforcement learning, and a large language model (LLM) to learn how to handle novel objects. In particular, we leverage the common sense reasoning capability of the LLM to identify missing operators, generate plans with the symbolic AI planner, and write reward functions to guide the reinforcement learning agent in learning control policies for newly identified operators. Our method outperforms the state-of-the-art methods in operator discovery as well as operator learning in continuous robotic domains.

</details>


### [135] [MirrorDrift: Actuated Mirror-Based Attacks on LiDAR SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11364)
*Rokuto Nagata,Kenji Koide,Kazuma Ikeda,Ozora Sako,Shion Horie,Kentaro Yoshioka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MirrorDrift攻击利用镜面反射在LiDAR扫描中产生鬼点，通过优化镜面放置、对准和驱动来系统性地偏置扫描匹配对应关系，成功攻击了具有先进干扰缓解功能的生产级安全LiDAR系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LiDAR SLAM攻击主要依赖信号注入欺骗，需要传感器特定的时序知识且易被现代防御机制（如时序混淆和注入拒绝）缓解。本研究探索基于镜面反射的无注入替代方案，以攻击具有先进安全防护的LiDAR系统。

Method: 提出MirrorDrift攻击方法，使用驱动平面镜产生LiDAR扫描中的鬼点，系统性地偏置扫描匹配对应关系。通过优化镜面放置、对准和驱动参数来最大化定位误差。

Result: 仿真中攻击使平均姿态误差增加6.1倍，三个SLAM系统的平均APE达到2.29-3.31米。在实际实验中，对具有先进干扰缓解功能的现代LiDAR诱导了高达6.03米的定位误差。

Conclusion: 镜面反射提供了一种无需信号注入的有效攻击途径，成功突破了生产级安全LiDAR的防御，这是首个针对生产级安全LiDAR的SLAM定向攻击。

Abstract: LiDAR SLAM provides high-accuracy localization but is fragile to point-cloud corruption because scan matching assumes geometric consistency. Prior physical attacks on LiDAR SLAM largely rely on LiDAR spoofing via external signal injection, which requires sensor-specific timing knowledge and is increasingly mitigated by modern defense mechanisms such as timing obfuscation and injection rejection. In this work, we show that specular reflection offers an injection-free alternative and demonstrate an attack, MirrorDrift, that uses an actuated planar mirror to cause ghost points in LiDAR scans and systematically bias scan-matching correspondences. MirrorDrift optimizes mirror placement, alignment, and actuation. In simulation, it increases the average pose error (APE) by 6.1x over random placement, degrading three SLAM systems to 2.29-3.31 m mean APE. In real-world experiments on a modern LiDAR with state-of-the-art interference mitigation, it induces localization errors of up to 6.03 m. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first successful SLAM-targeted attack against production-grade secure LiDARs.

</details>


### [136] [D-SLAMSpoof: An Environment-Agnostic LiDAR Spoofing Attack using Dynamic Point Cloud Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11365)
*Rokuto Nagata,Kenji Koide,Kazuma Ikeda,Ozora Sako,Kentaro Yoshioka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: D-SLAMSpoof是一种针对LiDAR SLAM的新型攻击方法，通过激光干扰注入虚假测量，即使在特征丰富的环境中也能成功攻击；同时提出了ISD-SLAM防御方法，仅使用惯性航位推算信号检测和缓解此类攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LiDAR欺骗攻击在特征丰富的环境（如城市区域和室内空间）中往往失败，因此需要开发更有效的攻击方法来暴露LiDAR SLAM的固有漏洞，同时需要实用的防御方法来提高自主系统的安全性和可靠性。

Method: D-SLAMSpoof攻击通过外部激光干扰向LiDAR扫描注入虚假测量，设计了基于扫描匹配原理的空间注入形状和时间协调的动态注入模式。防御方法ISD-SLAM仅利用自主系统中常见的惯性航位推算信号来检测LiDAR欺骗攻击并缓解位置漂移。

Result: D-SLAMSpoof在真实世界特征丰富环境中显著提高了攻击成功率，而ISD-SLAM能够准确检测包括D-SLAMSpoof在内的LiDAR欺骗攻击，并有效缓解由此产生的位置漂移。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了LiDAR SLAM的固有漏洞，并首次提出了仅使用标准车载传感器的实用防御方法，为改善自主系统的安全性和可靠性提供了关键见解。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce Dynamic SLAMSpoof (D-SLAMSpoof), a novel attack that compromises LiDAR SLAM even in feature-rich environments. The attack leverages LiDAR spoofing, which injects spurious measurements into LiDAR scans through external laser interference. By designing both spatial injection shapes and temporally coordinated dynamic injection patterns guided by scan-matching principles, D-SLAMSpoof significantly improves attack success rates in real-world, feature-rich environments such as urban areas and indoor spaces, where conventional LiDAR spoofing methods often fail. Furthermore, we propose a practical defense method, ISD-SLAM, that relies solely on inertial dead reckoning signals commonly available in autonomous systems. We demonstrate that ISD-SLAM accurately detects LiDAR spoofing attacks, including D-SLAMSpoof, and effectively mitigates the resulting position drift. Our findings expose inherent vulnerabilities in LiDAR-based SLAM and introduce the first practical defense against LiDAR-based SLAM spoofing using only standard onboard sensors, providing critical insights for improving the security and reliability of autonomous systems.

</details>


### [137] [Vision-Based Hand Shadowing for Robotic Manipulation via Inverse Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11383)
*Hendrik Chiche,Antoine Jamme,Trevor Rigoberto Martinez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于单目RGB-D相机的离线手影重定向管道，用于低成本机械臂遥操作，通过手部关键点检测、3D重建、逆运动学求解生成关节指令，在结构化任务中达到90%成功率，但在非结构化环境中因遮挡降至9.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本机械臂遥操作面临的主要挑战是如何将复杂的人手关节运动映射到机器人关节指令。现有方法需要复杂的硬件设置或数据驱动的策略，本文旨在开发基于单目RGB-D相机的无标记分析式重定向方案。

Method: 使用安装在3D打印眼镜上的单目RGB-D相机采集数据，通过MediaPipe Hands检测每只手21个关键点，结合深度信息反投影到3D空间，转换到机器人坐标系，在PyBullet中使用阻尼最小二乘法求解逆运动学生成6自由度SO-ARM101机器人的关节指令。夹爪控制器根据拇指-食指几何关系映射到抓取开度，采用四级后备机制。动作先在物理仿真中预览，再通过LeRobot框架在真实机器人上回放。

Result: 在结构化拾放基准测试（5格网格，每格10次抓取）中达到90%成功率。与四种基于视觉-语言-动作策略（ACT、SmolVLA、pi0.5、GR00T N1.5）相比，展示了分析式方法的优势。在非结构化真实环境（杂货店、药店）测试中，由于周围物体导致的手部遮挡，成功率降至9.3%（N=75）。

Conclusion: 基于单目RGB-D相机的无标记分析式重定向管道在结构化任务中表现优异，但在非结构化环境中因遮挡问题成功率显著下降，显示了该方法的潜力和当前局限性。未来需要改进遮挡处理能力或结合数据驱动方法。

Abstract: Teleoperation of low-cost robotic manipulators remains challenging due to the complexity of mapping human hand articulations to robot joint commands. We present an offline hand-shadowing and retargeting pipeline from a single egocentric RGB-D camera mounted on 3D-printed glasses. The pipeline detects 21 hand landmarks per hand using MediaPipe Hands, deprojects them into 3D via depth sensing, transforms them into the robot coordinate frame, and solves a damped-least-squares inverse kinematics problem in PyBullet to produce joint commands for the 6-DOF SO-ARM101 robot. A gripper controller maps thumb-index finger geometry to grasp aperture with a four-level fallback hierarchy. Actions are first previewed in a physics simulation before replay on the physical robot through the LeRobot framework. We evaluate the IK retargeting pipeline on a structured pick-and-place benchmark (5-tile grid, 10 grasps per tile) achieving a 90% success rate, and compare it against four vision-language-action policies (ACT, SmolVLA, pi0.5, GR00T N1.5) trained on leader-follower teleoperation data. We also test the IK pipeline in unstructured real-world environments (grocery store, pharmacy), where hand occlusion by surrounding objects reduces success to 9.3% (N=75), highlighting both the promise and current limitations of marker-free analytical retargeting.

</details>


### [138] [Deployment-Time Reliability of Learned Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11400)
*Christopher Agia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出三种部署时机制来提高学习型机器人策略的可靠性：运行时监控、基于影响函数的数据中心可解释性框架、以及面向长时域任务执行的策略协调方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型机器人策略在实际部署中面临可靠性挑战，包括分布偏移、误差累积和复杂任务依赖等问题，这些因素共同削弱系统性能，阻碍了学习型策略在现实世界的实际应用。

Method: 提出了三类互补的部署时机制：1) 运行时监控方法，通过识别闭环策略行为的不一致性和任务进度偏差来检测即将发生的故障；2) 数据中心的可解释性框架，使用影响函数将部署时的成功和失败追溯到有影响的训练演示；3) 可靠的长时域任务执行方法，将策略协调表述为估计和最大化行为序列成功概率的问题，并扩展到语言指定任务的可行性感知任务规划。

Result: 这些机制共同为学习型机器人策略的可靠、实际使用提供了实用基础，能够在不依赖故障数据或任务特定监督的情况下检测故障，实现原则性的诊断和数据集管理，并支持开放式的语言指定任务执行。

Conclusion: 通过专注于部署的核心挑战，这些贡献为学习型机器人策略的可靠、实际使用推进了实用基础，这些基础的持续进步对于未来实现可信和可扩展的机器人自主性至关重要。

Abstract: Recent advances in learning-based robot manipulation have produced policies with remarkable capabilities. Yet, reliability at deployment remains a fundamental barrier to real-world use, where distribution shift, compounding errors, and complex task dependencies collectively undermine system performance. This dissertation investigates how the reliability of learned robot policies can be improved at deployment time through mechanisms that operate around them. We develop three complementary classes of deployment-time mechanisms. First, we introduce runtime monitoring methods that detect impending failures by identifying inconsistencies in closed-loop policy behavior and deviations in task progress, without requiring failure data or task-specific supervision. Second, we propose a data-centric framework for policy interpretability that traces deployment-time successes and failures to influential training demonstrations using influence functions, enabling principled diagnosis and dataset curation. Third, we address reliable long-horizon task execution by formulating policy coordination as the problem of estimating and maximizing the success probability of behavior sequences, and we extend this formulation to open-ended, language-specified tasks through feasibility-aware task planning. By centering on core challenges of deployment, these contributions advance practical foundations for the reliable, real-world use of learned robot policies. Continued progress on these foundations will be essential for enabling trustworthy and scalable robot autonomy in the future.

</details>


### [139] [Real-time Rendering-based Surgical Instrument Tracking via Evolutionary Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11404)
*Hanyang Hu,Zekai Liang,Florian Richter,Michael C. Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将CMA-ES进化优化策略集成到手术器械跟踪框架中，通过批量渲染并行评估多个位姿候选，实现高效准确的手术器械位姿和关节配置联合估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助微创手术中，手术器械的准确高效跟踪至关重要。现有视觉方法在视觉质量下降和数据稀缺时特征检测不可靠，而基于渲染的方法存在计算成本高和收敛性差的问题。

Method: 提出一个结合CMA-ES进化优化策略的通用跟踪框架，通过批量渲染并行评估多个位姿候选，联合估计手术器械的位姿和关节配置，支持无关节角度和双手操作设置。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在准确性和运行时间上显著优于现有方法，适用于视觉反馈控制和在线手术视频校准。

Conclusion: 提出的基于CMA-ES和批量渲染的跟踪框架为手术器械跟踪提供了高效准确的解决方案，能够处理部分遮挡和特殊关节设计带来的挑战，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Accurate and efficient tracking of surgical instruments is fundamental for Robot-Assisted Minimally Invasive Surgery. Although vision-based robot pose estimation has enabled markerless calibration without tedious physical setups, reliable tool tracking for surgical robots still remains challenging due to partial visibility and specialized articulation design of surgical instruments. Previous works in the field are usually prone to unreliable feature detections under degraded visual quality and data scarcity, whereas rendering-based methods often struggle with computational costs and suboptimal convergence. In this work, we incorporate CMA-ES, an evolutionary optimization strategy, into a versatile tracking pipeline that jointly estimates surgical instrument pose and joint configurations. Using batch rendering to efficiently evaluate multiple pose candidates in parallel, the method significantly reduces inference time and improves convergence robustness. The proposed framework further generalizes to joint angle-free and bi-manual tracking settings, making it suitable for both vision feedback control and online surgery video calibration. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms prior approaches in both accuracy and runtime.

</details>


### [140] [Grounding Robot Generalization in Training Data via Retrieval-Augmented VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11426)
*Jensen Gao,Dorsa Sadigh,Sandy Huang,Dhruv Shah*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RADAR是一个用于评估机器人策略泛化能力的可扩展框架，通过检索训练数据并使用视觉语言模型分析测试任务与训练数据的关系，从而精确评估泛化需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人操作领域的研究虽然提升了策略在新场景中的泛化能力，但难以准确评估不同测试设置如何代表从训练分布中的泛化。需要更精确的方法来评估机器人策略的泛化性能。

Method: RADAR采用两阶段流程：1) 使用通用策略嵌入进行检索，识别与给定测试任务相关的训练示例；2) 使用视觉语言模型分析测试任务与检索数据的关系，沿多个维度输出可解释的分析，并对所需泛化类型进行分类。

Result: 通过控制实验证明：1) VLMs能有效分析泛化数据；2) 检索步骤能准确识别分类所需的相关训练示例。在大规模数据集上，RADAR的结果与先前工作中人工定义的基准条件一致。

Conclusion: RADAR提供了一个可扩展的框架，能够精确评估机器人策略的泛化需求，通过结合检索和视觉语言模型分析，实现了对测试任务与训练数据关系的系统化评估。

Abstract: Recent work on robot manipulation has advanced policy generalization to novel scenarios. However, it is often difficult to characterize how different evaluation settings actually represent generalization from the training distribution of a given policy. To work towards more precise evaluation of generalization in robotics, we propose RADAR, a scalable framework for directly comparing test-time evaluation tasks to policy training data, to determine what form of policy generalization is required. RADAR consists of a two-stage pipeline: first, retrieval using generalist policy embeddings identifies which training examples are relevant for a given evaluation task. Next, vision-language models (VLMs) analyze the evaluation task against the retrieved data, outputting interpretable analysis on how they compare along a variety of axes, and an overall classification of what type of policy generalization is required. Through controlled experiments, we demonstrate that VLMs are effective at analyzing data for generalization, and that our retrieval step effectively identifies examples needed to make accurate classifications with respect to the training data. Furthermore, we scale RADAR to large-scale datasets, where we observe agreement with human-defined benchmark conditions from prior work. We provide demonstrations at radar-analysis.github.io.

</details>


### [141] [A Generalized Theory of Load Distribution in Redundantly-actuated Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11431)
*Joshua Flight,Clément Gosselin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种广义理论，用于描述冗余驱动机器人系统中刚性体的载荷分布，解决了多指抓取器、腿式机器人等过约束机构的力控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前冗余驱动机器人系统（如多指抓取器、腿式机器人、协作机器人）在处理刚性体时缺乏统一的载荷分布理论，现有方法存在计算效率低、需要数值方法和大矩阵求逆等问题，需要更高效的理论框架。

Method: 建立了广义理论框架，完全描述了给定合力作用下刚性体的可行操纵力分布集合，推导了力合成和力分析问题的显式解，算法计算效率高且与施加力数量呈线性关系。

Result: 理论能够完全表征刚性体的可行力分布集合，提出的显式解无需数值方法或大矩阵求逆，计算复杂度线性增长，同时指出了现有方法的不足并提出了改进方案。

Conclusion: 该理论为冗余驱动机器人系统的力控制提供了统一框架，显式解显著提高了计算效率，改进方法在多种过约束机构中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: This paper presents a generalized theory which describes how applied loads are distributed within rigid bodies handled by redundantly-actuated robotic systems composed of multiple independent closed-loop kinematic chains. The theory fully characterizes the feasible set of manipulating wrench distributions for a given resultant wrench applied to the rigid body and has important implications for the force-control of multifingered grippers, legged robots, cooperating robots, and other overconstrained mechanisms. We also derive explicit solutions to the wrench synthesis and wrench analysis problems. These solutions are computationally efficient and scale linearly with the number of applied wrenches, requiring neither numerical methods nor the inversion of large matrices. Finally, we identify significant shortcomings in current state-of-the-art approaches and propose corrections. These are supported by illustrative examples that demonstrate the advantages of the improved methods.

</details>


### [142] [Enhancing Lightweight Vision Language Models through Group Competitive Learning for Socially Compliant Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11447)
*Xinyu Zhang,Atsushi Konno,Toshihiko Yamasaki,Ling Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Group Competitive Learning (GCL)策略，通过Group Competitive Objective (GCO)和Asymmetric Group Optimization (AGO)增强轻量级视觉语言模型的社会导航能力，在保持高效推理的同时显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会机器人导航需要结合场景语义和人类社交规范。大型视觉语言模型(VLMs)推理能力强但计算开销大，不适合实时部署；轻量级VLMs推理高效但性能较弱。需要同时实现强推理能力和高效率的解决方案。

Method: 提出Group Competitive Learning (GCL)策略，包含两个核心组件：1) Group Competitive Objective (GCO)：协调全局语义与分布正则化；2) Asymmetric Group Optimization (AGO)：探索模型性能上限。该方法旨在增强轻量级VLMs的能力。

Result: 在社会导航基准测试中，GCL显著提升VLM性能：Qwen2.5-VL-3B模型F1分数达到0.968，Qwen3-VL-4B达到0.914，分别比普通监督微调提升40%和12%。特别地，3B模型通过GCL超越了8B基线模型28%。

Conclusion: GCL为在实际部署中同时实现高精度和计算效率提供了有效解决方案，证明了通过适当的学习策略可以显著增强轻量级模型的性能，使其超越更大模型。

Abstract: Social robot navigation requires a sophisticated integration of scene semantics and human social norms. Scaling up Vision Language Models (VLMs) generally improves reasoning and decision-making capabilities for socially compliant navigation. However, increased model size incurs substantial computational overhead, limiting suitability for real-time robotic deployment. Conversely, lightweight VLMs enable efficient inference but often exhibit weaker reasoning and decision-making performance in socially complex environments. Achieving both strong reasoning ability and efficiency remains an open challenge. To bridge this gap, we propose Group Competitive Learning (GCL), a strategy designed to amplify the capabilities of lightweight VLMs. Our strategy introduces the Group Competitive Objective (GCO) to harmonize global semantics with distributional regularization, alongside Asymmetric Group Optimization (AGO) to explore the upper limits of model performance. Empirical evaluations on social navigation benchmarks demonstrate that GCL significantly elevates VLM performance. Specifically, GCL enables the Qwen2.5-VL-3B learner model and guide Qwen3-VL-4B to achieve an F1 score of 0.968 and 0.914, representing 40\% and 12\% improvement over vanilla supervised fine-tuning (SFT). Notably, under vanilla SFT, the 3B model initially trails the 8B model (F1: 0.692 vs. 0.755). However, through the GCL, the 3B model outperforms (28\%) the 8B baseline model. These results suggest that GCL provides an effective solution for achieving both high accuracy and computational efficiency in real-world deployment.

</details>


### [143] [SPARK: Skeleton-Parameter Aligned Retargeting on Humanoid Robots with Kinodynamic Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11480)
*Hanwen Wang,Qiayuan Liao,Bike Zhang,Kunzhao Ren,Koushil Sreenath,Xiaobin Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两阶段流程：骨架校准和运动学动力学轨迹优化，将人类运动数据转化为机器人可用的高质量运动参考


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类运动数据为训练人形机器人控制策略提供丰富先验，但原始演示与机器人运动学和动力学不兼容，限制了直接使用

Method: 1. 骨架校准：将人类运动转换为URDF骨架表示并校准到目标人形机器人尺寸；2. 渐进式运动学动力学轨迹优化：分三阶段（运动学TO、逆动力学、完整运动学动力学TO），以前一阶段解为初始值

Result: 显著减少逆运动学误差和调参工作量，生成动态一致的状态轨迹和关节扭矩剖面，为学习型控制器提供高质量参考

Conclusion: 骨架校准和运动学动力学轨迹优化相结合，能够为不同人形机器人平台生成自然、物理一致的运动参考

Abstract: Human motion provides rich priors for training general-purpose humanoid control policies, but raw demonstrations are often incompatible with a robot's kinematics and dynamics, limiting their direct use. We present a two-stage pipeline for generating natural and dynamically feasible motion references from task-space human data. First, we convert human motion into a unified robot description format (URDF)-based skeleton representation and calibrate it to the target humanoid's dimensions. By aligning the underlying skeleton structure rather than heuristically modifying task-space targets, this step significantly reduces inverse kinematics error and tuning effort. Second, we refine the retargeted trajectories through progressive kinodynamic trajectory optimization (TO), solved in three stages: kinematic TO, inverse dynamics, and full kinodynamic TO, each warm-started from the previous solution. The final result yields dynamically consistent state trajectories and joint torque profiles, providing high-quality references for learning-based controllers. Together, skeleton calibration and kinodynamic TO enable the generation of natural, physically consistent motion references across diverse humanoid platforms.

</details>


### [144] [MiNI-Q: A Miniature, Wire-Free Quadruped with Unbounded, Independently Actuated Leg Joints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11537)
*Daniel Koh,Suraj Shah,Yufeng Wu,Dennis Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MiNI-Q²是一款微型无线四足机器人，采用独立驱动的机械无限制2自由度腿关节，能够实现振荡步态和旋转运动，可折叠至2.5厘米高度，具备多种运动能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统腿式机器人的物理关节限制会限制工作空间、约束步态设计并增加硬件损坏风险。本文旨在开发具有机械无限制关节的微型四足机器人以克服这些限制。

Method: 提出MiNI-Q²机器人的机械设计、运动学分析和实验验证。采用独立驱动的机械无限制2自由度腿关节设计，无线架构改进了之前的Q8bot设计，提高了微型尺度的装配可靠性。

Result: MiNI-Q²最高速度达0.46 m/s，能够实现低间隙爬行、爬楼梯、倒置行走、跳跃和后空翻等多种运动能力。机器人可折叠至2.5厘米最小高度。

Conclusion: MiNI-Q²展示了机械无限制关节在微型四足机器人中的优势，提供了更大的工作空间和运动灵活性。所有机械和电气设计文件开源发布，支持可重复性和进一步研究。

Abstract: Physical joint limits are common in legged robots and can restrict workspace, constrain gait design, and increase the risk of hardware damage. This paper introduces MiNI-Q^2, a miniature, wire-free quadruped robot with independently actuated, mechanically unbounded 2-DOF leg joints. We present the mechanical design, kinematic analysis, and experimental validation of the proposed robot. The leg mechanism enables both oscillatory gaits and rotary locomotion while allowing the robot to fold to a minimum height of 2.5 cm. Experimentally, MiNI-Q achieves speeds up to 0.46 m/s and demonstrates low-clearance crawling, stair climbing, inverted locomotion, jumping, and backflipping. The wire-free architecture extends our previous Q8bot design, improving assembly reliability at miniature scale. All mechanical and electrical design files are released open source to support reproducibility and further research.

</details>


### [145] [RoboClaw: An Agentic Framework for Scalable Long-Horizon Robotic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11558)
*Ruiying Li,Yunlang Zhou,YuYao Zhu,Kylin Chen,Jingyuan Wang,Sukai Wang,Kongtao Hu,Minhui Yu,Bowen Jiang,Zhan Su,Jiayao Ma,Xin He,Yongjian Shen,Yangyang,Guanghui Ren,Maoqing Yao,Wenhao Wang,Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboClaw：一种基于VLM的机器人框架，通过纠缠动作对实现自主数据收集和策略学习，统一数据收集、策略学习和任务执行，显著提升长时程任务的稳定性和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA系统在长时程任务上面临扩展性挑战，传统流水线将数据收集、策略学习和部署分离，导致依赖人工环境重置和多策略执行脆弱。需要一种统一框架来减少人工干预并提高鲁棒性。

Method: 提出RoboClaw框架，引入纠缠动作对(EAP)，将前向操作行为与逆向恢复动作耦合，形成自重置循环用于自主数据收集。同一智能体在部署时执行高层推理并动态编排学习到的策略基元。

Result: 在真实世界操作任务中，相比传统开环流水线，RoboClaw提高了稳定性和可扩展性，长时程任务成功率提升25%，人工时间投入减少53.7%。

Conclusion: RoboClaw通过统一数据收集、策略学习和任务执行，减少了阶段间不匹配，提高了多策略鲁棒性，显著降低了机器人生命周期中的人工努力，为长时程VLA任务提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) systems have shown strong potential for language-driven robotic manipulation. However, scaling them to long-horizon tasks remains challenging. Existing pipelines typically separate data collection, policy learning, and deployment, resulting in heavy reliance on manual environment resets and brittle multi-policy execution. We present RoboClaw, an agentic robotics framework that unifies data collection, policy learning, and task execution under a single VLM-driven controller. At the policy level, RoboClaw introduces Entangled Action Pairs (EAP), which couple forward manipulation behaviors with inverse recovery actions to form self-resetting loops for autonomous data collection. This mechanism enables continuous on-policy data acquisition and iterative policy refinement with minimal human intervention. During deployment, the same agent performs high-level reasoning and dynamically orchestrates learned policy primitives to accomplish long-horizon tasks. By maintaining consistent contextual semantics across collection and execution, RoboClaw reduces mismatch between the two phases and improves multi-policy robustness. Experiments in real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate improved stability and scalability compared to conventional open-loop pipelines, while significantly reducing human effort throughout the robot lifecycle, achieving a 25% improvement in success rate over baseline methods on long-horizon tasks and reducing human time investment by 53.7%.

</details>


### [146] [Unsupervised LiDAR-Based Multi-UAV Detection and Tracking Under Extreme Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11586)
*Nivand Khosravi,Rodrigo Ventura,Meysam Basiri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无监督LiDAR检测与跟踪管道，解决固态LiDAR扫描稀疏性问题，通过自适应聚类与概率数据关联实现无人机检测跟踪


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态LiDAR非重复扫描导致点云极度稀疏（小型无人机在10-25米距离仅产生1-2个回波），现有检测方法假设的点密度不足，且缺乏标记训练数据，需要无监督解决方案

Method: 1) 检测器：结合距离自适应DBSCAN聚类与三阶段时间一致性检查；2) 跟踪器：比较确定性匈牙利分配与联合概率数据关联(JPDA)，均与交互多模型滤波结合；3) 评估策略：结合真实世界检测（RTK-GPS地面真值）与仿真跟踪（身份标注地面真值）

Result: 最佳检测配置达到0.891精度、0.804召回率和0.63米RMSE；minPts扫描验证大多数扫描最多包含1-2个目标点；JPDA在四种仿真场景中将身份切换减少64%，对MOTA影响可忽略

Conclusion: 提出的无监督LiDAR管道能有效处理稀疏点云下的无人机检测与跟踪，概率数据关联在轨迹接近时优于确定性方法，双环境评估策略克服了GNSS在2米以下距离的限制

Abstract: Non-repetitive solid-state LiDAR scanning leads to an extremely sparse measurement regime for detecting airborne UAVs: a small quadrotor at 10-25 m typically produces only 1-2 returns per scan, which is far below the point densities assumed by most existing detection approaches and inadequate for robust multi-target data association. We introduce an unsupervised, LiDAR-only pipeline that addresses both detection and tracking without the need for labeled training data. The detector integrates range-adaptive DBSCAN clustering with a three-stage temporal consistency check and is benchmarked on real-world air-to-air flight data under eight different parameter configurations. The best setup attains 0.891 precision, 0.804 recall, and 0.63 m RMSE, and a systematic minPts sweep verifies that most scans contain at most 1-2 target points, directly quantifying the sparsity regime. For multi-target tracking, we compare deterministic Hungarian assignment with joint probabilistic data association (JPDA), each coupled with Interacting Multiple Model filtering, in four simulated scenarios with increasing levels of ambiguity. JPDA cuts identity switches by 64% with negligible impact on MOTA, demonstrating that probabilistic association is advantageous when UAV trajectories approach one another closely. A two-environment evaluation strategy, combining real-world detection with RTK-GPS ground truth and simulation-based tracking with identity-annotated ground truth, overcomes the limitations of GNSS-only evaluation at inter-UAV distances below 2 m.

</details>


### [147] [Diversity You Can Actually Measure: A Fast, Model-Free Diversity Metric for Robotics Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11634)
*Sreevardhan Sirigiri,Nathan Samuel de Lara,Christopher Agia,Florian Shkurti,Fabio Ramos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FAKTUAL算法，通过基于签名核的熵度量量化机器人模仿学习数据集多样性，并利用该度量进行数据筛选以提升下游任务性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿学习数据集通常包含不同长度的长时程轨迹，难以在尊重轨迹结构和几何特性的前提下量化多样性。现有方法缺乏直接操作演示数据集的有效多样性度量

Method: 将香农熵和冯·诺依曼熵扩展到轨迹数据，定义基于签名变换的熵度量，通过签名核的Gram矩阵计算。基于此提出FAKTUAL算法，在给定子集大小预算下选择最大化熵的演示子集

Result: 在图像和状态基的RoboMimic、MetaWorld基准测试以及四个真实世界操作任务上，FAKTUAL相比随机选择持续提升下游成功率，计算效率显著高于现有机器人数据筛选方法

Conclusion: 演示数据集的熵是理解和改进机器人模仿学习数据集多样性的实用工具，FAKTUAL提供了一种简单、模型无关且计算高效的数据筛选方法

Abstract: Robotics datasets for imitation learning typically consist of long-horizon trajectories of different lengths over states, actions, and high-dimensional observations (e.g., RGB video), making it non-trivial to quantify diversity in a way that respects the underlying trajectory structure and geometry. We extend Shannon and von Neumann entropy to this setting by defining signature transform-based entropy on the Gram matrix of a signature kernel over demonstrations, yielding entropy and diversity metrics that operate directly on the demonstration dataset. Building on these metrics, we study how dataset diversity affects generalization performance in robot imitation learning and propose a simple, model-free way to curate diverse demonstrations. We introduce FAKTUAL (FAst trajectory Kernel enTropy cUration for imitation Learning), a data curation algorithm that selects a subset of demonstrations maximizing entropy given a subset-size budget. FAKTUAL is fully model-free, requires no access to the imitation policy or rollouts, and adds negligible overhead relative to policy training. We evaluate our approach on image and state-based RoboMimic and MetaWorld benchmarks, as well as four real-world manipulation tasks. Across tasks and architectures, diversity-aware curation with FAKTUAL consistently improves downstream success rates over random selection, while being substantially more computationally efficient compared to recent robot data curation methods. Our results suggest that the entropy of demonstration datasets is a practical tool for understanding and improving dataset diversity in robot imitation learning.

</details>


### [148] [Learn Structure, Adapt on the Fly: Multi-Scale Residual Learning and Online Adaptation for Aerial Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11638)
*Samaksh Ujjawal,Naveen Sudheer Nair,Shivansh Pratap Singh,Rishabh Dev Yadav,Wei Pan,Spandan Roy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Factorized Dynamics Transformer (FDT)和Latent Residual Adapter (LRA)的预测-自适应框架，用于自主空中机械臂的非平稳多尺度残差动力学建模与实时补偿


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主空中机械臂(AAMs)是固有的耦合非线性系统，在机械臂重构和突加载荷变化时表现出非平稳和多尺度残差动力学。传统解析模型依赖固定参数结构，静态数据驱动模型假设平稳动力学，在配置变化和载荷变化时性能下降。现有学习架构未显式分解跨变量耦合和多尺度时间效应，混淆了瞬时惯性动力学与长时域状态演化。

Method: 提出预测-自适应框架，核心是Factorized Dynamics Transformer (FDT)，将物理变量视为独立token，实现显式跨变量注意力，结构上分离短时域惯性依赖与长时域气动效应。为处理部署时分布偏移，Latent Residual Adapter (LRA)通过递归最小二乘法在潜空间进行快速线性自适应，保留离线非线性表示且计算开销可控。自适应残差预测直接集成到残差补偿自适应控制器中。

Result: 在承受未知载荷的空中机械臂上进行真实世界实验，相比最先进的学习基线，展示了更高的预测保真度、加速的扰动衰减和优越的闭环跟踪精度，同时保持严格的实时可行性。

Conclusion: Factorized Dynamics Transformer与Latent Residual Adapter相结合，为自主空中机械臂的非平稳多尺度残差动力学建模提供了有效的预测-自适应框架，实现了实时补偿和优越的控制性能。

Abstract: Autonomous Aerial Manipulators (AAMs) are inherently coupled, nonlinear systems that exhibit nonstationary and multiscale residual dynamics, particularly during manipulator reconfiguration and abrupt payload variations. Conventional analytical dynamic models rely on fixed parametric structures, while static data-driven model assume stationary dynamics and degrade under configuration changes and payload variations. Moreover, existing learning architectures do not explicitly factorize cross-variable coupling and multi-scale temporal effects, conflating instantaneous inertial dynamics with long-horizon regime evolution. We propose a predictive-adaptive framework for real-time residual modeling and compensation in AAMs. The core of this framework is the Factorized Dynamics Transformer (FDT), which treats physical variables as independent tokens. This design enables explicit cross-variable attention while structurally separating short-horizon inertial dependencies from long-horizon aerodynamic effects. To address deployment-time distribution shifts, a Latent Residual Adapter (LRA) performs rapid linear adaptation in the latent space via Recursive Least Squares, preserving the offline nonlinear representation without prohibitive computational overhead. The adapted residual forecast is directly integrated into a residual-compensated adaptive controller. Real-world experiments on an aerial manipulator subjected to unseen payloads demonstrate higher prediction fidelity, accelerated disturbance attenuation, and superior closed-loop tracking precision compared to state-of-the-art learning baselines, all while maintaining strict real-time feasibility.

</details>


### [149] [Chunk-Boundary Artifact in Action-Chunked Generative Policies: A Noise-Sensitive Failure Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11642)
*Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究发现动作分块策略中的边界不连续是一种可分析的、噪声主导的、可干预的故障机制，而非不可避免的执行缺陷


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作分块已成为生成式视觉运动策略的核心设计选择，但分块边界处出现的执行不连续性仍未被充分理解。研究者希望深入分析这些边界不连续现象的本质和机制

Method: 在预训练的动作分块策略中，通过以下方法研究分块边界伪影：1) 关联分析伪影与任务失败的关系；2) 在固定观测上下文中，通过改变潜在噪声系统性地调节伪影幅度；3) 识别噪声空间中与伪影相关的方向，并应用轨迹级引导来可靠地改变伪影幅度

Result: 研究发现：1) 分块边界伪影与任务失败显著相关(p<1e-4)，且出现在执行过程中而非事后症状；2) 伪影幅度可通过噪声系统性地调节；3) 通过噪声空间中的轨迹级引导可可靠地改变所有评估任务中的伪影幅度；4) 在困难任务中，成功/失败分布相应变化；在接近上限的任务中，正面增益被策略饱和压缩，但负面因果效应仍然可见

Conclusion: 研究将边界不连续性重新定义为一种可分析的、噪声主导的、可干预的故障机制，而非不可避免的执行缺陷，为改进动作分块策略提供了新的理解和干预途径

Abstract: Action chunking has become a central design choice for generative visuomotor policies, yet the execution discontinuities that arise at chunk boundaries remain poorly understood. In a frozen pretrained action-chunked policy, we identify chunk-boundary artifact as a noise-sensitive failure mechanism. First, artifact is strongly associated with task failure (p < 1e-4, permutation test) and emerges during the rollout rather than only as a post-hoc symptom. Second, under a fixed observation context, changing only latent noise systematically modulates artifact magnitude. Third, by identifying artifact-related directions in noise space and applying trajectory-level steering, we reliably alter artifact magnitude across all evaluated tasks. In hard-task settings with remaining outcome headroom, the success/failure distribution shifts accordingly; on near-ceiling tasks, positive gains are compressed by policy saturation, while the negative causal effect remains visible. Overall, we recast boundary discontinuity from an unavoidable execution nuisance into an analyzable, noise-dominated, and intervenable failure mechanism.

</details>


### [150] [Concurrent Prehensile and Nonprehensile Manipulation: A Practical Approach to Multi-Stage Dexterous Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11655)
*Hao Jiang,Yue Wu,Yue Wang,Gaurav S. Sukhatme,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexMulti：一种样本高效的灵巧多任务操作方法，通过将演示分解为物体中心技能，采用检索-对齐-执行范式，在少量演示下实现多阶段操作任务


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手能够同时进行抓握和非抓握操作（如持有一个物体同时与另一个物体交互），这是日常任务的关键能力，但在机器人领域尚未充分探索。学习这种长时程、接触丰富的多阶段行为具有挑战性，因为演示数据收集成本高，端到端策略需要大量数据才能泛化到不同的物体几何形状和放置位置。

Method: DexMulti方法将演示分解为具有明确定义时间边界的物体中心技能，而非学习单一策略。采用检索-对齐-执行范式：基于当前物体几何检索演示技能，使用不确定性感知估计器（跟踪质心和偏航角）将技能对齐到观察到的物体状态，然后执行对齐后的技能。

Result: 在三个需要并发操作的多阶段任务（抓握+拉动、抓握+打开、抓握+抓握）上，使用两种灵巧手（Allegro和LEAP）进行了1000多次真实世界试验。该方法在训练物体上平均成功率66%，每个物体仅需3-4个演示，性能优于扩散策略基线2-3倍，同时所需演示数量少得多。结果展示了对未见物体和空间变化（±25厘米）的鲁棒泛化能力。

Conclusion: DexMulti提供了一种样本高效的灵巧多任务操作方法，通过技能分解和检索-对齐-执行范式，在少量演示下实现了多阶段操作任务的鲁棒执行和泛化，为解决真实世界灵巧操作中的长时程、接触丰富行为提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Dexterous hands enable concurrent prehensile and nonprehensile manipulation, such as holding one object while interacting with another, a capability essential for everyday tasks yet underexplored in robotics. Learning such long-horizon, contact-rich multi-stage behaviors is challenging because demonstrations are expensive to collect and end-to-end policies require substantial data to generalize across varied object geometries and placements. We present DexMulti, a sample-efficient approach for real-world dexterous multi-task manipulation that decomposes demonstrations into object-centric skills with well-defined temporal boundaries. Rather than learning monolithic policies, our method retrieves demonstrated skills based on current object geometry, aligns them to the observed object state using an uncertainty-aware estimator that tracks centroid and yaw, and executes them via a retrieve-align-execute paradigm. We evaluate on three multi-stage tasks requiring concurrent manipulation (Grasp + Pull, Grasp + Open, and Grasp + Grasp) across two dexterous hands (Allegro and LEAP) in over 1,000 real-world trials. Our approach achieves an average success rate of 66% on training objects with only 3-4 demonstrations per object, outperforming diffusion policy baselines by 2-3x while requiring far fewer demonstrations. Results demonstrate robust generalization to held-out objects and spatial variations up to +/-25 cm.

</details>


### [151] [Coupling Tensor Trains with Graph of Convex Sets: Effective Compression, Exploration, and Planning in the C-Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11658)
*Gerhard Reinerth,Riddhiman Laha,Marcello Romano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TANGO是一个结合张量压缩和图优化的运动规划框架，通过张量列车分解压缩可行配置空间，在GCS类结构中嵌入任务相关区域，实现高效几何感知运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于优化的运动规划器（如GCS）虽然能生成平滑最优轨迹，但通常需要预定义高维配置空间的凸表征，这对一般机器人任务往往难以实现。需要更高效、可扩展的配置空间表示方法。

Method: TANGO采用张量列车分解来近似压缩可行配置空间，快速发现和估计任务相关区域，然后将这些区域嵌入到类似GCS的结构中，实现同时尊重系统约束和环境复杂性的几何感知运动规划。

Result: 在平面和真实机器人上的严格仿真研究证实了该方法的有效压缩能力和更高质量轨迹生成。框架实现了高效、几何感知的运动规划。

Conclusion: TANGO通过耦合张量压缩与结构化图推理，为未来机器人系统中更富表达力和可扩展的配置空间表示奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present TANGO (Tensor ANd Graph Optimization), a novel motion planning framework that integrates tensor-based compression with structured graph optimization to enable efficient and scalable trajectory generation. While optimization-based planners such as the Graph of Convex Sets (GCS) offer powerful tools for generating smooth, optimal trajectories, they typically rely on a predefined convex characterization of the high-dimensional configuration space-a requirement that is often intractable for general robotic tasks. TANGO builds further by using Tensor Train decomposition to approximate the feasible configuration space in a compressed form, enabling rapid discovery and estimation of task-relevant regions. These regions are then embedded into a GCS-like structure, allowing for geometry-aware motion planning that respects both system constraints and environmental complexity. By coupling tensor-based compression with structured graph reasoning, TANGO enables efficient, geometry-aware motion planning and lays the groundwork for more expressive and scalable representations of configuration space in future robotic systems. Rigorous simulation studies on planar and real robots reinforce our claims of effective compression and higher quality trajectories.

</details>


### [152] [RADAR: Closed-Loop Robotic Data Generation via Semantic Planning and Autonomous Causal Environment Reset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11811)
*Yongzhong Wang,Keyu Zhu,Yong Zhong,Liqiong Wang,Jinyu Yang,Feng Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RADAR是一个完全自主的机器人数据采集系统，通过四模块流水线消除人工干预，实现大规模物理交互数据的自动生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人学习所需的大规模物理交互数据采集面临严重瓶颈，人工参与的成本高昂且可扩展性有限，需要突破这一限制。
