Method: 采用四模块流水线：1) VLM基于2-5个3D人体演示进行任务生成；2) GNN策略通过上下文模仿学习执行动作；3) VLM通过结构化VQA进行自动成功评估；4) 有限状态机实现自主环境重置和数据路由。

Result: 在仿真中，系统在复杂长时程任务上达到90%成功率，远超传统基线方法；在真实世界中，系统能可靠执行多样化、接触密集的技能，无需领域特定微调。

Conclusion: RADAR提供了一个高度可扩展的机器人数据采集范式，通过完全自主的闭环数据生成引擎，实现了从人工密集型到自持续数据采集的范式转变。

Abstract: The acquisition of large-scale physical interaction data, a critical prerequisite for modern robot learning, is severely bottlenecked by the prohibitive cost and scalability limits of human-in-the-loop collection paradigms. To break this barrier, we introduce Robust Autonomous Data Acquisition for Robotics (RADAR), a fully autonomous, closed-loop data generation engine that completely removes human intervention from the collection cycle. RADAR elegantly divides the cognitive load into a four-module pipeline. Anchored by 2-5 3D human demonstrations as geometric priors, a Vision-Language Model first orchestrates scene-relevant task generation via precise semantic object grounding and skill retrieval. Next, a Graph Neural Network policy translates these subtasks into physical actions via in-context imitation learning. Following execution, the VLM performs automated success evaluation using a structured Visual Question Answering pipeline. Finally, to shatter the bottleneck of manual resets, a Finite State Machine orchestrates an autonomous environment reset and asymmetric data routing mechanism. Driven by simultaneous forward-reverse planning with a strict Last-In, First-Out causal sequence, the system seamlessly restores unstructured workspaces and robustly recovers from execution failures. This continuous brain-cerebellum synergy transforms data collection into a self-sustaining process. Extensive evaluations highlight RADAR's exceptional versatility. In simulation, our framework achieves up to 90% success rates on complex, long-horizon tasks, effortlessly solving challenges where traditional baselines plummet to near-zero performance. In real-world deployments, the system reliably executes diverse, contact-rich skills (e.g., deformable object manipulation) via few-shot adaptation without domain-specific fine-tuning, providing a highly scalable paradigm for robotic data acquisition.

</details>


### [153] [Energy Prediction on Sloping Ground for Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11963)
*Mohamed Ounally,Cyrille Pierre,Johann Laconte*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出仅使用标准车载传感器的简单能量模型，研究地形坡度和航向对四足机器人能量消耗的影响，支持路径级能量预测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿式机器人在户外环境中的能量管理是基本挑战，续航直接影响任务成功率，而高效资源使用减少生态影响

Method: 引入仅依赖标准车载传感器的简单能量模型，避免专用仪器，在商业四足机器人上进行现场运行识别，将能量表达为坡度角和航向的紧凑函数

Result: 自然地形现场验证显示力等效成本与坡度角呈近线性趋势，横向成本始终更高，轨迹段间具有可加性，支持面向规划的路径级能量预测评估

Conclusion: 提出的简单能量模型能够有效预测四足机器人在不同地形坡度和航向下的能量消耗，为路径规划提供实用的能量评估工具

Abstract: Energy management is a fundamental challenge for legged robots in outdoor environments. Endurance directly constrains mission success, while efficient resource use reduces ecological impact. This paper investigates how terrain slope and heading orientation influence the energetic cost of quadruped locomotion. We introduce a simple energy model that relies solely on standard onboard sensors, avoids specialized instrumentation, and remains applicable in previously unexplored environments. The model is identified from field runs on a commercial quadruped and expressed as a compact function of slope angle and heading. Field validation on natural terrain shows near-linear trends of force-equivalent cost with slope angle, consistently higher lateral costs, and additive behavior across trajectory segments, supporting path-level energy prediction for planning-oriented evaluation.

</details>


### [154] [Learning Visuomotor Policy for Multi-Robot Laser Tag Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11980)
*Kai Li,Shiyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于多机器人激光标签任务的端到端视觉运动策略，通过多智能体强化学习训练教师策略，然后蒸馏到基于视觉的学生策略，在真实机器人上实现了优于经典方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人激光标签任务中，经典的模块化方法面临有限可观测性、依赖深度映射和机器人间通信等挑战，需要更有效的解决方案。

Method: 使用多智能体强化学习训练高性能教师策略，通过知识蒸馏将其转移到基于视觉的学生策略；采用置换不变特征提取器和深度热图输入等技术设计提升性能。

Result: 提出的策略在命中准确率上比经典方法提高16.7%，在碰撞避免上提高6%，并成功部署到真实机器人上。

Conclusion: 端到端视觉运动策略能够有效解决多机器人激光标签任务中的挑战，通过知识蒸馏和特定技术设计实现了优越性能，并验证了真实机器人部署的可行性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study multi robot laser tag, a simplified yet practical shooting-game-style task. Classic modular approaches on these tasks face challenges such as limited observability and reliance on depth mapping and inter robot communication. To overcome these issues, we present an end-to-end visuomotor policy that maps images directly to robot actions. We train a high performing teacher policy with multi agent reinforcement learning and distill its knowledge into a vision-based student policy. Technical designs, including a permutation-invariant feature extractor and depth heatmap input, improve performance over standard architectures. Our policy outperforms classic methods by 16.7% in hitting accuracy and 6% in collision avoidance, and is successfully deployed on real robots. Code will be released publicly.

</details>


### [155] [Sim-to-reality adaptation for Deep Reinforcement Learning applied to an underwater docking application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12020)
*Alaaeddine Chaarani,Narcis Palomeras,Pere Ridao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度强化学习的自主水下对接方法，使用Girona AUV和高保真数字孪生环境，通过多进程RL框架加速训练，实现了超过90%的成功率并成功验证了仿真到现实的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习为自主水下对接提供了比传统控制方法更鲁棒的替代方案，特别是在适应不可预测环境条件方面。然而，"仿真到现实"的鸿沟和高训练延迟仍然是实际部署的主要瓶颈。

Method: 1) 将Stonefish模拟器适配到多进程RL框架中，显著加速学习过程，同时包含真实的AUV动力学、碰撞模型和传感器噪声；2) 使用近端策略优化算法开发6自由度控制策略；3) 在无头环境中训练，随机化起始位置以确保泛化性能；4) 奖励函数考虑距离、方向、动作平滑度和自适应碰撞惩罚，以促进软对接。

Result: 1) 智能体在仿真中实现了超过90%的成功率；2) 在物理测试池中成功验证了仿真到现实的适应性；3) DRL控制器表现出涌现行为，如基于俯仰的制动和偏航振荡，以辅助机械对齐。

Conclusion: 该方法通过高保真数字孪生环境和多进程RL框架有效解决了仿真到现实的鸿沟和训练延迟问题，实现了高性能的自主水下对接控制，并在物理环境中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) offers a robust alternative to traditional control methods for autonomous underwater docking, particularly in adapting to unpredictable environmental conditions. However, bridging the "sim-to-real" gap and managing high training latencies remain significant bottlenecks for practical deployment. This paper presents a systematic approach for autonomous docking using the Girona Autonomous Underwater Vehicle (AUV) by leveraging a high-fidelity digital twin environment. We adapted the Stonefish simulator into a multiprocessing RL framework to significantly accelerate the learning process while incorporating realistic AUV dynamics, collision models, and sensor noise. Using the Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) algorithm, we developed a 6-DoF control policy trained in a headless environment with randomized starting positions to ensure generalized performance. Our reward structure accounts for distance, orientation, action smoothness, and adaptive collision penalties to facilitate soft docking. Experimental results demonstrate that the agent achieved a success rate of over 90% in simulation. Furthermore, successful validation in a physical test tank confirmed the efficacy of the sim-to-reality adaptation, with the DRL controller exhibiting emergent behaviors such as pitch-based braking and yaw oscillations to assist in mechanical alignment.

</details>


### [156] [CRAFT: A Tendon-Driven Hand with Hybrid Hard-Soft Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12120)
*Leo Lin,Shivansh Patel,Jay Moon,Svetlana Lazebnik,Unnat Jain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CRAFT手是一种肌腱驱动的仿人手，采用混合刚柔顺应性设计，关节处使用软材料而连杆保持刚性，通过滚动接触关节表面保持重复运动路径，15个电机驱动，成本低于600美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决接触丰富操作中手的顺应性问题，传统设计在接触分布不均匀时存在局限性，需要一种既能承受载荷又具有适当顺应性的仿人手设计。

Method: 采用混合刚柔顺应性设计：关节处使用软材料吸收冲击，连杆保持刚性承受载荷；使用滚动接触关节表面确保屈曲运动路径的可重复性；15个电机通过肌腱驱动手指，保持紧凑外形和轻量化。

Result: 结构测试显示CRAFT提高了强度和耐久性，同时保持可比的可重复性；遥操作中改善了脆弱和低摩擦物品的处理能力，覆盖Feix分类法中33/33种抓握；成本低于600美元。

Conclusion: CRAFT手通过创新的混合刚柔顺应性设计，在保持结构强度的同时提供了适当的顺应性，在遥操作和抓握能力方面表现出色，低成本开源设计有望促进机器人手研究与应用。

Abstract: We introduce CRAFT hand, a tendon-driven anthropomorphic hand with hybrid hard-soft compliance for contact-rich manipulation. The design is based on a simple idea: contact is not uniform across the hand. Impacts concentrate at joints, while links carry most of the load. CRAFT places soft material at joints and keeps links rigid, and uses rollingcontact joint surfaces to keep flexion on repeatable motion paths. Fifteen motors mounted on the fingers drive the hand through tendons, keeping the form factor compact and the fingers light. In structural tests, CRAFT improves strength and endurance while maintaining comparable repeatability. In teleoperation, CRAFT improves handling of fragile and low-friction items, and the hand covers 33/33 grasps in the Feix taxonomy. The full design costs under $600 and will be released open-source with visionbased teleoperation and simulation integration. Project page: http://craft-hand.github.io/

</details>


### [157] [ComFree-Sim: A GPU-Parallelized Analytical Contact Physics Engine for Scalable Contact-Rich Robotics Simulation and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12185)
*Chetan Borse,Zhixian Xie,Wei-Cheng Huang,Wanxin Jin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ComFree-Sim：基于无互补性接触建模的GPU并行物理引擎，通过阻抗式预测-校正更新在闭式求解接触冲量，实现接触计算的解耦和GPU并行化，在密集接触场景中提供近线性运行时扩展和2-3倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统物理仿真引擎在处理接触密集型机器人任务时，接触分辨率成为瓶颈：主流引擎通过互补性约束或约束优化强制执行非穿透和库仑摩擦，需要每步迭代求解，其成本随接触密度超线性增长。这限制了实时控制和高频仿真的应用。

Method: 提出ComFree-Sim引擎，基于无互补性接触建模，通过阻抗式预测-校正更新在库仑摩擦对偶锥中闭式计算接触冲量。接触计算在接触对之间解耦，并在锥面可分离，自然映射到GPU内核。进一步扩展到统一的6D接触模型，捕捉切向、扭转和滚动摩擦，并引入实用的对偶锥阻抗启发式方法。

Result: 实验表明，与MuJoCo Warp相比，ComFree-Sim在密集接触场景中实现近线性运行时扩展和2-3倍吞吐量提升，同时保持相当的物理保真度。在实际应用中，用于多指LEAP手的实时MPC控制，在接触丰富的灵巧操作任务中实现更高的闭环成功率；在动力学感知运动重定向中，低延迟仿真支持实用高频控制。

Conclusion: ComFree-Sim通过无互补性接触建模和GPU并行化，解决了接触密集型物理仿真的计算瓶颈，实现了近线性扩展和高吞吐量，为实时机器人控制和高频仿真提供了实用解决方案，在灵巧操作和运动重定向等应用中展现出优势。

Abstract: Physics simulation for contact-rich robotics is often bottlenecked by contact resolution: mainstream engines enforce non-penetration and Coulomb friction via complementarity constraints or constrained optimization, requiring per-step iterative solves whose cost grows superlinearly with contact density. We present ComFree-Sim, a GPU-parallelized analytical contact physics engine built on complementarity-free contact modeling. ComFree-Sim computes contact impulses in closed form via an impedance-style prediction--correction update in the dual cone of Coulomb friction. Contact computation decouples across contact pairs and becomes separable across cone facets, mapping naturally to GPU kernels and yielding near-linear runtime scaling with the number of contacts. We further extend the formulation to a unified 6D contact model capturing tangential, torsional, and rolling friction, and introduce a practical dual-cone impedance heuristic. ComFree-Sim is implemented in Warp and exposed through a MuJoCo-compatible interface as a drop-in backend alternative to MuJoCo Warp (MJWarp). Experiments benchmark penetration, friction behaviors, stability, and simulation runtime scaling against MJWarp, demonstrating near-linear scaling and 2--3 times higher throughput in dense contact scenes with comparable physical fidelity. We deploy ComFree-Sim in real-time MPC for in-hand dexterous manipulation on a real-world multi-fingered LEAP hand and in dynamics-aware motion retargeting, demonstrating that low-latency simulation yields higher closed-loop success rates and enables practical high-frequency control in contact-rich tasks.

</details>


### [158] [SaPaVe: Towards Active Perception and Manipulation in Vision-Language-Action Models for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12193)
*Mengzhen Liu,Enshen Zhou,Cheng Chi,Yi Han,Shanyu Rong,Liming Chen,Pengwei Wang,Zhongyuan Wang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SaPaVe是一个端到端框架，通过解耦相机控制与操作动作、采用自底向上训练策略，实现了语义驱动的主动感知与鲁棒执行的统一，在真实世界任务中比现有方法提升31.25%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以统一语义驱动的主动感知与鲁棒、视角不变的执行能力，需要一种数据高效的方式同时学习这两种能力。

Method: 提出SaPaVe框架：1) 解耦相机控制与操作动作而非共享动作空间；2) 自底向上训练策略：先在大规模数据集上训练语义相机控制，再用混合数据联合优化两种动作类型；3) 引入3D几何感知模块提升动态视角下的执行鲁棒性；4) 构建ActiveViewPose-200K数据集和ActiveManip-Bench基准。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的大量实验表明，SaPaVe优于GR00T N1和π_0等最新视觉-语言-动作模型，在真实世界任务中成功率提升高达31.25%。

Conclusion: 通过解耦但协调的策略训练紧密耦合的感知与执行，能够实现高效且可泛化的主动操作，证明了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Active perception and manipulation are crucial for robots to interact with complex scenes. Existing methods struggle to unify semantic-driven active perception with robust, viewpoint-invariant execution. We propose SaPaVe, an end-to-end framework that jointly learns these capabilities in a data-efficient manner. Our approach decouples camera and manipulation actions rather than placing them in a shared action space, and follows a bottom-up training strategy: we first train semantic camera control on a large-scale dataset, then jointly optimize both action types using hybrid data. To support this framework, we introduce ActiveViewPose-200K, a dataset of 200k image-language-camera movement pairs for semantic camera movement learning, and a 3D geometry-aware module that improves execution robustness under dynamic viewpoints. We also present ActiveManip-Bench, the first benchmark for evaluating active manipulation beyond fixed-view settings. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world environments show that SaPaVe outperforms recent vision-language-action models such as GR00T N1 and \(π_0\), achieving up to 31.25\% higher success rates in real-world tasks. These results show that tightly coupled perception and execution, when trained with decoupled yet coordinated strategies, enable efficient and generalizable active manipulation. Project page: https://lmzpai.github.io/SaPaVe

</details>


### [159] [HandelBot: Real-World Piano Playing via Fast Adaptation of Dexterous Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12243)
*Amber Xie,Haozhi Qi,Dorsa Sadigh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HandelBot框架通过两阶段方法实现多指灵巧手的高精度双手钢琴演奏：先通过结构化精调修正空间对齐，再通过残差强化学习学习精细校正动作，仅需30分钟物理交互数据即可实现毫米级精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指灵巧手的灵巧操作一直是机器人学的重大挑战，高质量数据收集困难是主要瓶颈。虽然强化学习和仿真到现实迁移提供了有前景的替代方案，但对于毫米级精度任务（如双手钢琴演奏），迁移策略往往失败。

Method: HandelBot采用两阶段流水线：1) 结构化精调阶段：基于仿真训练的策略，通过物理rollout调整横向手指关节来修正空间对齐；2) 残差强化学习阶段：自主学习精细校正动作。

Result: 在五首公认歌曲的广泛硬件实验中，HandelBot成功实现了精确的双手钢琴演奏。系统性能比直接仿真部署提高了1.8倍，仅需30分钟物理交互数据。

Conclusion: HandelBot框架通过结合仿真策略和快速适应，有效解决了多指灵巧手毫米级精度操作的数据收集难题，为高精度灵巧操作任务提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Mastering dexterous manipulation with multi-fingered hands has been a grand challenge in robotics for decades. Despite its potential, the difficulty of collecting high-quality data remains a primary bottleneck for high-precision tasks. While reinforcement learning and simulation-to-real-world transfer offer a promising alternative, the transferred policies often fail for tasks demanding millimeter-scale precision, such as bimanual piano playing. In this work, we introduce HandelBot, a framework that combines a simulation policy and rapid adaptation through a two-stage pipeline. Starting from a simulation-trained policy, we first apply a structured refinement stage to correct spatial alignments by adjusting lateral finger joints based on physical rollouts. Next, we use residual reinforcement learning to autonomously learn fine-grained corrective actions. Through extensive hardware experiments across five recognized songs, we demonstrate that HandelBot can successfully perform precise bimanual piano playing. Our system outperforms direct simulation deployment by a factor of 1.8x and requires only 30 minutes of physical interaction data.

</details>


### [160] [HumDex:Humanoid Dexterous Manipulation Made Easy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12260)
*Liang Heng,Yihe Tang,Jiajun Xu,Henghui Bao,Di Huang,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HumDex是一个用于人形机器人全身灵巧操作的便携式遥操作系统，通过IMU运动追踪和学习式重定向方法解决现有系统的可移植性、遮挡和精度问题，并提出了两阶段模仿学习框架来高效收集高质量演示数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人全身灵巧操作中，高质量演示数据的高效收集是一个核心瓶颈。现有遥操作系统存在可移植性有限、遮挡问题或精度不足等缺陷，限制了它们在复杂全身任务中的应用。

Method: 1. 引入HumDex便携式遥操作系统，利用IMU运动追踪解决可移植性与精度的权衡问题；2. 提出学习式重定向方法生成平滑自然的手部动作，无需手动参数调整；3. 构建两阶段模仿学习框架：先在多样化人类运动数据上预训练学习可泛化先验，然后在机器人数据上微调以弥合本体差距实现精确执行。

Result: HumDex系统能够实现准确的全身体追踪并保持易于部署的特性。该方法显著提高了对新配置、物体和背景的泛化能力，同时最小化了数据采集成本。整个系统完全可复现并已开源。

Conclusion: HumDex系统有效解决了人形机器人全身灵巧操作中高质量演示数据收集的瓶颈问题，通过创新的遥操作技术和两阶段学习框架，为实现高效、精确的机器人操作提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates humanoid whole-body dexterous manipulation, where the efficient collection of high-quality demonstration data remains a central bottleneck.
  Existing teleoperation systems often suffer from limited portability, occlusion, or insufficient precision, which hinders their applicability to complex whole-body tasks. To address these challenges, we introduce HumDex, a portable teleoperation system designed for humanoid whole-body dexterous manipulation. Our system leverages IMU-based motion tracking to address the portability-precision trade-off, enabling accurate full-body tracking while remaining easy to deploy. For dexterous hand control, we further introduce a learning-based retargeting method that generates smooth and natural hand motions without manual parameter tuning. Beyond teleoperation, HumDex enables efficient collection of human motion data. Building on this capability, we propose a two-stage imitation learning framework that first pre-trains on diverse human motion data to learn generalizable priors, and then fine-tunes on robot data to bridge the embodiment gap for precise execution. We demonstrate that this approach significantly improves generalization to new configurations, objects, and backgrounds with minimal data acquisition costs. The entire system is fully reproducible and open-sourced at https://github.com/physical-superintelligence-lab/HumDex.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [Wide-Area GNSS Spoofing and Jamming Detection Using AIS-Derived Spatiotemporal Integrity Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11055)
*Sanghyeon Park,DeukJae Cho,Pyo-Woong Son*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个三阶段AIS框架，通过预过滤通信层缺陷、提取运动学异常、时空聚类分析，实现GNSS欺骗和干扰检测，无需专用传感器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNSS欺骗和干扰威胁海上导航，而原始AIS消息包含通信层缺陷（重复MMSI、时间戳错误、陈旧重传、多站重播延迟），这些缺陷可能被误判为欺骗或干扰，因此需要预过滤才能可靠使用AIS数据进行干扰检测。

Method: 三阶段框架：1) 基于规则的诊断丢弃通信故障；2) 应用交互多模型滤波器和传输间隔分析提取运动学一致性和连续性异常；3) 应用时空DBSCAN根据多船一致性和时间持续性对异常进行聚类，分类为传感器故障、欺骗或干扰。

Result: 在韩国沿海水域约9.66亿条AIS消息上测试，检测到17个欺骗集群和343个干扰集群，相对于朴素聚类方法减少了98.6%的误报。

Conclusion: 经过严格的预过滤后，AIS数据可以在没有专用传感器的情况下实现广域GNSS干扰检测，为海上导航安全提供有效解决方案。

Abstract: Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) spoofing and jamming threaten maritime navigation by corrupting positions from Automatic Identification System (AIS) transponders. Crucially, raw AIS messages contain communication-layer defects (duplicated MMSIs, timestamp errors, stale retransmissions, and multi-station rebroadcast delays) that can mimic spoofing or jamming. Thus, AIS positions are unreliable without pre-filtering. We propose a three-stage AIS-based framework that (1) uses rule-based diagnostics to discard communication faults, (2) applies an interacting multiple model filter and transmission-interval analysis to extract kinematic-consistency and continuity anomalies, and (3) applies spatiotemporal DBSCAN to group anomalies by multi-vessel coherence and temporal persistence and classify them as sensor faults, spoofing, or jamming. Tested on approximately 966 million AIS messages from Korean coastal waters, the framework detected 17 spoofing and 343 jamming clusters and reduced false alarms by 98.6% relative to naive clustering. These results show that, after rigorous pre-filtering, AIS data can enable wide-area GNSS interference detection without dedicated sensors.

</details>


### [162] [Primitive-Root Determinant Densities over Prime Fields and Implications for PRIM-LWE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11196)
*Vipin Singh Sehrawat*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文解决了prim-LWE问题中关于矩阵行列式为原根的概率下界问题，证明了inf_{p prime}c(p)=0，给出了c(p)的渐近阶和分布特性，并为密码学相关素数提供了具体的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: prim-LWE问题要求秘密矩阵的行列式为原根，其维度均匀约化常数c(p)定义为具有原根行列式的n×n矩阵在F_p上的密度下确界。Sehrawat等人询问inf_{p prime}c(p)=0是否成立，并指出如果原根素数猜想成立，则答案为肯定。本文旨在无条件解决此问题，无需依赖原根素数猜想。

Method: 使用狄利克雷定理和梅滕斯乘积公式，绕过原根素数假设。通过数论方法分析c(p)的渐近行为，建立c(p)的分布特性，并推导出仅依赖于ω(q-1)的显式下界，其中ω(q-1)表示q-1的不同素因子个数。

Result: 1. 无条件证明了inf_{p prime}c(p)=0；2. 建立了min_{p≤x}c(p)≍1/log log x的精确阶；3. 证明了c(p)在素数上具有连续的纯奇异极限分布，支撑集恰好为[0,1/2]；4. 为密码学相关素数q推导了仅依赖于ω(q-1)的显式下界；5. 对于NIST标准化模数q=3329和q=8380417，维度均匀拒绝采样开销上界分别为2.17和3.42；6. 对于任意素数q>2^30，有1/c(q)≤1.79 log q；7. 最坏情况开销在p≤x时为Θ(log log x)，点态上1/c(q)=O(log log q)。

Conclusion: 本文完全解决了prim-LWE问题中关于c(p)下界的问题，无需依赖未证明的猜想。结果为密码学中基于prim-LWE的方案提供了实用的性能界限，特别是对于NIST标准化的模数，证明了拒绝采样开销是可管理的。方法仅依赖于经典的数论结果，具有理论上的简洁性和实用性。

Abstract: The \textsc{prim-lwe} problem (Sehrawat, Yeo, and Desmedt, \emph{Theoret.\ Comput.\ Sci.}\ 886, 2021) is a variant of Learning with Errors requiring the secret matrix to have a primitive-root determinant. The dimension-uniform reduction constant is $c(p)=\inf_{m\ge 1}c_m(p)$, where $c_n(p)$ is the density of $n\times n$ matrices over~$\mathbb{F}_p$ with primitive-root determinant. Those authors asked whether $\inf_{p\text{ prime}}c(p)=0$, noting that an affirmative answer would follow from the conjectural infinitude of primorial primes.
  We resolve this unconditionally using only Dirichlet's theorem and Mertens' product formula, bypassing the primorial-prime hypothesis entirely. We establish the sharp order $\min_{p\le x}c(p)\asymp 1/\log\log x$, prove that $c(p)$ possesses a continuous purely singular limiting distribution over the primes with support exactly $[0,1/2]$, and derive explicit lower bounds on $c(q)$ for primes of cryptographic interest parameterized solely by~$ω(q{-}1)$, the number of distinct prime factors of~$q{-}1$. These bounds apply to every prime~$q$ whose predecessor has controlled factorization structure, as measured by~$ω(q{-}1)$; this includes many NTT-friendly moduli, though NTT-friendliness alone does not imply the needed bound. For the NIST-standardized moduli $q=3329$ (ML-KEM) and $q=8380417$ (ML-DSA), the dimension-uniform expected rejection-sampling overhead~$1/c(q)$ is at most $2.17$ and $3.42$, respectively. As a simple conservative bound, for any prime $q>2^{30}$ one has $1/c(q)\le 1.79\log q$. The worst-case overhead among primes $p\le x$ is $Θ(\log\log x)$, and pointwise $1/c(q)=O(\log\log q)$.

</details>


### [163] [Security-by-Design for LLM-Based Code Generation: Leveraging Internal Representations for Concept-Driven Steering Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11212)
*Maximilian Wendlinger,Daniel Kowatsch,Konstantin Böttinger,Philip Sperl*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SCS-Code方法，通过引导CodeLLMs的内部表征来生成安全且功能正确的代码，解决了现有方法在安全性和功能性方面改进有限的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CodeLLMs虽然能生成功能正确的代码，但经常产生不安全的代码，带来严重安全风险。现有改进方法在基准测试中表现不足，主要因为缺乏对代码生成内部机制和安全漏洞根本原因的理解，研究者只能依赖启发式和经验观察。

Method: 研究CodeLLMs内部安全概念的表征，发现模型在生成不安全代码时通常意识到漏洞存在。基于此提出SCS-Code方法：在token生成过程中引导LLMs的内部表征朝向安全和功能正确的代码输出，形成轻量级模块化机制，可集成到现有代码模型中。

Result: SCS-Code在多个安全编码基准测试中优于最先进的方法，实现了卓越的性能表现。

Conclusion: 通过理解CodeLLMs内部安全概念表征并引导这些表征，可以有效提升AI生成代码的安全性，SCS-Code为实际应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) show remarkable capabilities in understanding natural language and generating complex code. However, as practitioners adopt CodeLLMs for increasingly critical development tasks, research reveals that these models frequently generate functionally correct yet insecure code, posing significant security risks. While multiple approaches have been proposed to improve security in AI-based code generation, combined benchmarks show these methods remain insufficient for practical use, achieving only limited improvements in both functional correctness and security. This stems from a fundamental gap in understanding the internal mechanisms of code generation and the root causes of security vulnerabilities, forcing researchers to rely on heuristics and empirical observations. In this work, we investigate the internal representation of security concepts in CodeLLMs, revealing that models are often aware of vulnerabilities as they generate insecure code. Through systematic evaluation, we demonstrate that CodeLLMs can distinguish between security subconcepts, enabling a more fine-grained analysis than prior black-box approaches. Leveraging these insights, we propose Secure Concept Steering for CodeLLMs (SCS-Code). During token generation, SCS-Code steers LLMs' internal representations toward secure and functional code output, enabling a lightweight and modular mechanism that can be integrated into existing code models. Our approach achieves superior performance compared to state-of-the-art methods across multiple secure coding benchmarks.

</details>


### [164] [LROO Rug Pull Detector: A Leakage-Resistant Framework Based on On-Chain and OSINT Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11324)
*Fatemeh Shoaei,Mohammad Pishdar,Mozafar Bag-Mohammadi,Mojtaba Karami*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种泄漏感知的早期Rug Pull检测框架，整合链上行为指标与时间对齐的OSINT信号，使用TabPFN模型在严格时间约束下从多模态表格数据中学习，实现了强判别性能和改进的概率校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约生态系统的不可变性和无需许可特性虽然支持去中心化应用，但也为大规模欺诈（特别是Rug Pull攻击）提供了便利。现有检测方法主要依赖反应式链上信号，且常受时间数据泄漏影响，限制了其实际可靠性。

Method: 构建包含1,000个代币项目的手工标注数据集，所有特征在流动性撤出前提取以确保因果有效性。框架整合结构链上指标与来自社交媒体活动和搜索趋势的外部关注信号，采用TabPFN作为核心建模组件，在严格时间约束下从多模态表格数据中学习。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提框架相比经典基线方法实现了强判别性能和改进的概率校准，同时保持低假阴性率。通过将Rug Pull检测构建为因果多模态预测问题，强调了泄漏弹性评估和校准风险估计在区块链安全系统中的必要性。

Conclusion: 该研究强调了泄漏感知评估和校准风险估计在区块链安全系统中的重要性，通过将Rug Pull检测构建为因果多模态预测问题，为早期欺诈检测提供了更可靠的框架。

Abstract: Smart contract-based ecosystems enable decentralized applications without trusted intermediaries, but their immutability and permissionless design also facilitate large-scale fraud. One of the most prevalent attacks is the rug pull, where project operators abruptly withdraw liquidity after artificially inflating token value. Existing detection methods primarily rely on reactive on-chain signals and often suffer from temporal data leakage, limiting their real-world reliability. This paper proposes a leakage-aware framework for early rug-pull detection that integrates on-chain behavioral metrics with temporally aligned Open Source Intelligence (OSINT) signals. We construct a hand-labeled dataset of 1,000 token projects, spanning DeFi and non-DeFi settings, with all features extracted strictly prior to any liquidity withdrawal to preserve causal validity. The dataset combines structural on-chain indicators with external attention signals derived from social media activity and search trends. Within this framework, TabPFN is employed as a core modeling component for learning from multimodal tabular data under strict temporal constraints. Experimental results show that the proposed framework achieves strong discriminative performance and improved probability calibration compared to classical baselines, while maintaining low false-negative rates. By framing rug-pull detection as a causal, multimodal forecasting problem, this work emphasizes the necessity of leakage-resilient evaluation and calibrated risk estimation for deployment in blockchain security systems.

</details>


### [165] [Unclonable Encryption in the Haar Random Oracle Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11437)
*James Bartusek,Eli Goldin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 在Haar随机预言机模型中构造了可重用、支持任意长度消息的不可克隆加密方案，首次证明计算假设下的可重用UE可能在"微密码"世界中存在


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在"微密码"（micocrypt）世界中构建不可克隆加密的可能性，该世界可能不存在单向函数，但存在计算假设下的可重用UE

Method: 在Haar随机预言机模型中构造UE方案，所有参与方可以查询U、U†、U*、UT，利用路径记录框架证明"酉重编程引理"

Result: 成功构造满足不可克隆不可区分性安全标准、支持密钥重用、可加密任意长度消息的UE方案，为计算假设下的可重用UE在微密码世界中的存在性提供了首个证据

Conclusion: 证明了在Haar随机预言机模型中可构建可重用不可克隆加密，提出的酉重编程引理具有独立研究价值，为微密码世界中的密码学构造提供了新思路

Abstract: We construct unclonable encryption (UE) in the Haar random oracle model, where all parties have query access to $U,U^\dagger,U^*,U^T$ for a Haar random unitary $U$. Our scheme satisfies the standard notion of unclonable indistinguishability security, supports reuse of the secret key, and can encrypt arbitrary-length messages. That is, we give the first evidence that (reusable) UE, which requires computational assumptions, exists in "micocrypt", a world where one-way functions may not exist.
  As one of our central technical contributions, we build on the recently introduced path recording framework to prove a natural ``unitary reprogramming lemma'', which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [166] [Software-Hardware Binding for Protection of Sensitive Data in Embedded Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11727)
*Bernhard Fischer,Daniel Dorfmeister,Flavio Ferrarotti,Manuel Penz,Michael Kargl,Martina Zeinzinger,Florian Eibensteiner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种结合硬件指纹和布尔逻辑的新型IP保护机制，使未经授权的软件副本在克隆设备上仍能运行但性能不佳，无需额外硬件支持


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业嵌入式系统中的数据是公司的重要知识产权，需要有效保护；传统保护方法容易被绕过，需要更难以破解的保护机制

Method: 结合硬件指纹（基于SRAM的物理不可克隆函数PUF）与布尔逻辑，将软件参数（如PID控制器参数）绑定到特定微控制器单元，无需额外硬件

Result: 安全评估表明，只有对在真实目标设备上运行的受保护软件进行复杂的动态分析才能揭示秘密数据，保护机制更难被绕过

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种更难以绕过的IP保护方案，无需额外硬件，仅需相对简单的软件更新，特别适用于工业嵌入式系统

Abstract: Embedded software used in industrial systems frequently relies on data that ensures the correct and efficient operation of these systems. Thus, companies invest considerable resources in fine-tuning this data, making it their valuable intellectual property (IP). We present a novel protection mechanism for this IP that combines hardware fingerprints with Boolean logic. Unlike usual copy-protection approaches, unauthorised copies of the software still run on cloned devices but suboptimally. According to our security evaluation, only a complex dynamic analysis of the protected software running on the genuine target device can reveal the secret data. This makes the protection offered by our method more difficult to bypass. Notably, our approach does not require additional hardware, relying only on relatively simple updates to the software. We evaluate our protection mechanism by binding the parameters of a PID controller to a microcontroller unit (MCU) by using a physically unclonable function (PUF) based on its SRAM.

</details>


### [167] [The Mirror Design Pattern: Strict Data Geometry over Model Scale for Prompt Injection Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11875)
*J Alex Corll*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Mirror提出了一种数据整理设计模式，通过构建匹配的正负样本单元，训练稀疏字符n-gram线性SVM作为第一层提示注入检测器，在亚毫秒延迟下达到95.97%召回率和92.07% F1分数，证明严格的数据几何结构比模型规模更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示注入防御常被框架为语义理解问题并委托给大型神经检测器，但第一层筛选有不同需求：需要在每个请求上运行，因此必须快速、确定性、不可提示且可审计。需要一种轻量级但有效的检测方法。

Method: 引入Mirror数据整理设计模式，将提示注入语料组织成匹配的正负样本单元，使分类器学习控制平面攻击机制而非偶然的语料捷径。使用5,000个严格筛选的开源样本，定义32单元镜像拓扑，填充31个单元，训练稀疏字符n-gram线性SVM，将其权重编译为静态Rust工件。

Result: 在524个测试案例上获得95.97%召回率和92.07% F1分数，亚毫秒延迟，无外部模型运行时依赖。相比之下，2200万参数的Prompt Guard~2模型在同一测试集上仅达到44.35%召回率和59.14% F1分数，中位数延迟49ms，p95延迟324ms。

Conclusion: 线性模型虽然会留下如使用与提及等语义模糊性给后续管道层处理，但在L1提示注入筛选范围内，严格的数据几何结构比模型规模更重要。Mirror模式为快速、确定性的第一层检测提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Prompt injection defenses are often framed as semantic understanding problems and delegated to increasingly large neural detectors. For the first screening layer, however, the requirements are different: the detector runs on every request and therefore must be fast, deterministic, non-promptable, and auditable. We introduce Mirror, a data-curation design pattern that organizes prompt injection corpora into matched positive and negative cells so that a classifier learns control-plane attack mechanics rather than incidental corpus shortcuts. Using 5,000 strictly curated open-source samples -- the largest corpus supportable under our public-data validity contract -- we define a 32-cell mirror topology, fill 31 of those cells with public data, train a sparse character n-gram linear SVM, compile its weights into a static Rust artifact, and obtain 95.97\% recall and 92.07\% F1 on a 524-case holdout at sub-millisecond latency with no external model runtime dependencies. On the same holdout, our next line of defense, a 22-million-parameter Prompt Guard~2 model reaches 44.35\% recall and 59.14\% F1 at 49\,ms median and 324\,ms p95 latency. Linear models still leave residual semantic ambiguities such as use-versus-mention for later pipeline layers, but within that scope our results show that for L1 prompt injection screening, strict data geometry can matter more than model scale.

</details>


### [168] [Understanding LLM Behavior When Encountering User-Supplied Harmful Content in Harmless Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11914)
*Junjie Chu,Yiting Qu,Ye Leng,Michael Backes,Yun Shen,Savvas Zannettou,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了大语言模型在内容层面的伦理漏洞：即使执行看似无害的任务，当遇到用户提供的有害内容时，主流LLMs仍会继续处理而非拒绝，存在被滥用的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLMs的对齐训练主要关注任务层面的伦理（拒绝执行直接有害的任务），但忽视了内容层面的伦理问题：在执行看似无害的任务时，如果用户提供的材料中包含有害内容，LLMs是否会像有道德意识的人类一样拒绝继续处理？

Method: 1) 构建包含1,357个条目、涵盖10个有害类别（不符合OpenAI使用政策）的有害知识数据集；2) 设计9个无害任务（符合OpenAI政策），根据所需用户提供内容量分为广泛、中等、有限三类；3) 评估9个LLMs在执行无害任务时遇到用户提供有害内容时的行为；4) 分析有害知识类别与任务类型之间的动态关系对LLMs的影响。

Result: 1) 当前LLMs（包括最新的GPT-5.2和Gemini-3-Pro）在执行无害任务时经常无法坚持人类对齐的伦理，继续处理有害内容；2) "暴力/图像"类别的外部知识和"翻译"任务更容易引发LLMs的有害响应；3) 通过消融研究探讨了影响这种新型滥用漏洞的潜在因素。

Conclusion: LLMs存在被忽视的内容层面伦理风险，即使执行无害任务也可能被用于处理有害内容。研究呼吁利益相关者加强安全措施，减轻这种被忽视的伦理风险。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly trained to align with human values, primarily focusing on task level, i.e., refusing to execute directly harmful tasks. However, a subtle yet crucial content-level ethical question is often overlooked: when performing a seemingly benign task, will LLMs -- like morally conscious human beings -- refuse to proceed when encountering harmful content in user-provided material? In this study, we aim to understand this content-level ethical question and systematically evaluate its implications for mainstream LLMs. We first construct a harmful knowledge dataset (i.e., non-compliant with OpenAI's usage policy) to serve as the user-supplied harmful content, with 1,357 entries across ten harmful categories. We then design nine harmless tasks (i.e., compliant with OpenAI's usage policy) to simulate the real-world benign tasks, grouped into three categories according to the extent of user-supplied content required: extensive, moderate, and limited. Leveraging the harmful knowledge dataset and the set of harmless tasks, we evaluate how nine LLMs behave when exposed to user-supplied harmful content during the execution of benign tasks, and further examine how the dynamics between harmful knowledge categories and tasks affect different LLMs. Our results show that current LLMs, even the latest GPT-5.2 and Gemini-3-Pro, often fail to uphold human-aligned ethics by continuing to process harmful content in harmless tasks. Furthermore, external knowledge from the ``Violence/Graphic'' category and the ``Translation'' task is more likely to elicit harmful responses from LLMs. We also conduct extensive ablation studies to investigate potential factors affecting this novel misuse vulnerability. We hope that our study could inspire enhanced safety measures among stakeholders to mitigate this overlooked content-level ethical risk.

</details>


### [169] [Delayed Backdoor Attacks: Exploring the Temporal Dimension as a New Attack Surface in Pre-Trained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11949)
*Zikang Ding,Haomiao Yang,Meng Hao,Wenbo Jiang,Kunlan Xiang,Runmeng Du,Yijing Liu,Ruichen Zhang,Dusit Niyato*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新型后门攻击范式——延迟后门攻击（DBA），通过将触发与激活时间解耦，使得日常词汇也能作为触发器，突破了传统后门攻击的"即时性假设"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战传统后门攻击的"即时性假设"，探索时间维度作为新的攻击面。传统攻击要求触发器出现时立即激活恶意行为，这限制了攻击的隐蔽性和可行性，特别是无法使用常见词汇作为触发器。研究者认为时间维度的引入可以解锁一类先前不可行的攻击。

Method: 提出了基于非线性衰减的延迟后门攻击（DND）原型。该方法嵌入轻量级、有状态的逻辑模块，将激活推迟到可配置阈值达到时才触发。建立了描述延迟行为的正式模型，并设计了双指标评估框架（ASR和ASR_delay）来实证测量延迟效果。

Result: 在四个NLP基准测试上的广泛实验验证了DND的核心能力：1）可在可控时间内保持休眠状态；2）维持高清洁准确率（≥94%）；3）达到接近完美的激活后攻击成功率（≈99%，其他方法平均低于95%）；4）对多种最先进防御方法表现出韧性。

Conclusion: 时间维度构成了预训练模型中可行但未受保护的攻击面，这为后门攻击研究开辟了新方向。研究强调了开发下一代有状态、时间感知的防御机制的必要性，以应对这种新型威胁。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks against pre-trained models (PTMs) have traditionally operated under an ``immediacy assumption,'' where malicious behavior manifests instantly upon trigger occurrence. This work revisits and challenges this paradigm by introducing \textit{\textbf{Delayed Backdoor Attacks (DBA)}}, a new class of threats in which activation is temporally decoupled from trigger exposure. We propose that this \textbf{temporal dimension} is the key to unlocking a previously infeasible class of attacks: those that use common, everyday words as triggers. To examine the feasibility of this paradigm, we design and implement a proof-of-concept prototype, termed \underline{D}elayed Backdoor Attacks Based on \underline{N}onlinear \underline{D}ecay (DND). DND embeds a lightweight, stateful logic module that postpones activation until a configurable threshold is reached, producing a distinct latency phase followed by a controlled outbreak. We derive a formal model to characterize this latency behavior and propose a dual-metric evaluation framework (ASR and ASR$_{delay}$) to empirically measure the delay effect. Extensive experiments on four (natural language processing)NLP benchmarks validate the core capabilities of DND: it remains dormant for a controllable duration, sustains high clean accuracy ($\ge$94\%), and achieves near-perfect post-activation attack success rates ($\approx$99\%, The average of other methods is below 95\%.). Moreover, DND exhibits resilience against several state-of-the-art defenses. This study provides the first empirical evidence that the temporal dimension constitutes a viable yet unprotected attack surface in PTMs, underscoring the need for next-generation, stateful, and time-aware defense mechanisms.

</details>


### [170] [EmbTracker: Traceable Black-box Watermarking for Federated Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12089)
*Haodong Zhao,Jinming Hu,Yijie Bai,Tian Dong,Wei Du,Zhuosheng Zhang,Yanjiao Chen,Haojin Zhu,Gongshen Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: EmbTracker是一个面向联邦语言模型的服务器端可追踪黑盒水印框架，通过嵌入基于后门的水印实现黑盒验证，并为每个客户端注入独特的身份特定水印，从而实现客户端级别的溯源能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦语言模型（FedLM）允许在不共享原始数据的情况下进行协作学习，但引入了关键漏洞，因为每个不可信的客户端都可能泄露接收到的功能模型实例。现有的FedLM水印方案通常需要白盒访问和客户端合作，仅提供组级别的所有权证明而非个体可追溯性。

Method: EmbTracker是一个服务器端、可追踪的黑盒水印框架，专门为FedLM设计。通过嵌入基于后门的水印实现黑盒可验证性，可通过简单的API查询检测。客户端级别的可追溯性通过向分发给每个客户端的模型中注入独特的身份特定水印来实现。

Result: 在各种语言和视觉语言模型上的广泛实验表明，EmbTracker实现了稳健的可追溯性，验证率接近100%，对移除攻击（微调、剪枝、量化）具有高抵抗力，对主要任务性能影响可忽略（通常在1-2%以内）。

Conclusion: EmbTracker为联邦语言模型提供了一个有效的服务器端可追踪黑盒水印解决方案，能够在非合作参与者的情况下实现客户端级别的溯源，解决了现有水印方案在联邦学习环境中的局限性。

Abstract: Federated Language Model (FedLM) allows a collaborative learning without sharing raw data, yet it introduces a critical vulnerability, as every untrustworthy client may leak the received functional model instance. Current watermarking schemes for FedLM often require white-box access and client-side cooperation, providing only group-level proof of ownership rather than individual traceability. We propose EmbTracker, a server-side, traceable black-box watermarking framework specifically designed for FedLMs. EmbTracker achieves black-box verifiability by embedding a backdoor-based watermark detectable through simple API queries. Client-level traceability is realized by injecting unique identity-specific watermarks into the model distributed to each client. In this way, a leaked model can be attributed to a specific culprit, ensuring robustness even against non-cooperative participants. Extensive experiments on various language and vision-language models demonstrate that EmbTracker achieves robust traceability with verification rates near 100\%, high resilience against removal attacks (fine-tuning, pruning, quantization), and negligible impact on primary task performance (typically within 1-2\%).

</details>


### [171] [Understanding Disclosure Risk in Differential Privacy with Applications to Noise Calibration and Auditing (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12142)
*Patricia Guerra-Balboa,Annika Sauer,Héber H. Arcolezi,Thorsten Strufe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出"重构优势"作为统一的风险度量，替代传统成员推断和重构鲁棒性分析，为差分隐私提供更准确的风险评估和噪声校准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私风险分析主要关注成员推断攻击（威胁范围有限）或依赖重构鲁棒性，但在实际假设下，重构鲁棒性可能产生误导性风险估计并违反声称的界限，限制了其在原则性DP校准和审计中的实用性。

Method: 引入重构优势作为统一风险度量，推导差分隐私噪声与对抗优势之间的严格界限，并针对任意DP机制和攻击者知识特征化最优对抗策略。

Result: 重构优势提高了DP审计的准确性和范围，支持风险驱动的噪声校准，并为系统化DP审计提供理论基础，在DP使能的数据管理系统中实现更有效的效用-隐私权衡。

Conclusion: 重构优势作为一个统一的风险度量，能够一致地捕捉成员推断、属性推断和数据重构的风险，为差分隐私系统的风险评估、校准和审计提供了更可靠的理论基础和实践方法。

Abstract: Differential Privacy (DP) is widely adopted in data management systems to enable data sharing with formal disclosure guarantees. A central systems challenge is understanding how DP noise translates into effective protection against inference attacks, since this directly determines achievable utility. Most existing analyses focus only on membership inference -- capturing only a threat -- or rely on reconstruction robustness (ReRo). However, under realistic assumptions, we show that ReRo can yield misleading risk estimates and violate claimed bounds, limiting their usefulness for principled DP calibration and auditing.
  This paper introduces reconstruction advantage, a unified risk metric that consistently captures risk across membership inference, attribute inference, and data reconstruction. We derive tight bounds that relate DP noise to adversarial advantage and characterize optimal adversarial strategies for arbitrary DP mechanisms and attacker knowledge. These results enable risk-driven noise calibration and provide a foundation for systematic DP auditing. We show that reconstruction advantage improves the accuracy and scope of DP auditing and enables more effective utility-privacy trade-offs in DP-enabled data management systems.

</details>


### [172] [Privacy in ERP Systems: Behavioral Models of Developers and Consultants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12195)
*Alicia Pang,Katsiaryna Labunets,Olga Gadyatskaya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究聚焦ERP系统中的隐私问题，调查开发人员与管理者/顾问的隐私意识，应用Fogg行为模型分析隐私行为模式，旨在促进更恰当的隐私相关行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ERP系统作为企业数字基础设施的核心，存储大量敏感数据，企业需遵守GDPR等法规处理这些数据。但由于开发人员缺乏隐私知识等多种原因，合规具有挑战性。研究旨在了解ERP系统相关人员的隐私意识现状。

Method: 在国际咨询公司背景下，调查ERP系统开发人员以及负责ERP相关服务的经理和顾问两个群体的隐私意识。采用主题分析法，运用Fogg行为模型框架，提取这两个群体的隐私行为模型。

Result: 研究发现揭示了开发人员和顾问在隐私设计及数据最小化方面的行为模式。通过FBM框架分析，识别了影响隐私相关行为的关键因素和障碍。

Conclusion: 研究结果为促进开发人员和顾问更恰当的隐私相关行为提供了方法和手段。通过理解现有行为模式，可以设计更有效的干预措施来提升ERP系统中的隐私保护水平。

Abstract: Applications like Enterprise Resource Planning (ERP) systems have become an indispensable part of the corporate digital infrastructure. These systems store sensitive data about customers, suppliers, and employees, and thus companies have to process these data in accordance with applicable regulations like the GDPR (the EU General Data Protection Regulation). This can be challenging due to a variety of reasons. For example, prior research has shown that developers sometimes lack knowledge about privacy.
  In this work, we focus on privacy in ERP systems in the context of an international consultancy firm. We investigate the privacy awareness regarding privacy-by-design and data minimization of two important populations: developers of ERP systems and managers and consultants responsible for services related to ERP systems. Applying thematic analysis, we elicit privacy behavioral models of these two populations using Fogg's Behavioral Model (FBM) framework. Our findings provide a means to stimulate more adequate privacy-related behaviors for developers and consultants.

</details>
